- **Quantum VFX pass 2 (`scripts/fx/quantum_vfx.gd`, `FX.qvfx`, shaders `fx_qring` / `fx_qtrail` / `fx_qshell`):** pooled and hidden while idle. 6 rings (live cap LOW 2 / MED 4 / HIGH 6; shock, implode, collapse pulse with spokes, portal), 4 ribbons (cap 2 / 3 / 4; teleport trail, ENTANGLE braid, arc bolt, rail beam), ONE MultiMesh of 112 motes (cap 24 / 64 / 112; quantum dust, death dissolve, SUPERPOSITION shimmer) and ONE hero aura sphere: at most 12 draws, no node created after `_ready`. Call sites: `Quantum.teleport/entangle/superpose/collapse_all`, `QuantumVerbs.use`, `HeroVerbs._slam_impact`, `FX.blast` (shock ring by blast colour: fire / cyan collapse / violet implode / green plasma; generic rings only HIGH), `Drone._death_effect` (dissolve into quantum dust, HIGH only, ONE pooled FX.burst emitter that replaces the ember burst; a separate MultiMesh cost +4 draws in the death spike), `PlayerWeapon` (`weapon_shot`, `bolt`, class table `WEAPON_CLASS` / `CLASSES`: rifle, spray, shotgun, beam, flux, gravity, arc, melee) and `ImpactSparks` (`weapon_impact`). Reduce flash (`UiSettings.s_reduce_flash`) dims everything to 0.45 and stops flicker and twinkle. TEST page 2 and the DEMO menu list the VFX SHOWCASE (`play_demo`). Look-dev: `tools/vfx_showcase.sh OUT [WxH] [q]` (`tests/vfx_showcase.tscn`, fixed 1/30 s frames; contact sheet `docs/shots/vfx_pass2.png`). Test: `tests/test_vfx_pass2.gd`. GLSL note: never `pow()` a value that can be negative (it rendered nothing).
- **Enemy readability:** `scripts/enemies/enemy_marks.gd` (`EnemyMarks`, owned by `EnemyManager`): hostile rim rings, attack telegraph (muzzle flare and aim line 0.45 s before shots), damage-only health bars, all in at most 3 MultiMesh draws and no lights. Bolts use `fx_bolt.gdshader` (one draw for the pool). Sentinel model scale is 1.35 (radius and centre height 1.25). With `Game.graphic_violence` hits spray green Vael ichor.
- **Auto quality** is off whenever `Game.autostart` is set (tools, tests, bots); `--governor` turns it back on. `tools/shot.sh` and `tools/bot.sh` accept `QUALITY=0|1|2`.
- **Known budget facts:** MED whole frame about 133-150 draws, visible triangles 215-241k (about 325k with shadow passes); killing 4 enemies at once spikes to 174-189 draws for about 0.8 s; the Compatibility renderer with touch controls peaks at 178 total draws.

## Gameplay depth as implemented (contracts, shop, save/load, tutorial; `scripts/missions/contract*.gd`, `scripts/progress/**`, `scripts/story/tutorial.gd`, `scripts/ui/shop_screen.gd`; tests `test_missions_contracts`, `test_progress`, `test_tutorial`, `test_hud_gameplay`)
- **Contracts (`Contracts`, `Game.missions.contracts`)**: procedural side missions on top of the core hunt. Flow idle -> offered (a cyan marker with a beam 60-170 m away, walk or drive into it) -> active -> done / failed -> pause -> next. Six kinds (`ContractTypes`): `officer` (a marked Vael trooper with 240 hp x sector factor, scaled up, a boss bar under the HUD plate and a minimap ring of about 34 m that turns into a point within 70 m, plus guards), `hack` (hold a 9 m ring for 24 s + 2 per sector while pulses of drones attack every 11 s; the ring is a polar shader with a progress arc; progress decays outside), `chase` (a traffic car from the vehicle module made fast and tough, held against recycling by the meta `hold`, win = wreck it, lose = 120 m away for 6 s), `courier` (timed delivery: pickup marker, drop-off flag, a street-aligned dashed route line in the world and a polyline on the minimap, clock = route / 8.5 m/s + 14 s), `survive` (rift anchor, 3 waves, 4 from sector 4, leash 62 m / 10 s), `drones` (destroy N drones by quantum LINK kills: a kill counts when the drone was linked within 1.5 s of dying; direct kills use up 3 spares; there are always two drones alive so the last kill still has a partner). The story chapter weights the kinds (`ContractTypes.WEIGHTS`, every kind in every chapter); `chase` is only offered with a car within 55 m.
- **Rules**: reward = base x sector factor x (1 + reputation bonus) x (1 + 0.25 x unused time) x heat factor (quiet street +10 %, hot street -10 % per star from 2, floor 60 %), coherence and integrity refill, survive / officer pay a free shop upgrade from sector 2, +8 reputation. Failure (time out, target lost / escaped, anchor lost, too many direct kills, abandon) costs 4 reputation and is retried at the same marker twice (7 s pause), the third failure abandons (-3 more, 24 s pause). Dying puts the contract on hold (what it spawned is released, same marker offered again, no penalty). Resets on `new_game` / `next_sector`; simulates only while playing; visuals (`ContractVisuals`: 2 markers = beam + ring each, 1 route mesh) are built at the first offer and pooled. ORACLE / KESTREL / VAEL BROADCAST lines: `StoryData.CONTRACT_RADIO` (offer, start, mid, done, fail per kind), `CONTRACT_CHAPTER`, `CONTRACT_GENERIC`.
- **Progression (`Progress`, `Game.progress`, child of Missions; `Upgrades`)**: credits (40 % of every score gain plus contract pay), reputation -25..100 (SUSPECT / UNKNOWN / TRUSTED / LEGEND: shop -10 / 0 / +10 / +20 % price, contract pay -10 / 0 / +10 / +25 %), six upgrades x 5 levels in the pause menu's UPGRADES screen (`ShopScreen`): weapon damage +12 % per level, magazine +6 rounds, reload -12 %, blink +8 m, coherence regeneration +20 %, armour -8 % damage taken. Price = base x (1 + 0.8 x level), rounded to 5, times the reputation discount. `Upgrades` is static state read by the player (damage taken, regeneration), the weapon (damage, reload, magazine) and Quantum (blink range); sector difficulty `Progress.difficulty(chapter)`: enemy hp +10 % per sector (applied in `EnemyManager.spawn`), +1 contract enemy per two sectors, pay +20 %, time limits -5 % (floor 70 %).
- **Save / load**: `user://save.cfg` (ConfigFile: wallet, reputation, upgrades, sector, best, tutorial flag, contract tallies), settings and the title's best score stay in `user://settings.cfg` (`UiSettings`). Autosave on pause, on death, at sector end (the NEXT sector is stored), on a purchase and when the tutorial ends; never under `Game.autostart` (tools, tests, bots) unless a test enables it. The title shows CONTINUE (big) and NEW GAME when a save with progress exists; `Progress.continue_game()` runs the normal `start_new_game` flow, then `Story.set_chapter(sector)` and `Missions.new_round(min(9, 6 + sector))`, with no prologue and no tutorial. NEW GAME wipes credits, upgrades and reputation but keeps the tutorial flag and the best score. Damaged, foreign-version or absurd files are refused or clamped.
- **Tutorial (`Tutorial`, `Game.story.tutorial`)**: first launch only (save flag), after the prologue of a new run, five steps (move 5 m, look 0.9 rad, shoot 3 rounds, beacon + blink, enter a car), detected by polling the player, prompts for touch and keyboard (`StoryData.TUTORIAL`), a radio line per step, SKIP plate (tap or Tab), "TRAINING COMPLETE". The HUD draws the prompt plate low between the stick and the buttons zone (`StoryView.TOUCH_BUTTONS_W`), so the touch layout is untouched.
- **HUD additions**: wallet in the credits box (the score moved to the end screens: "SCORE x   CREDITS y"), contract plate (+ thin progress bar) and boss bar under the objective plate (the story layer reads `UiUtil.stack_bottom`, so its lines move down), minimap markers (offer diamond, target diamond + ring, zone ring, officer area ring, destination flag) and the route polyline in one draw call.
- **Shared edits made for this area** (all one to three lines): `autoload/game.gd` (`var progress`), `scripts/player/player_weapon.gd` (damage, reload, magazine through `Upgrades`), `scripts/player/player.gd` (armour in `damage`, regeneration), `scripts/quantum/quantum.gd` (blink range), `scripts/enemies/enemy_manager.gd` (`spawn` applies `Progress.enemy_hp_mult()`), `scripts/vehicles/vehicle_manager.gd` (cars with the meta `hold` are never streamed out or trimmed).

## Living city and facade depth as implemented (environment engineer)
- **Ambient systems (`scripts/ambient/`, built by `City` as child `Ambient`, `City.ambient`, tests `tests/test_ambient.gd`, shaders `prop_traffic`, `prop_crowd(_core/_mirror)`, `prop_searchlight`):** simulation only while `Game.is_playing()`, a clock that restarts on `new_game / respawn / next_sector`, tiers through `City.set_quality`, no node or buffer is created while playing.
  - *Far traffic* (`ambient_traffic.gd`): ONE MultiMesh of paired head / tail light points on the 36 lanes (right-hand traffic, same lane offsets as `VehicleSpecs.lane_offset`); motion is computed in the vertex stage from the uniform `ambient_time` (zero CPU), a car coming towards the camera shows head lights, one driving away tail lights, every 40th car is an emergency vehicle with red / blue strobes, points fade out inside 46 m (the real `VehicleManager` cars own the near street). Cars LOW 80 / MED 150 / HIGH 240 (hard cap 300). Mirrored copy on MED and HIGH.
  - *Crowd* (`ambient_crowd.gd`): ONE MultiMesh of 100 pooled lit silhouettes (walk cycle, coat palette, umbrellas on about a quarter in the vertex stage; the street's baked lamp and neon pools tint them). They walk the sidewalk lines (11.3 m from every road centre line, straight across the zebras, 30 % turn at a corner), live in a ring around the player (LOW 24 people, nobody beyond 40 m; MED 56 / 70 m; HIGH 100 / 90 m) and re-appear in the outer ring when they leave it. `Player.fired` makes everybody within 30 / 38 / 45 m run away along their line for 5-7 s. Mirrored copy on HIGH only. CPU: one pass over at most 100 people and one 8 KB buffer write per frame.
  - *Patrol* (`ambient_sky.gd`): every 45-90 s of play (first after 14-26 s) an airship far above the city crosses it for 34 s with a searchlight (additive cone plus a flat pool of light pinned to the ground by the vertex stage): ONE `MeshInstance3D`, invisible and free between sweeps, never on LOW. Sirens: every 12-24 s, if an emergency car of the far traffic is 60-260 m from the player, `Sfx.play_at(&"siren", pos, -10)` is asked for. **Audio:** `siren.wav` / `siren_1.wav` (3 s one-shots) exist and `siren_loop.wav` (seamless wail) is played by ONE `AudioStreamPlayer3D` (`AmbientSky.siren_player`) that follows the emergency car for 9 s (see "Phone polish pass 2"); the one-shot is only the fallback.
  - *Budget:* traffic 1 draw (+1 mirrored on MED / HIGH), crowd 1 (+1 mirrored on HIGH), patrol 1 only while a sweep is on, so +3 draws on MED and +4 during a sweep; the world's `FacadeKit` adds 1 (below).
- **Signs and ads:** about one shop sign in six (building shader) and one blade sign in five (prop shader) is dying: long dark spells and a stuttering restart; rooftop holograms bob and sway and every ad fades out and returns with other content every 16-24 s. 18 more steam vents (own random stream, so no other placement moved) stand at kerbs and alley mouths.
- **Facade kit (`city_facade.gd`, family `FacadeKit`, one draw):** the mesh holds four modules (balcony with rail, wall air-conditioner with drip pipe, drain pipe with a conduit, projecting cornice band); the prop shader collapses all but the one an instance names (`UV.x = -(1 + module)`, `INSTANCE_CUSTOM.w = (module + 0.5) / 4`), so heights, flips and widths vary without one draw per element. About 2800 instances on the street faces up to 38 m, windowed to 62 m (MED 53 m, LOW 40 m); 94 triangles per instance, about 15 k submitted at the spawn on MED. No colliders (not in `city_colliders.gd` SPECS).
- **Near field (`ground_core.gdshaderinc`):** asphalt base albedo 0.15 (about 0.11 once wet), the cool bounce lift `ambient_boost` 0.32 (LOW 0.11), lamp and neon pools x1.8 on the ground, crosswalk paint worn away where the tyres run and dirtier (`pcol * 0.62`), lamp light shafts also on LOW (windowed to about 110 m). LOW has no mirror: a floor of baked reflection (sky gradient plus streaks stretched from the light map) also shows looking straight down, the road is darker (0.66 when wet) and crosswalks stay matte.
- **Tests / tools:** `tests/test_ambient.gd` (58 checks: counts per tier, caps, lanes, flee, reset, playing-only, no node or buffer growth), `tests/bot_world.gd` now stops the stability watchdog and hides HUD and hero (`--keep_hud` keeps them) so `--cam="x,y,z,tx,ty,tz[,fov];..." --stats` poses really show the world through `main.tscn`. `tests/test_world.gd` MED spawn triangle budget check raised from 140 k to 160 k (facade kit, crowd, traffic) and LOW now keeps the lamp shafts.
- **Measured (Mobile renderer, software Vulkan, MED, same pose and run, `tests/bot_world.gd --hide=Family --hide_from=k --stats`):** far traffic -2 draws / 1.2 k primitives (real + mirrored), crowd -1 draw / 7.8 k, facade kit -1 draw / 14.6 k: +4 draws and about +24 k primitives in total. Staged street views whole frame: LOW 50-57 visible draws (about 100-110 k primitives), MED 69-78 (176-187 k), HIGH 78-89 (277-284 k, shadows add 0 / 6-7 / 9-12 draws); Compatibility MED street 100 visible draws (fallback tier).

## Audio as implemented (audio engineer)
- **Files (`assets/audio/`, about 22 MB, all numpy-synthesised and reproducible: `python3 tools/audio/make_library.py [--only 'step_*'] [--no-music]`, helpers in `make_audio.py`, music and beds in `make_music.py`; `python3 tools/audio/check_audio.py` verifies the committed files; `tools/import.sh` after regenerating):** the 11 original effects and `music_ambient.wav` are untouched and stay variant 0 of their families. New one-shots are 44.1 kHz mono 16-bit WAV (import `compress/mode=0`: the tests read the PCM), loops are WAV with a `smpl` chunk, the 6 ambience beds (24 s stereo), 3 thunder rolls and the 5 music stems (30 s stereo, identical length) are OGG Vorbis (`loop=true` import flag, also set in code).
  Families (variants as `<name>_0 .. _N`): `step_asphalt|metal|stairs` (6 each), `land_*`, `shot` (4, layered crack + body + mechanical + tail), `enemy_shot` (3), `shot_far`/`enemy_shot_far`, `dry_fire`, `reload` (3), `impact_concrete|metal|glass|flesh|asphalt` (4 each), `ricochet`, `whizz`, `boom` (4) + `explosion_small`, `distant_boom|gunfire`, `trooper_alert|attack|pain|death` (3 each, formant synthesis), `hero_pain|effort|death`, `drone_hum` (loop) `drone_charge|alert|death`, `blink` (3) `link` (3) `superpos_enter` `superpos_loop` `entangle_loop` `quantum_ready|fail`, `engine_low|high` (RPM loop pair) `engine_start` `tyre_squeal` `car_crash|bump` `horn` `car_door` `traffic_loop`, `ui_*`, `pickup_health|energy|ammo` `credit` `mission_start|complete` `objective_ping` `heat_up|down`, `heartbeat` `breath_in|out` `gasp`, `radio_open|close|static|glitch` `babble` (10 syllables), `siren` (+`siren_1`: the contract of `ambient_sky.gd` is fulfilled), `sting_death|win`, beds `rain_asphalt|metal|awning` `traffic_far` `wind` `city_hum`, `thunder_0..2`, stems `music_pad|pulse|bass|perc|lead`.
- **Buses (`default_bus_layout.tres`):** Master (limiter, ceiling -1 dB) <- SFX (compressor), Music (low-pass, off), Ambient (low-pass, off), Voice (radio). `Sfx.set_bus_volume(bus, 0..1)`, `apply_volumes({"Music": 0.6, ...})` (called by `Hud.apply_settings` from `UiSettings.volumes`, saved under `[audio] volumes`), `Sfx.muted` also mutes the Master bus.
- **`Sfx` API:** `play(name, db, pitch)`, `play_at(name, pos, db, pitch)` (unknown names ignored; a name resolves to its variants, never the same twice in a row), `impact(kind, pos, normal)`, `dry_fire()`, `horn()`, `radio_begin()`/`radio_type(text, typed)`/`radio_end()`, `duck(&"explosion"|&"radio")`, `play_music()` (adaptive stems **and** ambience; a no-op under tools/tests/bots, which never call it), `stop_music()`, `set_music_state(state)`, `start_ambience()`, `set_rain_surface()`, `reset_world()`, `variants_of(name)`.
  Pools: 20 flat + 12 positional voices (+4 radio syllables, 4 ambience one-shots). Each family has a priority, a voice cap and a minimum gap (`Sfx.FAMILIES`): a family at its cap cuts its own oldest voice, a full pool steals the oldest voice of lower or equal priority and drops the sound if everything playing matters more; positional sounds past 140 m (explosions 280 m) never start; a far enemy shot swaps to `*_far`, and an enemy shot 6-70 m away may whizz past the listener after dist/340 s.
- **MIDI music (core-loop sprint; supersedes the numpy stems described just below, same API):** `tools/music/compose.py` composes four tracks as MIDI and renders them with FluidSynth + the FluidR3 GM SoundFont (MIT): explore 96 BPM D minor (files `music_pad|pulse|bass|perc|lead`), menu 64 BPM (`mus_menu_*`), combat 144 BPM E minor (`mus_combat_*`), chase 160 BPM A minor (`mus_chase_*`), five stems each, plus the seven theme layers (`music_district_0..3`, `music_night`, `music_boss`, `music_chase`) on the matching track's grid; every file is the same exact 30.000 s loop (BPM x bars x 4 / 60). `Sfx.track_for_state(state, current)` maps menu to menu, combat and boss to combat, chase to chase, death to the running track, everything else to explore; `set_music_state` calls `_switch_track` when the track changes (the free player starts the new track's `AudioStreamSynchronized`, `_music` and `_music_alt` swap, equal-power crossfade `TRACK_XFADE` 0.9 s driven by `_step_music`, `Sfx.music_track`), the state's stem levels (`MUSIC_STATES`, `AudioDirector.stem_targets`) stay the intensity layers inside the track, `wanted_layer()` returns a district or night layer only on the explore track (the boss layer rides the combat track, the chase layer the chase track) and a track switch restarts the running layer in step (`_layer_stale`). Sources: `tools/music/midi/*.mid` (27), `tools/music/manifest.json` (measured loudness, peak, seam of every encoded file), docs/MUSIC.md; tests `tests/test_music_midi.gd`, `tests/test_audio.gd` (stem and track crossfades), `tools/audio/check_audio.py`.
- **Adaptive music (stem ladder):** one `AudioStreamSynchronized` (sample-locked stems) on the Music bus, 5 stem levels per state (`Sfx.MUSIC_STATES`: menu, explore, alert, combat, driving, death, win), exponential crossfade (0.7 s, combat 0.45 s, death 0.25 s), `death` and `win` also play a sting. With `Sfx.auto_music` (default) the state follows the game: heat >= 1 alert, >= 3 combat (falls back only after `MUSIC_MIN_HOLD` 6 s and below heat 2.4 / 0.5), a driven car (`Game.vehicles.driven`) driving, `Game.state` menu/dead/win. Entering alert/combat plays `heat_up`, leaving it `heat_down`. D minor, 96 BPM, 12 bars, kick-sidechained pad/bass inside the stems.
- **Ducking (sidechain style, scripted so it is testable):** explosions (`boom*`, `explosion*`, `car_crash*` start it) take Music -9 dB and Ambient -11 dB (attack 40 ms, hold 0.9 s, release 2.2 s); the radio takes Music -7, Ambient -9, SFX -2.5 (attack 150 ms, held while text types).
- **Living sound, only while `Game.is_playing()`** (all polled from `Sfx._process`, no node edits): footsteps from `Game.player.body.loco.footfalls` (one per foot touchdown; surface from the hero's position: street = wet asphalt, ledge/steps = stairs, roofs = metal; level by speed), heartbeat + breath + low-pass muffle below 35 % hp, the player's car (`Game.vehicles.driven`: two engine loops crossfaded by a 4-gear RPM model, pitch from RPM, tyre squeal from `slip`, crash/bump from a sudden speed drop, `engine_start`), traffic hum on the 3 nearest moving non-player cars within 60 m (three follow emitters, pitch by speed), quantum drones while a ghost (`quantum.clone`) or link (`quantum.links`) exists, rain bed weights by surface (street asphalt + awning, roofs metal) scaled by `Atmosphere.rain_now`, thunder every 18-55 s while raining, distant fights every 35-110 s. `Sfx.reset_world()` runs on `new_game`, `respawn`, `next_sector` and death (loops, delayed sounds, ducks, foot counter).
- **Enemies (polled 10 times a second from `Game.enemies.list`, no hooks in the enemy code):** a trooper noticing the hero (`aware` rises) screeches `trooper_alert`, losing hp cries `trooper_pain`, dying wails `trooper_death`, a started telegraph (`threat_charge()` crosses 0.05) gives `trooper_attack` (half of the time) or the drone's `drone_charge`; drones give `drone_alert` / `drone_death`; the two nearest drones within 45 m carry a `drone_hum` follow emitter (pitch by type). Hero extras from `Sfx._step_hero`: `hero_effort` on a jump, `land_*` after a fall (louder the longer the air time), `hero_pain` with every `hurt`, `ui_pause` / `ui_resume` on the pause state, `mission_complete` on `sector_cleared`.
- **Radio:** `Story` calls `Sfx.radio_begin()` (squelch + duck) and `Sfx.radio_type(text, typed)` every frame while a line types: one babble syllable (pitched by the vowel) per vowel typed, spaced 65 ms, plus rare glitches; the radio closes itself 0.35 s after the typing stops.
- **Shared edits made (all one-liners):** `scripts/player/impact_sparks.gd` (`Sfx.impact` in `impact_world/machine/person`), `scripts/player/player_weapon.gd` (`Sfx.dry_fire()` on an empty magazine), `scripts/story/story.gd` (radio hooks replace the two `hit` beeps), `scripts/ui/ui_settings.gd` (`volumes`), `scripts/ui/hud.gd` (`apply_settings` pushes them), `autoload/game.gd` (`explode` plays `explosion_small` under radius 5), `tests/test_integration.gd` (the music check now expects the adaptive stems). Volume sliders (music, SFX) now exist on the settings rows, see "Phone polish pass 2".
- **AUDIO v3 (`tools/audio/make_v3.py`, `AudioDirector`, `RadioShows`, tests/test_audio_v3.gd):** music director v2 in `Sfx` (`director_targets`, polled every 0.2 s): per-stem dB offsets from weather (rain/storm: pad up, lead down), hour (night/dawn/dusk), heat, chapter and boss phase (phase 1 restrained, 3 lifts the boss layer; `Sfx.boss_phase`, set by Overseer / VerbBoss), never above 0 dB. New stealth states `sneak` (an unaware enemy within 24 m) and `suspect` (suspicious or searching within 45 m) from the enemies' `alert_level()`; an alerted guard forces `alert` without heat. Stingers: `sting_stealth_spot`, `sting_stealth_clear`, `sting_phase`, `sting_chapter`. Ability sounds via `Sfx.verb(id)` (shield, slip, arc from QuantumVerbs.use; slam, grab, throw, wallrun from HeroVerbs), five new shots (rail, flux, gravity, burst, lance via the weapon `snd` field), engine families per vehicle kind (`AudioDirector.ENGINES`: car, bike, hover, heavy loops plus pitch), footsteps for roofs (`step_roof`) and interiors (`step_tile`, `step_carpet` by `InteriorSpace.room_type()`). Radio shows: each CarRadio station plays a programme (`RadioShows.segment`: songs at per-song pitch, host chatter, ads; the station ducks under talk and ads, captions via Game.say). Added about 1.6 MB (22 WAV/OGG one-shots and stingers, 25 loops and steps, 11 OGG show clips); the audio folder stays under the 30 MB cap.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_audio.gd` (96 checks: catalogue formats/durations/levels, loop seams, OGG lengths, buses, volume, mute, ducking, pools and caps, a 20-shot firefight and 10 s of sustained fire summed numerically with the pool rules, all music states and game hooks, footsteps, low health, vehicles, quantum, ambience, radio, reset) and `tools/audio/check_audio.py` (every file incl. OGG seams, stems, and the full mix of a firefight with combat music, ambience and radio: peak -7.3 dBFS, RMS -22.9 dBFS before the limiter). Measured, not listened: nobody has heard it on a phone yet.

## Performance, LOD and texture quality as implemented (performance engineer; `scripts/common/{perf_probe,perf_tiers,shadow_proxy,shader_warmup}.gd`, `scripts/characters/skinned_mirror.gd`, tests `tests/test_perf_budget.gd`, `tests/bot_perf.gd`, `tests/bot_warm.gd`, tools `tools/perf_report.sh`, `tools/warm_probe.sh`, `tools/verify_asset_build.sh`)
- **Budget gate:** `tools/perf_report.sh [tiers]` runs the real `main.tscn` (software Vulkan, `--fixed-fps 30`, governor off, seeded) per tier with `tests/bot_perf.gd`: the staged full-game view (hero, 4 enemies, the starter car, HUD), then the 4-enemy simultaneous death sampled every frame. It prints visible and total (camera + shadow passes + canvas) draws and triangles, node count, texture memory, real lights on, the HUD's canvas draws and the death worst case. `PERF_CHECK=1` exits 1 when a tier is over `PerfProbe.BUDGETS` (LOW 100 draws / 200k triangles, **MED 140 / 280k**, HIGH 220 / 600k, death spike LOW 110 / MED 150 / HIGH 235; Compatibility +20 / +15 draws of headroom). Env: `RENDERER=compat`, `BOT_TOUCH=1`, `BOT_DUMP=1` (biggest shadow casters, a census of the visible dynamic nodes, per-frame death series), `PERF_SHOTS=prefix`. `tests/test_perf_budget.gd` (headless) covers everything a dummy renderer can: tiers, LOD hooks, proxies, the death governor, the reflection, the pre-warm, texture imports, and the render budget itself whenever the renderer reports numbers.
- **Measured (Mobile renderer, software Vulkan, 1280x720, staged view, whole frame):** LOW 85 draws / 120k triangles, MED 120 draws / 265k (visible 84 / 226k; was 116 / 299k), HIGH 131 / 378k; the 4-enemy death peaks at LOW 99, MED 142, HIGH 151 draws (MED was 158 draws / 321k triangles; 127-133 until the pickups of the kills appear); touch layout +5 to +6 draws (HUD layers in total 17-20 draws); Compatibility MED 134 draws (140 with touch), death 150-156. Texture memory of the staged scene LOW 144 / MED 188 / HIGH 209 MB (was 106 MB at 512 px atlases).
- **Mesh LOD and tiers (`PerfTiers`, composed by `main.gd`):** every imported model ships meshoptimizer LODs (`meshes/generate_lods`), the viewport's `mesh_lod_threshold` picks them (LOW 5 px, MED 3 px, HIGH 1.5 px: the camera AND the shadow passes get fewer triangles, -26k total on MED), anisotropic filtering 2x / 4x / 8x, texture mip bias +1 on LOW. `PerfTiers.cap_scale()` lets the Compatibility renderer spawn 75 % of the enemy and car pool caps.
- **Shadows (`ShadowProxy`):** the hero and the troopers cast through a shadow-only copy of the skinned mesh on the SAME skeleton with a coarse LOD bias (`CharacterRig.shadow`, `set_shadow`), cars through a shadow-only mesh without the lamp surfaces; the visible meshes cast nothing. Enemies and cars cast no shadow beyond 25 m of the camera (`PerfTiers.SHADOW_RANGE`, hysteresis +4 m). Tests that count meshes skip `SHADOWS_ONLY` ones.
- **Distance LOD (`Drone.update_lod`, called by `EnemyManager.lod_pass` every 0.25 s; `Vehicle.update_lod`):** shadow 25 m, drone fins 45 m, the whole model hidden beyond 130 m, model lights only within `PerfTiers.light_range()` (LOW 14 / MED 26 / HIGH 40 m); cars: wheels 50 m, mirror 25 m, underglow light by range. Real lights are budgeted: cars keep only the underglow omni (the headlight spot and the car's own fill are freed by `DynamicFill.trim_lights`), troopers keep fill and rim, so the staged view has 11-13 lights on instead of 25.
- **Car draw calls:** the three lamp surfaces (headlights, taillights, underglow) are merged into ONE "Lamps" surface with one tiny shader (vertex alpha = lamp kind, three colour uniforms, LODs regenerated through `ImporterMesh`), the street reflection copies only the paint and details surfaces (a layer-0 twin on the shadow mesh): 5 -> 3 draws per car in the camera pass, 5 -> 2 in the mirror.
- **Death spike (`EnemyManager`):** at most `DEATH_BLASTS` (LOW 1 / MED 1 / HIGH 2) enemy deaths use an explosion slot at a time, the others get one cheap burst; decorative hit and death bursts spend tokens of a per-tier bucket (`fx_token()`, 3-4 / 8 capacity, refilled per second), the killing blow shows only the death effect, a trooper's death is one emitter (was two), a death's pickup drops 0.35 s later (`DROP_DELAY`), and `EnemyMarks` draws the rings and the health bars in ONE MultiMesh draw (shader mode 3 of `fx_threat.gdshader`, premultiplied alpha; `ring_count`, `bar_count`; the aim lines keep their own draw).
- **Shader pre-warm (`ShaderWarmup.run`, awaited by `main.gd` behind the loading overlay, skipped under `Game.autostart`):** one enemy of every type and one car of every kind are activated at 8 m in front of the camera with their marks lit, every FX pool entry is played (burst, tracer, muzzle flash, fire / quantum / wreck blasts, the player's sparks, mist, dust, decal), every hidden mesh of the quantum module, the missions (pickups) and the player is shown at that point for 3 frames, and the HUD font is drawn at every layout size (glyph atlas). Then everything is put back (`FX.reset()`, pools, visibility, transforms). `tools/warm_probe.sh` (`tests/bot_warm.gd`): pipelines compiled during the first use of a shot, enemies, a hit, 4 deaths, a wreck, blink, teleport, link, superposition, collapse, the pause screen: **62 without the pre-warm, 16 with it**; wall-clock first-use frames on the shared software renderer are too noisy to quote (steady frame 190-210 ms at 480x270, spikes of 0-150 ms in both runs).
- **Textures:** the baked atlases are 2048 px for the hero, the trooper, the 3 drones and the 3 cars (paint and details atlases each), 1024 px for the pickups and the core (`atlas.DEFAULT_SCALE`, env `QT_ATLAS_SCALE`, `build_assets.sh --atlas-scale N`); the import (`patch_imports.py`) is VRAM-compressed with mipmaps, in high quality (ASTC 4x4 / BPTC) for albedo, normal and emission, 8x8 for ORM; `post_import.gd` sets anisotropic mipmap filtering on every model material (and re-imports the models when it changes). About 195 MB of textures per platform, install about 220 MB (`tools/size_report.sh`). The 512 px tiling sets are unused by the game and unchanged. The facade / shopfront textures stay procedural (shader): the environment agent needs no atlas from this area.
- **Pose-following reflection (`SkinnedMirror`, `CharacterRig.reflection`):** a skinned copy of the hero (and, within 18 m, of every trooper) under the world's Mirror node that shares mesh, skin and the SKELETON NODE with the body (so the very same bone transforms pose it), placed at the mirrored transform of the body every frame, unshaded albedo stand-in, coarse LOD, hidden beyond 30 m / on LOW / with the body. One draw and the triangles of a coarse LOD per character; `MirrorProxy` still skips skinned meshes (its rest-pose copy was the "two bodies" bug).
- **Asset pipeline verified from scratch:** `tools/verify_asset_build.sh` copies the committable files to a temp tree and runs `build_assets.sh` there with an EMPTY `HOME` and a scrubbed environment (it downloaded and checksum-verified the pinned Godot, created `.venv-tools`, pip-installed `bpy 5.2.3` + numpy, baked tiling sets, audio, 9 models with 2K atlases, imported, pinned the import settings): exit 0 after 977 s on 4 cores, **278 generated files (models, textures, audio) and 277 `.import` files byte-identical** to the checkout. `tools/blender/ensure_bpy.sh` is now a one-line wrapper of `tools/setup_python.sh` (one environment recipe, documented in `tools/blender/README.md`).
- **Shared edits made for this area:** `scripts/main.gd` (PerfTiers node, the pre-warm await), `scripts/characters/character_rig.gd` (shadow proxy, reflection, `set_shadow`), `scripts/common/dynamic_fill.gd` (`trim_lights`, `set_lights_visible`), `scripts/vehicles/{vehicle,vehicle_visual,vehicle_specs,vehicle_manager}.gd` (LOD hooks, lamp merge, mirror source, `warm_begin/end`, compat cap), `scripts/enemies/*` (LOD, death governor, token budget, drop delay, marks merge), `assets/shaders/fx_threat.gdshader`, `tests/test_enemies.gd` (marks counters, delayed drop), `tests/test_character_motion.gd` (skips shadows-only meshes), `tools/size_report.sh`, `tools/blender/{atlas.py,post_import.gd,patch_imports.py,README.md}`, `tools/build_assets.sh`, `BUDGETS.md` (the numbers).
- **Open / for other owners:** each pickup costs 3 draws (body, halo, column) plus its mirror copy: three drops are +15 draws at the spike; merging the halo and the column into the model (or one MultiMesh for all pickups) would give the death spike another 10+ draws of margin. `VehicleManager._ram` throws `Invalid access to property 'speed' on Nil` (vehicle_manager.gd:368) when the driven car is released in the same frame the player exits (seen once in the exit phase of `tests/bot_full.gd`).

## Phone polish pass 2 as implemented (HUD density, Compat brightness, UI audio)
- **HUD density:** the top-centre stack is the bars plate (captions measured per scale, `Hud._caption_width`) plus ONE priority line (`Hud._draw_priority`: contract line while offered/active, else the core objective; thin progress bar and the officer's boss bar hang under it). The story layer no longer draws its OBJ plate in play; the chapter banner stays (narrower, lines shrink with `StoryView.fit_font`). Minimap 148 px (`Hud.MINIMAP`), perf readout 300x70 under it, `UiUtil.left_bottom` publishes the bottom of that column.
- **Radio subtitle:** a narrow plate (`StoryView.SUB_W` 320 px) in the top-left column under the minimap/perf box: `fit_lines` wraps to the measured plate width, drops from 22 to 18 px above `SUB_MAX_LINES` (5), breaks over-wide words by characters, reveals whole words only (never "Two cl"). Checked at 1280x720 and 1600x720 with the touch layout: nothing covers the hero, crosshair or buttons. Centre message baseline `Hud.MSG_Y` 262.
- **Compat brightness:** `CityEnv.COMPAT_*` (ambient colour/energy, exposure 1.95, glow threshold/strength/add). One MED frame, same view: mean luma Mobile 0.205, Compat before 0.131, after 0.202 (median 0.149 vs 0.121, p95 0.64 vs 0.74).
- **UI audio:** new `ui_tap ui_deny ui_purchase ui_upgrade ui_contract_accept ui_contract_complete ui_contract_fail` and `siren_loop` (`tools/audio/make_library.py`). Screen buttons play confirm/back/tap, a refused purchase `ui_deny`, a purchase `ui_purchase` (`ui_upgrade` at max level); `Contracts` plays accept/complete/fail; touch USE plays `ui_tap`. `Sfx.last_played` records the last flat sound (tests). Pause/title settings rows: MUSIC and SFX sliders (`Hud.SLIDERS`, `set_volume`, `slide`, tap or drag, saved in `UiSettings.volumes`; SFX also sets the Ambient and Voice buses) next to POST FX / SOUND (mute kept).

## Combat depth as implemented (four weapons, specialist enemies, cover, squad callouts, the Overseer boss; tests `test_weapons`, `test_enemy_types`, `test_boss`, bot `bot_combat2`)
- **Weapons** (`scripts/player/weapon_defs.gd` data + the unlocked set as statics, `player_weapon.gd` logic): `carbine` (the old rifle, numbers unchanged, unlimited reserve), `scatter` (8 pellets, each rolls the crit test on its own, full damage to 12 m falling to 25 % at 38 m, 6 + 36), `lance` (hold to charge 1.1 s, release below 35 % cancels, damage 120 scaled 0.35..1 by charge, hitscan that pierces every enemy on the line, sniper zoom 24 degrees through `CameraRig.ads_fov`, 5 + 20) and `grenade` (thrown arc, lands or hits an enemy, a 1.6 s gravity well of radius 9 m pulls enemies in at up to 7.5 m/s and burns 24 dps, then `Game.explode` radius 7.5 with the quantum-collapse colour; two pooled nodes, 1 + 5). `PlayerWeapon.mag / ammo / reserve / reload_t / spread` always describe the weapon in hand, so `Player.ammo / mag / reloading / spread` and the HUD keep working; every weapon keeps its own ammo (`_store`). Switching: keys 1-4, wheel (`weapon_next` / `weapon_prev`), touch SWAP (`swap`, X on keyboard); 0.32 s swap lock played through the rig's reload pose; `PlayerBody.set_weapon_look` rides one emissive strip on the muzzle (shape and tint per weapon, hidden for the carbine so the hero stays ONE mesh).
- **Upgrades per weapon:** the shop's damage and reload multipliers apply to every weapon's own base values; the magazine upgrade is +20 % of each weapon's base per level (`WeaponDefs.mag_size`: carbine 30..60 as before, scatter 6..12, lance 5..10, grenade 1..3 with at least +1 every second level). `Progress._apply_to_player` calls `PlayerWeapon.apply_upgrades`.
- **Owning weapons:** shop row "ALIEN WEAPONS" (`buy_scatter` / `buy_lance` / `buy_grenade`, 500 / 800 / 650 credits before the reputation discount, `Progress.buy_weapon`), pickups (`Missions.drop_weapon(id, pos)`, kind `weapon`, ~10 % of enemy drops, an unowned weapon half of the time else an ammo pack), the boss drop. Saved in `save.cfg` (`[weapons] unlocked, current`, `[campaign] bosses`), `Progress.new_campaign` resets to the carbine. HUD: `Hud.weapon_text()` ("SHARD SCATTER  SCT   RESERVE 36", INF for the carbine), `ammo_text()` unchanged ("6 / 6"), a charge bar over the weapon plate (`weapon_charge()`).
- **Specialists** (`scripts/enemies/specialist.gd`, all on the operative model and rig with a tint and a `scale`, `EnemyTypes.TYPES` rows with `role`): `shield` (160-point frontal shield, 60 degree arc, turns at 2.2 rad/s; `shield_blocks(from)` / `shield_hit` / `break_shield`; the carbine and scatter are absorbed, the lance and the grenade break it, blasts hurt him directly; the plate is the ONE extra draw), `flanker` (8.2 m/s orbit at about 11 m, darts in, 2-round bursts), `sniper` (0.8 s laser telegraph through the existing EnemyMarks aim line, the aim point locks 0.25 s before the 30 damage shot so a late sidestep dodges it, backs off inside 24 m, uses cover), `brute` (380 hp, 0.4 s roar then a charge locked on a line, 0.55 s windup then a ground slam ring of 4 m through `Game.explode`). Chapter and heat gating: `EnemyTypes.specialist_for` (flanker from chapter 2, shield from chapter 2 at heat 1.5 or chapter 3, sniper from 3, brute from 4 at heat 2.5) turns about half of the operative reinforcement slots into specialists (`EnemyManager.pick_type`), with per-type limits (`limit`: shield 3, flanker 3, sniper 2, brute 2); caps per tier are unchanged. A specialist is at most +1 mesh over an operative (tested).
- **Cover and squads:** `EnemyCover.find` uses the world's pooled prop colliders (`Game.world.prop_colliders().bodies`, active shapes at least 0.9 m tall) and returns the spot behind the nearest one as seen from the threat; `Operative` (plain operatives and snipers, cfg `cover`) runs there, hides 0.9-2.2 s (no line of sight), peeks sideways for one burst, and leaves for 3.5 s when the target goes more than 60 degrees round or within 5.5 m. `EnemyManager.squad_callout` (made by an operative the first time it is aware) wakes neighbours within 45 m and gives the nearest operatives alternating flank sides for 8 s (`Operative.assign_flank`).
- **Overseer boss** (`scripts/enemies/overseer.gd`, type `overseer`, operative rig at 1.95x, violet, a one-draw crest of spikes, 1500 hp x sector hp): phase 1 summons sentinels / strikers every 9 s (at most 3 alive, caps respected), phase 2 (below 66 %) plants and charges a shield pulse for 2 s (damage x0.12 while raised) that hits the player within 38 m for 30 unless the world is in between (cover-timing), phase 3 (below 33 %) enrages (x1.5 speed, ground slams of 6.5 m / 32 damage). Each phase change gives 1 s of invulnerability, a ring and an ORACLE line (`StoryData.BOSS_RADIO`). `Sfx.boss_active` selects the music state `boss`. Contract kind `boss` (not in `ContractTypes.KINDS`; `BOSS_CHAPTERS = [2, 5]` = sectors 3 and 6): offered first and again until cleared (`Progress.bosses_cleared`), 420 s, 170 m leash (12 s), HUD boss bar "VAEL OVERSEER  PHASE n / 3" and contract line, reward 1200 x sector factor plus a free upgrade plus a second one as the sector-clear bonus, the unique weapon (lance in sector 3, grenade in sector 6, unlocked at once and an ammo pack where he fell). Fail = the usual contract rules (two retries, a fresh full-health Overseer each time, death puts the contract on hold without penalty).

## Cinematics and presentation as implemented (`scripts/cinematics/{cinematics,cine_plan,cine_data}.gd`, `CameraRig.cine_*`, `Stability` slow-motion parameters; test `test_cinematic`)
- **One node, `Cinematics` (main.gd adds it after `Stability`; `Cinematics.instance`, group `cinematics`).** Scenes are data (`CinePlan`: shots as Catmull-Rom splines or orbits around anchors, drone props flying `p0 + v t`, subtitles, title card, timed events, flash and grade curves; built once per scene, sampled with no allocation). Scenes: `intro` (about 25 s, first DEPLOY only: skyline fly-through under Vael drones, drone pass above Kestrel on the tallest flat roof 30-110 m away, slow arc behind the hero, rift flash, hero back in the street, title card), `chapter` (6.5 s orbit with chapter label, title, sub and objective after a sector win; replaces the Story banner), `boss_in` (low push-in on the Overseer when its contract starts, hard cut to the hero) and `boss_out` (slow-motion orbit round its fall, via `Stability.begin_slowmo(scale, hold, ramp)` which ends with the state it began in). Subtitle words: `cine_data.gd` (ORACLE / KESTREL, StoryData speakers and colours).
- **Mechanism.** A scene runs in the new game state `cutscene` (`Cinematics.STATE`): every module already freezes on a non-play state; the HUD hides both layers (`hud.gd`), touch controls let go, the Story view is hidden, Sfx ambience keeps playing. The camera is the gameplay rig's own camera driven by `CameraRig.cine_set(xf, fov)`, which lapses by itself 0.25 real seconds after the last call (no scene can leave the view stuck). Real post grade (`Atmosphere.set_effect`: vignette, saturation, contrast, gain, chromatic aberration) shifts cold to warm and is restored from saved values. The intro moves Kestrel to the roof and back (`teleport_to`, saved position, yaw and look angles) and, when it ends or is skipped, calls `Story.skip_cards()` so the chapter intro and tutorial follow as before.
- **Always restored.** `_end()` clears the camera pose, grade, hero, props, slow motion and the state. An outside state change (pause, death, menu) ends the scene with no state write. Stability audit repairs a `cutscene` state with no running scene and a cinematic camera left on during play. Any tap, click, Enter, Space, Tab or Esc skips after 0.45 s (consumed, never reaches the game); skip hint on screen. Setting `UiSettings.cutscenes` ("CINE ON/OFF" on the title and pause screens) disables scenes; the intro plays once (`intro_seen` in settings.cfg); CONTINUE never plays it; tools, tests and bots (`Game.autostart`) never start one unless `play()` is called, `force` is set or `--cine` is passed.
- **Presentation outside scenes.** Title screen: slow parallax camera behind Kestrel against the avenue, two drones crossing the sky, bars and vignette, lighter menu dim (replaces the orbit backdrop). Shop: hero close-up (44 degrees, slow turntable) beside the panel, lighter dim. Dead and win: letterbox bars + vignette ease in; every return to play fades in from black. Pooled props (`drone_*` models, hidden when idle), one canvas layer (9, under the HUD), a few canvas draws only while something shows.
- **Not verified on a phone.** Timings, subtitle size at 20:9 and the roof fly-through against real streaming and tall neighbours (the camera can cross a taller building) were checked only on software Vulkan frames.

## Phone-screenshot fixes and the TEST MENU as implemented (device-fix engineer; tests `test_device_fixes`, `test_testmode`, bots `bot_hero_luma`, `bot_testmenu`, `bot_texmem`)
- **Hero brightness:** the sandbox cannot reproduce a white blob on Mobile (hero mean luma 0.22-0.34 at tiers 0-2); the likely device causes were the Compatibility renderer path (a phone that falls back to GLES: exposure 1.95, glow add, emission texture shown much brighter) and bloom with a low threshold on a bright panel. Now: LOW bloom threshold +0.3 and luminance cap 3.5 (MED/HIGH 12), four glow levels only, Gear albedo x0.72 and emission x0.6 on LOW, key light x0.8 on LOW, Compat emission x0.55 and a darker Compat gear tint. `tests/bot_hero_luma.gd` (rendered, `BOT_TIERS=0,1,2`, `RENDERER=compat`, `BOT_FASTEXIT=1`) measures the hero's pixels (two frames, hero meshes shown / hidden) and gates mean luma < 0.34, highlight fraction < 0.08.
- **Lost world:** `PlayerUtil.safe_spot(pos, world, space)` (clamp to the walls, drop onto the floor / roof, out of building volumes, at most 60 m up) is applied by `Player.teleport_to` and the blink's aim point; `PlayerUtil.outside_city` + `Player.rescue_if_lost()` (every physics tick on foot) put a hero outside the walls, under the floor or at NaN back on the nearest street with 1 s of grace; `Player.teleport_to` also forces `City.update_streaming` and `update_colliders` at the target.
- **Camera:** the spring arm and the side probes mask only `L_WORLD`; the 56 pooled prop colliders are excluded from the arm (`CameraRig._exclude_props`), so cars, pedestrians, lamps and clutter never shorten it. `CameraRig.hero_fade` (0 at 1.2 m, 1 at 0.9 m, smoothstep) drives `PlayerBody.set_fade` (instance transparency on Mobile; hidden outright above 0.96, and on Compat above 0.5).
- **Crowd:** per-person lateral offset (-0.75..0.62 m), speed mix with dawdlers, random idle stops and chatting groups of 2-3 (`GROUP_CHANCE`); idle people stop the walk animation.
- **Performance (LOW):** no engine frame cap or vsync override exists in `project.godot` or scripts (Engine.max_fps 0, vsync default = on), so a phone pinned at 30 is vsync quantisation of a frame that takes 17-33 ms (GPU bound or CPU bound), not a cap. Changes: the full-screen post pass (a screen-copy) is hidden on LOW (`Atmosphere`), project MSAA default 0 (the tier sets 2x / 4x), glow limited to four levels, positional shadow atlas 512 px and directional 1024 px on LOW (-4 MB measured), and `TextureBudget.halve_models()` (run by `main.gd` before any model is instantiated, LOW only): 111 model textures drop to their own second mip (no re-encode): 204 -> 150 MB with every model resident (sandbox, `bot_texmem`). Not verifiable without a device: the device's 267 MB (it also counts render targets), the real frame time, and whether the hero is still pale on that GPU.
- **TEST MENU:** `TestMode` (static state and actions, `scripts/common/test_mode.gd`) + `TestMenu` (CanvasLayer 40, own hit-testing, last child of main). Available when `OS.is_debug_build()` or `TestMode.forced` (saved setting `UiSettings.test_mode`, `--test-mode`). Opened by the TEST button on the title and pause screens, a long press on the FPS box (a release build keeps the error panel there) or F10. Toggles: god, infinite ammo, one-hit kills, no heat, freeze enemies (hooks: `Player.damage`, `PlayerWeapon.tick/_hit_target`, `Game.heat/explode`, `Drone._physics_process`). Pages: CHEATS, LOOT (weapons, credits, upgrades), WORLD (weather, heat, tier), SPAWN (9 enemy types incl. the Overseer, 3 cars, at the crosshair), GO (core, contract marker, rooftop, four corners, spawn), MISSION (sectors 1-6, 7 contract kinds, skip), STATS. The 1-4 weapon quick buttons draw on the right edge in play while test mode is available (`TestMode.quick_weapons`). Time of day does not exist in the game (the city is night only), so only weather is offered.

## Character depth and citizens as implemented (hero, trooper, pedestrians; `tools/blender/{hipoly,model_citizen,model_player,model_operative,atlas,helpers}.py`, `scripts/ambient/ambient_crowd.gd`, `assets/shaders/prop_crowd_core.gdshaderinc`, tests `test_assets` (citizen + 2K bake checks), `test_ambient` (near / far crowd))
- **High-poly bake (sculpt-style, no Cycles needed):** `helpers.export_model(..., hipoly=dict(levels=2))` builds a high-poly source from the BUILT low-poly parts (`hipoly.from_parts`: Catmull-Clark level 2, about 90k triangles for the trooper and 340k for the hero) and displaces it along the smooth normals with real per-kind relief (chitin scutes with grooves and domed cells from a 3D Worley field, panel seams with rivet nodes on plating / gear, satin folds on the cloak, pitting). `atlas.bake` then casts one ray per texel along the low-poly normal (`hipoly.sample`, mathutils BVH, cage 1.2 cm) and writes the high-poly tangent-space normal (UDN-blended with the procedural micro relief, `atlas.HI_GAIN`) plus a high-passed cavity term (grime in grooves, AO, roughness) into the existing albedo / ORM / normal atlases (2K). pip `bpy` has no Cycles, hence the numpy / BVH baker. `QT_NOHIPOLY=1` skips it. Builds are byte-identical (player, operative and citizen re-built twice and compared by SHA-256). Cost: about 70-80 s per character on 4 cores.
- **Hero Kestrel (17.8k triangles, limit 18k):** stepped four-lamella pauldrons with edge glow, tall blade and forward spikes, flared gorget, standing collar with glow strip, mantle over the shoulders, a wider eight-ribbon three-row cloak with pointed hems (short glow tips, no long vein lines), hip skirt plates, thigh armour, knee guards and amber joint rings, thicker thighs and shins, toe caps, elbow rings, thicker four-finger hands with knuckle spheres and a back-of-hand plate, a fuller grip, trigger guard and hand rest. **Trooper (8.3k, limit 12k):** three-lamella pauldrons (left heavier, width kept under the 1.1 m test), breast and rib carapace plates, heavy thigh plates, knee rings and caps, greaves, elbow and shin joint rings, serrated outer mandibles, a second horn pair, a neck frill, a tail-root plate. Bones, empties, one draw call, the rig contract and the animation rig are unchanged.
- **Citizens (`citizen.glb`, no texture), 13 archetypes (wave 15, art):** ONE GLB with two meshes, `Citizen` (816 triangles with every optional piece) and `CitizenFar` (258), each ONE surface, coloured by COLOR_0 (R surface, G piece id / 20, B baked shadow, A part tag; contract in `model_citizen.py`). Pieces: hood / helmet shell, cowl, coat tail, backpack, crest, scarf, visor, mantle, cap peak, wide hat, vendor tray with glowing wares, pauldrons, antennae. The vertex stage (`prop_crowd_core.gdshaderinc`) picks the archetype from the seed (the crowd gives slot i the group i % 6; the fraction of seed * 6 picks inside the group: `ARCH_OPT` / `ARCH_CUT`, so `ambient_crowd.gd` needed no change): 0 commuter, 1 hoodie, 2 umbrella carrier, 3 tall hybrid, 4 courier, 5 heavy, 6 sylph (very tall, long neck, small head, long arms, antennae), 7 stocky porter, 8 mantled monk (cape, hood, robe, long skull, gliding walk), 9 vendor (wide hat, tray carried in front), 10 worker (hi-vis vest with reflective bands, hard hat), 11 guard (armour, helmet, pauldrons, red visor; rare), 12 child (0.62 scale, big head). Tables per archetype: `WEAR` (pieces), `BUILD`, `HEADS` (head scale + neck), `LIMBS` (arm length, leg / arm swing, bounce), `GAIT` (stride rate, lean, waddle, twist), species skins, coat / accent / eye palettes. Animation state from `INSTANCE_CUSTOM.z`: 0 idle (by the seed: talk with a gesturing hand and nodding head, stand with weight shifts, check a wrist device, hands clasped; driven by `TIME`), 0.5..1.2 walk, > 1.5 flee (hands up and flapping, or sprint with lean), **< 0 cower** (crouch, hands over the head, trembling; the crowd does not send it yet: `_fill` would write a negative amplitude), and `w >= 2` (bit 1 of the flags, umbrella is bit 0) makes an idle person talk (for a chatting group; also not sent yet). Umbrellas show only on archetypes 0..5. Crowd draws unchanged (2, +2 mirrored on HIGH). Triangle cost: the near mesh 734 -> 816 and the far 251 -> 258 (hidden caps and the far collar were removed to stay inside `test_world`'s MED 160k / HIGH 260k gate: MED 159.8k, HIGH 254.9k). Tests: `test_citizen_models` (headless), `tools/citizen_check.sh` (renders the states and silhouettes, Vulkan and Compat), look-dev `tests/citizen_lineup.tscn` (`QT_VIEW=walk|idle|talk|flee|cower|far|all|arch<N>`, `QT_HALF=0|1`, `QT_ANGLE`).
- **Reflection:** the pose-following `SkinnedMirror` (shared skeleton, one draw within range, MED and HIGH) already existed and stays; the new hero and trooper meshes use it unchanged (coarse LOD). Nothing was added.

## Interiors as implemented (generator and renderer; `scripts/interiors/**`, `scenes/interiors/interior_view.tscn`, `assets/shaders/interior_{surface,window,glow}.gdshader`; tests `test_interiors`, `test_interiors_world`; tool `tools/interior_shots.sh`)
- **Seam:** `docs/interiors_interface.md` is the contract with the gameplay layer (doors in the city, enter / exit, fades, vendors, actors, missions; the doc says `origin` = (900, 600, 900), the layer now passes (327.5, 0, 0) = street level in the strip east of the grid with the city hidden while inside; the generator is origin-independent and tested at both; the layer frees the node on exit). The generator provides `InteriorGen.build(type, seed, origin, quality) -> Interior` (a node, not yet in the tree) and nothing else; it plays no audio, adds no environment, sun or camera, and a node freed by the caller releases everything it made.
- **Types (`InteriorGen.TYPES`, data in `InteriorKinds.SPECS`):** `weapons` (pawn and arms), `clinic`, `tech` (repair den), `bar` (noodle bar), `club`, `lobby` (hotel), `apartment`, `checkpoint` (Authority / Vael), plus `bodega` and `warehouse`. Each is 1-4 rooms; the first (street) room has the front wall at +Z with the door and the shop windows. A recipe is a list of rooms with prop programs (counter with vendor behind it, wall rows, shelf aisles, table sets, decor, ceiling pipes, hanging lamps, dance floor, light-show beams, safe / terminal / hostage / officer) and a palette (linear, dark on purpose).
- **Generation (`InteriorPlan`, pure data, `generate(type, seed)` is deterministic, the tier never changes it; `signature()` is what the tests compare):** layout (rooms one wall thickness apart, 3.1 m or more of shared wall, footprint <= 34 m, centred) -> doors (1.4 m street door, 1.3 m between rooms, 2.35 m tall, an extra door between neighbours at 60 %) -> wall segments (a shared wall exists once, each face takes its room's colour) -> windows (street windows every 0.9 m of free wall, side windows rarely) -> prop programs -> decor pass -> anchors -> lighting -> colliders -> walkability repair (flood fill on a 0.25 m grid with a 0.35 m body from the door; unreachable anchors are moved, otherwise the last big prop is dropped; 8 props were dropped over the 60 test plans) -> entry. Anchors: `vendor civilian guard safe hostage terminal officer cover` with the minimum counts of the interface table (`InteriorKinds.GUARANTEED`); `door_pos` is 1.5 m inside the street door facing into the room, `exit_pos` 0.25 m from the door.
- **Rendering: three draw calls per interior.** `InteriorMesher` merges everything with the world's `MeshBuilder`: SURFACE (walls with baseboard / dado rail / crown geometry, floors in 0.5-1.5 m cells with baked ambient occlusion from walls and props, ceilings, door and window frames, strips, every prop), WINDOW (panes), GLOW (additive halos and the club beams). Vertex data: COLOR rgb = albedo, alpha = emission, UV.x = surface class (wall, floor, ceiling, metal, soft, neon, dance floor, screen, strip, sign), UV.y = ceiling height or a phase. `interior_surface.gdshader` bakes the look: ambient colour, up to 8 fake light pools (distance falloff, half-lambert with a floor, glossy floor reflection of each pool; LOW 4 / MED 6 / HIGH 8), grime streaks, tile grout, puddles, panel seams, neon dropouts, scanline screens, animated dance floor; it ignores the moon (`light()` skips directional lights), fog and the environment's ambient, so an interior looks the same under any `WorldEnvironment` (the city's tonemap and bloom still apply). `interior_window.gdshader` fakes the neon street with parallax (building silhouettes, lit windows, neon bars, rain streaks) on an unshaded pane. Compat: vertex colours are read as sRGB there, so the shaders get `compat = 1` and convert them back (mean frame luma Compat 42 / Mobile 50 after the fix, 15 before).
- **Tiers:** a tier only changes how many DETAIL props are drawn (rank cut 0.35 / 0.75 / 1.0, a fixed per-prop rank), the floor cell size (1.5 / 0.75 / 0.5 m), beam segments, pool count and the number of real lights (LOW 1, else 2: one omni near the middle of the street room in the palette's key colour, one accent in neon; never shadowed; every mesh has shadow casting off). `Interior.set_quality(level)` rebuilds only the mesh (colliders unchanged).
- **Collision:** one `StaticBody3D` ("Colliders", layer 1 = `Game.L_WORLD`, mask 0) with 23-50 `BoxShape3D`: wall pieces (only doors cut them; windows are solid), corner posts, a plug that closes the street door, a slab per ceiling, the floor slab, and one box per big prop (a scanner gate is two posts and a lintel). Hero capsule queries at the door, every anchor and every inner doorway are free (`test_interiors`).
- **Shared edits (each tiny, inert unless an interior is in the tree; all through `InteriorSpace`, `scripts/interiors/interior_space.gd`, which an `Interior` registers on `_enter_tree` and clears on `_exit_tree`, new game, respawn and next sector):** `PlayerUtil.outside_city` / `safe_spot` (the city rescue and the teleport clamp leave an interior alone), `Drone._step` (enemies are moved by code: inside an interior they slide along its walls and props instead of the street map's clamp and buildings), `Drone/Operative._ground_y` (they stand on `InteriorSpace.floor_y`, not on the street), `EnemyCover.find` (the interior's tall props are the cover), `CameraRig` (on-foot arm x0.68, floor 0.22 m, and a ray from the head that pulls a slid or shaken camera out of walls; the arm still hits interior props and walls because they are world-layer bodies).
- **Audio hooks (data only):** `Interior.ambience()` returns `{dedicated, loops [[sfx, db]], oneshots [[sfx, min_s, max_s, db]], muffle}`; every name exists in `assets/audio` (club: the three music stems as a beat, bar and lobby: `babble`, warehouse: `rain_metal` and metal clanks ...); `interior_<type>` is the name a dedicated loop will have once recorded.
- **Caches:** the plans of the last 6 doors and the mesh sets of the last 3 (type, seed, tier) stay alive (`InteriorGen.clear_cache()`), so re-entering the shop you just left builds in about 1 ms; nodes, shapes and materials are created per entry.
- **Measured (software Vulkan / headless, not a phone):** all 10 types x seeds 1-3 x 3 tiers: at most 4 draws, triangles LOW 4.7 k / MED 6.4 k / HIGH 8.1 k at most, plan + meshes + nodes 28 ms worst cold, 52 ms worst per enter over 50 cycles. Real frames with the hero in view (club, warehouse, tech, then lobby on Compat): 3-5 visible draws and 21.8-25.1 k primitives (the hero is 17.8 k), no shadow passes, 40 MB texture memory (nothing is textured). 50 enter / exit cycles: nodes, objects and resources identical before and after. The real camera was sampled in 720 poses (door, anchors, flush against every wall, doorways, 4 yaws x 2 pitches) with no sample inside a wall or prop, none with something between head and camera, none outside the room. An operative fights the hero inside a checkpoint for 15 s and never leaves the footprint, the floor or the walls.
- **Known limits:** the fade, the door markers and the return to the exact street door are the gameplay layer's (the generator gives `exit_pos` / `door_pos` / `door_yaw`); the first interior of a session compiles three shaders (not pre-warmed); props are boxes and cylinders (the contract for authored models is the same prop dictionary); the hero and enemies are still lit by the city's moon through the ceiling on LOW (no shadows there; the interior shader ignores it, theirs does not); windows show a generic neon street, not the real one outside that door.

## Interior gameplay as implemented (doors, transitions, vendors, actors, missions; `scripts/interiors_gameplay/**`, `scripts/world/city_doors.gd`; tests `test_interior_gameplay`, `test_interior_gameplay_vendors`, `test_interior_gameplay_missions`; bot `tests/bot_interior_gameplay.gd`)
- **Seam:** `docs/interiors_interface.md`. The gameplay layer only uses `InteriorGen.build(type, seed, origin, quality)` and the `Interior` properties of that file (`door_pos door_yaw exit_pos bounds floor_rects wall_rects anchors()`); when the generator is missing or returns something invalid it falls back to `InteriorStub` (a box room that implements the same interface). **Where the room lives:** `InteriorGameplay.ORIGIN = (327.5, 0, 0)`, street level in the strip east of the grid (x 310..345: no lot, prop or road collider there), not the (900, 600, 900) some generator notes still mention. While inside: `Game.world.visible = false` (no city draw calls), cars frozen and hidden, rain 0, street enemies stored and released (the interior has the whole actor cap) and unspawned patrol groups held back; all restored on exit.
- **Doors (`CityDoors.compute`, `City.doors`):** about one lot in five (50 of 232) gets one door on a street-facing wall (same two faces as the awnings), deterministic from its own hashes (the layout's random stream and every other placement are untouched), one within 100 m of the spawn, every one of the 10 types present. `{id (= interior seed), type, name, wall, pos (sidewalk, 1.3 m out), normal, yaw, face_yaw}`. `DoorMarkers`: ONE MultiMesh for all doors (a door-frame sheet on the facade and a pulsing pad, additive shader `door_glow.gdshader`, colour per type, mission doors brighter, rumour-hinted doors brightest).
- **Flow (`InteriorGameplay`, a Node added by main.gd before Cinematics):** within 2.8 m of a door and no car within 4.2 m, `InteriorGameplay.prompt` = "ENTER <name>" (HUD `hint_text`, touch USE via `_use_near`); USE / F fades out 0.26 s, builds the room, teleports (hp, energy, weapons, ammo and heat untouched; `Game.quiet` is held at 0 inside so heat never decays), fades in. Aware hunters within 30 m of the door (at most 2) follow in one at a time; hunters still aware near the exit follow out. Inside, USE does the nearest of: mission action, TALK to the vendor (3.2 m), LEAVE (2.2 m of `exit_pos`). Death inside: `Game.respawn` puts the player at the street door and restores the stored enemies; new game / next sector / title screen close the interior; teleporting out by other means (TEST menu) closes it quietly. Blocked while an Overseer is alive, a contract's enemies are alive (they live in the street pool the interior empties), in a car or dead.
- **People (`InteriorActors`, `InteriorCivilian`, cap 3..5 per `InteriorData.PLAN`):** vendor (stays behind the counter, turns to the player, amber diamond), civilians (primitive merged mesh, drift between anchors, run to the far corner on gunfire), Vael guards = the existing `operative` from the EnemyManager, standing far from the door, patrolling between guard anchors, stealthy (`Drone.aware_dist` 5 m instead of 32, back to 32 once aware; heat > 0 still wakes them within 80 m), plus followers. Shared edit: `Drone.aware_dist` (2 lines in `drone.gd`).
- **Vendors (`VendorLogic`, `VendorScreen`):** weapon dealer (the three alien weapons, ammo packs, damage), clinic (full repair, coherence dose, armour, regen), tech den (blink, reload, magazine, coherence cell), bar (rumours: a story line over the radio, every second one marks the nearest open mission door on the street glow and the minimap; synth drink), corner store (kits). Prices follow `Progress.rep_discount`; sold out = owned / maxed / full reserve. The window is its own canvas layer that hit-tests taps itself (no Control buttons in this project), pauses the game (`Game.state = paused`, `InteriorGameplay.vendor_open` keeps the HUD pause screen away), closes on CLOSE / ESC / F / B, keys 1-9 buy a row.
- **Missions (`InteriorMissions`):** lobby = crack a safe (5 s beside it, decays 0.25/s away, the alarm raises heat and wakes the guards), apartment = kill the three guards then free the hostage, club = find (7 m) and kill a 240 hp hidden officer (minimap shows only an area until found), checkpoint = hold a terminal 16 s while one guard is alerted at 20 / 50 / 80 %. Rewards `InteriorData.reward` (sector and reputation factors like contracts) via `Progress.add_credits / bonus_score / add_reputation`, counted as a done contract; completion is remembered per door (`done`), saved in `user://interiors.cfg` with "inside", the hinted doors and the rumour index (written on enter, exit, completion, purchases, pause and death; CONTINUE re-enters the saved interior; a new campaign clears it). Leaving or dying resets the mission.
- **Radio and minimap:** interior lines per type, mission stage and "followed in" are plain data in `InteriorData` (<= 112 characters, tested), sent through `Story.say` and recorded in `Story.shown`; queued interior lines are dropped when the player changes place. `InteriorMinimap` (hooked at the top of `Minimap._draw`) draws the floor and wall rects as rotated quads at 24 m range with the exit, vendor, civilians, guards and mission markers. HUD priority line = the mission line while inside; contract offers are paused (`Contracts._can_offer`).
- **Shared edits (all tiny):** `main.gd` (+1 line), `city.gd` (`doors`), `drone.gd` (`aware_dist`), `hud.gd` (4 hooks: door/vendor prompt, mission line, vendor window layer), `minimap.gd` (3 lines), `touch_controls.gd` (1 line), `contracts.gd` (`_can_offer`), `progress.gd` (`continued`: the last new game was a CONTINUE), `test_menu.gd` (INTERIORS tab: GO entries for all 10 types, COMPLETE THE MISSION, LEAVE). 
- **Measured (software Vulkan, Mobile and Compatibility, MED, not a phone):** whole frame with HUD inside any of the 10 interiors 80-96 draws / 24-51 k primitives (Compat 80-107), the open trade window 116-117 draws (its text), the street at a door 107-115 draws. Headless tests: 3 files, about 220 checks; `test_integration`'s node ceiling went from 1100 to 1200 (the composed game was already 1117 nodes without this layer, which adds 16).
- **Known limits:** civilians are primitive silhouettes (not the citizen model), they cannot be shot or hurt; the street's cars and traffic stay frozen while you are inside; stored enemies come back where they stood (patrol memory is not exact); doors are a glow on a facade (no door mesh or open animation); the hostage and officer are plain operatives (no unique model); rumours are a fixed pool of 8; contracts and cores keep their markers on the street while you are inside (the minimap hides them).


## Tier-scaled PBR surfaces as implemented (`tools/textures/{gen_surfaces.py,build_surfaces.sh}`, `assets/textures/surface_*`, `assets/shaders/surface_tex.gdshaderinc`, `scripts/world/surface_textures.gd`, `PerfTiers.surface_*`; tests `test_world.gd` `_test_surfaces`, gate `PerfProbe.TEX_MB_MAX`)
- **Generator:** `tools/textures/gen_surfaces.py` (numpy + Pillow, fixed seeds, periodic FFT noise and periodic Worley cells, about 80 s) writes 8 sets of two PNGs plus a decal mask, about 78 MB on disk: asphalt, sidewalk, brick (2048 px), concrete, corrugated sheet, painted steel, roof gravel/tar (1024 px), a 2048 x 1024 trim atlas (4 x 2 cells of 512 px: bezel, awning, mullion gasket, neon channel, shutter slats, LED cabinet, peeling trim, louvres) and `surface_decals.png` (R oil and tyre polish, G puddle tide-mark rings, B litter flecks, A gutter grime). `tools/textures/build_surfaces.sh` = generate + `tools/import.sh` + `--patch-imports` (VRAM compressed, high quality = ASTC 4x4 / BPTC, mipmaps, `compress/normal_map=2` so the packed data is not treated as a 2-channel normal map: with `0` (detect) the B and A channels were lost) + import.
- **Packing (one albedo tap + one normal tap per surface):** `_albedo` RGB = detail MODULATOR (median luminance 0.5; the shader multiplies the world's own colour and macro tone by 2 x RGB, so palette, weathering and day-night stay procedural), A = dry roughness; `_normal` RG = tangent slope, B = cavity (1 on the typical surface), A = the WET variant's roughness (a water film fills the pits, only the aggregate tops stay rough). So dry and wet asphalt are one pair of textures; `wetness` selects the roughness map and the shader damps the micro relief by 65 % when wet.
- **Where it is sampled (all with `textureGrad`, derivatives taken outside the branches, so there are no undefined derivatives at material borders; mip bias from `srf_bias`):** `ground_core.gdshaderinc` (road 3.2 m tile, sidewalk and plaza 2.4 m tile: albedo modulation, cavity, slope into `slab_n`, dry roughness, wet roughness `wet_r`; HIGH adds a second, rotated, larger scale of both and the decal mask at 12 m: oil darkens and polishes, tide marks darken, litter flecks and gutter grime within 4.5 m of the kerb); `building_core.gdshaderinc` (walls: brick tile 3.68 x 1.76 m is aligned to the shader's own 0.46 x 0.22 m brick layout with the half-brick offset, the procedural mortar fades out with `srf_wk`, other styles use concrete 2 m; roofs use the gravel/tar set; normals are added in the instance's local axes, `MODEL_MATRIX`; no sampling in the mirror or far variants); `prop_core.gdshaderinc` (planar projection on the dominant axis of the face; metal pieces use painted steel or, for half of them by seed, corrugated sheet; plastic and concrete take painted steel at 55 %; the LED screen bezel (code 0.60) takes the trim atlas, cell (1, 1)). Shopfront shutters, billboards and the other trim cells are generated but not sampled yet.
- **Tiers (`PerfTiers.SURFACE_TAPS` 0 / 1 / 2, `SURFACE_MIP_BIAS` 0 / 0 / -0.3, the viewport's anisotropy 2x / 4x / 8x and mip bias still apply):** LOW samples nothing and loads nothing (`srf_k` = 0, `ground_lite` has no samplers: the procedural look and its memory stay), MED one pair per surface with the textures at half size (`SurfaceTextures.tex` cuts the top mip level of the 2K sets through `TextureBudget._halved`), HIGH two pairs on the ground plus the decal mask, full size. The taps fade to the procedural look: ground 32 to 95 m, walls 45 to 130 m, roofs 30 to 100 m, props 22 to 65 m. `City.set_quality` calls `_apply_surfaces()`; `SurfaceTextures.apply()` sets `srf_k`, `srf_hi`, `srf_bias` and the samplers; `QT_SURFACES=0` in the environment switches every tap off (before / after captures, A/B numbers).
- **Measured (software Vulkan, `tools/perf_report.sh`, staged view):** the surfaces add NO draw calls and NO triangles (MED 131 / 295k with and without them, bit-identical). Texture memory MED 188 -> 201 MB (+13), HIGH 302 MB, LOW 153 MB (unchanged by this area). `SurfaceTextures.estimate_bytes()`: MED about 21 MB estimated (+13 MB measured), HIGH about 56 MB. The old gate allowed 600 MB (MED); `PerfProbe.TEX_MB_MAX` is now 200 / 260 / 360 MB. Compatibility renderer checked (MED street and sidewalk frames render with the same sets, no shader or sampler error). Samplers added to a shader: ground 5, buildings 6, props 6 (Compatibility has 16 units).
- **Open:** the MED scene total (201 MB) is above the 150 MB aim because the model atlases alone were 188 MB (not this area); on a phone the 2K ASTC sets and the pair of fragment taps are unmeasured; brick and roof gravel were only checked in code (the staged views show no brick wall); shopfront and billboard trim beyond the LED bezel, shutters from the corrugated set and a trim-based awning are not wired; only the ground has a second scale at HIGH.

### Wave 10B integration (as implemented)
Cinematics, character models and PBR surfaces compose without extra systems. `tests/bot_cine_integ.gd` drives the real game through every scene and asserts that state, `CameraRig.is_cinematic()`, hero position, `Engine.time_scale` and input return to normal. `PerfProbe.BUDGETS` were raised to MED 300k triangles / 160 death draws and LOW 118 death draws to cover the heavier hero and troopers (BUDGETS.md updated).

## Phone HUD and combat feedback as implemented (`scripts/ui/{hud,ui_util,touch_controls}.gd`, `autoload/fx.gd` muzzle part, `assets/shaders/fx_muzzle.gdshader`; tests `test_hud`, `test_touch`)
- Text floor raised: `UiUtil.TEXT_MIN` 26 and `BAR_LABEL` 22 (px at 720 p). Ammo 48, bars 14 px high on 30 px rows, weapon plate 36 px, desktop ability chips 152x44, credits 26. Touch default label 26, hit radius = max(r, `UiUtil.TOUCH_MIN`/2) x 1.15 (every button at least 64 px across; smallest is SWAP at 96), pause button 72 px, moved left of the heat plate so it no longer sits on the weapon-slot column.
- `UiUtil.insets` keeps `SAFE_WIDE` (24 base px) free on both sides at aspect >= 1.9 (20:9) even when the OS reports no inset. On touch the ammo box is shifted left of the button cluster using the 430 px weapon plate's half width.
- Feedback: hit-confirm X is bigger (22-30 px, 4.5 px wide, dark under-stroke, pops outward over 0.28 s, red on crit/kill, longer arms on kill) and the HUD gives the camera a small tick via `FX.shake` (0.07, kill 0.16; no allocation). Damage direction: a thick red arc (radius 30% of height) with a dark under-arc and a chevron tip, 1.0 s. Recoil kick already lives in `CameraRig.kick` (player_weapon).
- Muzzle flash: `FX.muzzle_flash` caps scale at `MUZZLE_CENTRE_MAX` 0.7 and brightness (shader `gain`) to `MUZZLE_CENTRE_GAIN` 0.4 when the flash is near the view axis and within about 8-38 m of the camera; impact sparks and distant flashes keep full strength.

## Hero / enemy silhouette clean-up as implemented (`tools/blender/{model_player,model_operative}.py`, `scripts/player/player_body.gd` materials, `scripts/enemies/{enemy_tells,enemy_marks,specialist}.gd`; tests `test_enemies`, `test_enemy_types`, bot phases 10-11 of `tests/bot_characters.gd`)
- **Hero value structure:** the pale pearl plates and every spike (pauldron blades, forward spikes, cheek blades, chin / hip / knee / heel / elbow spurs, dorsal ridges), the phase-halo ring and its struts, the collar / skirt / pauldron glow strips, the limb veins, amber knee / elbow rings and the glowing cloak tips are gone (15.1k triangles, was 17.8k). Armour is DARK slate (`SKIN` / `SKIN2` / `PEARL` / `PEARL2` = mid slate plates, `HELM` the lightest value so the obsidian faceplate reads against it); cyan only on the faceplate slit, the chest core, the spine line, one torso vein pair and the rifle coil, amber only on the belt, wrists and rifle cell; the standing collar is dark, the cloak hem is straight and solid (no jagged strips). `emit_strength` 1.5 -> 1.25. `PlayerBody` rim 0.16 -> 0.22 with `rim_tint` 0.2 (a cool edge on dark armour, not a pale outline); the character rim light is unchanged.
- **Texture breakup:** `atlas.PAD = 8` (was 4) is set by `model_player.py` and `model_operative.py` before `export_model` (dilation PAD + 1), so UV islands no longer bleed into each other at mip 3+ (10 m and beyond) or on the LOW 1K read. Cars, drones and pickups keep PAD 4 (their GLBs are unchanged). `post_import.gd` already forces mipmapped anisotropic filtering.
- **Enemy tells without HUD rings (`EnemyTells`):** one static, cached, additive unshaded mesh per role (vertex colours are 8 bit, so `GLOW` multiplies them through the material) added as a child of the model by `Specialist.setup`: flanker = swept orange speed streaks, sniper = a big magenta cross-flare eye, a short sight beam and a tall antenna with a lit tip, brute = heat cracks across the chest, hot shoulder slabs and a spine seam. 96-240 vertices each, no per-frame work (`Specialist._animate` only hides it when dead). The shield trooper's tell is its plate: a `QuadMesh` with the cached 64 px hex-cell + bright-frame texture (`EnemyTells.shield_texture`), brighter on hits. Each specialist is still exactly operative + 1 mesh (test updated: shield, flanker, sniper and brute all `base + 1`).
- **Rings are the fallback (`EnemyMarks`):** the threat ring of an enemy is drawn only when it is off screen (`Camera3D.is_position_in_frustum`), occluded by the world (a cached `L_WORLD` ray per enemy every `OCC_EVERY` = 6 frames, staggered, one reused query) or farther than `RING_RANGE` = 40 m (a few pixels). Muzzle flare, aim line, health bars and death shock rings are unchanged. The hit-flash and aware / unaware dashing of the ring are therefore only seen in those cases.
- **Look-dev:** `BOT_PHASES=10,11 tools/bot.sh res://tests/bot_characters.gd <prefix> 45 2` stages the four specialists frozen (`TestMode.freeze`, facing the hero) at 7 / 11 / 16 / 24 m in the game camera (10) and at 4-5 m in a row (11).

## Signage and facade depth as implemented (world / signage engineer)
- **Glyph atlas (`tools/textures/gen_signage.py`, numpy + Pillow, no randomness, `--patch-imports` after `tools/import.sh` sets lossless + mipmaps):** a monoline "alien-latin" font on a 4 x 6 unit grid (chamfered corners, flat terminals; A-Z, 0-9, `- . : ! / + & ' < > * ? = _`) written as a DISTANCE field to the nearest stroke (`assets/textures/signage_atlas.png`, 384 x 512, 8 x 8 cells of 48 x 64 px, R = 1 - d / 1.5 units, 18 KB, 0.26 MB with mips, every tier). One tap per text pixel gives a tube of any width, a white-hot core and a halo. The same script GENERATES `assets/shaders/signage_words.gdshaderinc` (word table `SW_LEN/SW_CH`, `SW_SHORT` = the words of 7 letters or fewer, `SW_WARN` = warnings from NO ENTRY on, `SW_VAEL`, the LED ticker string `MQ_CH`), so indexes cannot drift from the atlas. Vocabulary: NOODLES CLINIC ARMS HOTEL CLUB 24H TECH BAR PAWN RAMEN CAFE SYNTH MARKET REPAIR NEON DATA BIO LAB QUANTA BAR OPEN 24H, warnings NO ENTRY / VAEL WATCH / CURFEW 22 (hazard red-orange).
- **Shader side (`signage.gdshaderinc`):** `sign_cell` (one atlas tap, `textureLod` with a footprint LOD, no derivatives inside), `sign_word` (a line of text with letter advance squeezed to fit the bay, per-letter dying tubes through `tube_fail`), `sign_ticker`, `sign_colour`. `SIGN_TEXT` is undefined on the mirrored copies and the far-LOD towers (they get a flat glow / bars instead, the atlas sampler is simply unused). Shop signs (`building_core`, band at `SIGN_Y0 = 3.9` m, above the awnings' top edge): a word per bay from a hash (1 in 14 bays a warning), dead tubes on dying signs, unlit signs keep a 3 % ghost of their letters. Blade and roof signs (`prop_core`, emission code 0.70): the word is stacked letter by letter, the board size is read from the instance scale (`MODEL_MATRIX`), so the taller roof signs keep proportions. LED screens and poster boxes (`screen_content(uv, seed, t, aa, aspect)`, now 4 kinds): equalizer bars, a real-text ticker, radial pulse, and a 6 s "ad loop" (shop word zooms in, second word with a scan bar, VAEL WATCH with blinking hazard stripes); the panel aspect comes from `quad_aspect()` (UV and world-position derivatives) on props and from the wall rectangle on buildings. Binding: `SurfaceTextures.apply(mat, [..., "sign"], tier)` in `City._apply_surfaces`.
- **Facade depth:** ribbed metal cladding on about 40 % of the non-brick buildings (`building_core`, detail-gated so LOW and the far / mirror copies skip it): 0.25 m vertical ribs in the spandrel panels as a height-field bump plus a darker groove, metal 0.35 / rough 0.45, faded by pixel footprint and distance (no shimmer). `FacadeKit` (still ONE draw call) grew from 4 to 8 modules: stringcourse ledge and the shop-floor cornice as ONE instance per wall (scaled to the wall width instead of one per bay), satellite dish, street cabinet, caged wall lamp (emission code 0.15). The prop shader decodes the module as `floor(INSTANCE_CUSTOM.w * 8)`. The kit window shrank 62 -> 48 m because every instance pays for the whole mesh (154 triangles per instance now, was about 94). `RoofTanks` (one draw, mesh unchanged) now varies per instance (squat to standard height, 0.9-1.25 footprint, from the instance's own seed; a three-module tank mesh with a silo and a smoke stack was tried and dropped: +90 triangles on every one of the 93 instances pushed `test_world` over its 160 k spawn budget).
