- **Measured (Mobile renderer, software Vulkan, MED, `tests/bot_world.gd --cam=... --stats`, same pose, street view along the avenue):** visible primitives 173.2 k before vs 172.7 k after (the kit is 16.6 k of it, measured with `--hide=FacadeKit`); `test_world` spawn triangles 159.0 k (159.4 k before), draws unchanged (no new families); LOW 98.6 k prims / 50 draws and the Compatibility renderer (177.6 k prims, 80 draws, no shader errors) render the new signs. First attempt (per-bay ledges and cornices, radius 62) added 36 k primitives and was reworked to the numbers above.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_signage.gd` (atlas size and stroke coverage, parsed word table consistency, vocabulary, short-word rule, kit and tank module encodings, sign atlas bound to every lit material, kit modules present).
- **Open:** text legibility was judged from 1280 x 720 software frames, not on a phone; far-away LED screens are still the old bars when `FAR` (no text on the skyline copies); fire escapes, awnings and balconies were not changed; the facade kit lamps are emissive only (no real light); hologram columns still use pseudo-glyphs by choice.

- **Optimisation pass (as implemented, perf area):** `PerfTiers.shadow_range()` is per tier (LOW none, MED 16 m, HIGH 25 m; used by `Drone.update_lod` and `Vehicle.update_lod`), so the far troopers/drones/cars of the staged view stop casting on MED (their skinned shadow proxies were about 21k triangles and 2-3 draws per cascade). Pickup light columns are capped per tier (`Missions.COLUMN_CAP` LOW 1 / MED 2 / HIGH 8), and `Drone.DROP_DELAY` is 0.66 s so the pickups of a wipe appear after the explosion peak instead of stacking on it. Gate re-tightened in `PerfProbe.BUDGETS`: LOW 100 draws / 160k / death 115, MED 140 / 280k / death 150, HIGH 160 / 430k / death 180. Measured (software Vulkan, whole frame, before -> after): LOW 94 draws / 135k -> 95 / 132k, death 112 -> 106; MED 129 / 292k -> 126 / 268k, death 156 / 319k -> 146 / 294k; HIGH 135 / 395k -> 135 / 389k, death 169 -> 162; Compat MED 143 draws / 269k, death 154.

## Art library as implemented (wave 16: districts, street kit, landmarks, district sidewalks; `scripts/world/{city_districts,city_kit,city_kit_window,city_landmarks}.gd`, `assets/shaders/prop_kit.gdshader`, `tools/textures/gen_surfaces.py`; tests `test_art_library`, bot `tests/bot_art_library.gd`)
- **Districts (`CityDistricts`, pure data):** the four quadrants of the grid (x = 0 and z = 0 are road centre lines), numbered and named like the music layer (`Sfx.DISTRICTS`: 0 market x<0,z<0 / 1 foundry x>=0,z<0 / 2 slums x<0,z>=0 (the spawn is here) / 3 towers x>=0,z>=0). Each has a palette (prop paint tint, two glow accents, awning fabrics, facade multiplier, neon index, sidewalk slab tint, lamp bias), a prop mix (weights per kit kind) and a landmark. Where it reaches the world: facade tint and 60 % of the lots' `neon` index (`city_buildings.gd`, so ground spill and signs follow), cool street-lamp variants in the towers (`city_props.gd`; the random stream is untouched), a per-district sidewalk slab tint in the ground shader (`dist_walk0..3`, also on LOW), the paint of every kit prop.
- **Street kit (16 new props, one draw call):** standpipe, vent stack, recycler bin, bench, vending kiosk, glow bus pod, cable spool, antenna mast, billboard, hex planter with glowing flora, striped barrier gate, market stall (three awning fabrics per district), transformer, charge post, crates, lantern pole (40-170 triangles each, tested). A MultiMesh per kind would cost a draw call per kind in view and the MED frame has about three to spare, so the props are NOT MultiMeshes: `CityKitWindow` concatenates the cached local geometry of the props in the cells (20 m) around the camera into one ArrayMesh in one `MeshInstance3D` (`StreetKit`, no shadow, no mirror copy), re-filled when the camera has moved 10 m (about 1 ms: one native `Transform3D * PackedVector3Array` per prop, no nodes, no allocation of scene objects). LOD is per cell inside that single mesh: cells within `NEAR_BASE` 64 m x tier scale (LOW 0.65, MED 0.85, HIGH 1.0) get the full-detail geometry of every prop, cells out to `FAR_BASE` 175 m only the simplified geometry (14-32 triangles) of the five kinds that have one (vent stack, bus pod, antenna, billboard, stall, each with its own reach), everything else stops. The per-prop seed (grime, wares, screen content) rides as vertex attribute CUSTOM0.x (`#define KIT` in `prop_core.gdshaderinc`, material `prop_kit.gdshader`; Compat renders it); the kit gets a stronger fill (`col * 0.2`) than the street furniture so painted furniture reads at night.
- **Placement (`CityKit.build`, deterministic seed 160016, 1,108 new props + 85 folded ones):** props stand just inside the kerb of each block face (front towards the street, yaw from the face normal) or in the alley cross; every candidate is rejected inside a building (`lot_at`), on the road, near plazas and near the old street furniture (read back from the family buffers), and against its own spacing grid. Lit kinds add a few sprites to the shared Glow family (325; they are mirrored for free there).
- **Folded families:** hydrants, lit kiosks and vent grates are no longer MultiMesh families: `Props.kit.fold_family` hands their meshes and instances to the window (three draw calls less, and the district palette does not touch them). Their pooled colliders keep the old family names (`Hydrants`, `Kiosks`), the steam vents and kiosk glow sprites are unchanged.
- **Colliders:** `Colliders.build(parent, parts, extra)` takes extra instance lists (`Kit.collider_sources()`: family `Kit<Name>` per kind, shapes in `Kit.KINDS[k].col`), so every kit prop is cover / an obstacle through the same 56-body pool (bins and benches are waist-high cover, masts and lamps block like poles).
- **Landmarks (`CityLandmarks`):** one silhouette per district on the empty plaza lot nearest to its target: Lantern Spire 124 m (market), Twin Stacks 113 m (foundry), Relay Tower 125 m (slums), Vael Needle 150 m (towers; all taller than the 88 m towers). Near geometry 320-630 triangles within 230 m, a 106-208 triangle silhouette beyond; they are merged into the same window mesh (no nodes, no extra draw call, kept at any distance), plus a solid core column each in the static body. No mirrored copy (one more draw each).
- **District sidewalks (2K variants):** `gen_surfaces.py` makes `walk_market` (hex flagstones), `walk_foundry` (tread plate with seams and rust) and `walk_towers` (polished slabs), 2048 px tileable, same packed layout as the other sets; the slums keep the plain `sidewalk` set. The ground shader reads them as ONE texture array tap (`srf_walkv_a/n`, layer = district) instead of the single sidewalk pair (`SurfaceTextures.walk_array`: HIGH 2048 px layers, MED the 1024 px level of each (top mip cut off the compressed data), LOW loads nothing, fallback to the plain pair when the array cannot be built). Estimated memory MED 28 MB / HIGH 85 MB (the plain sidewalk pair is not loaded when the array is). Rebuild: `tools/textures/build_surfaces.sh --only walk_market,walk_foundry,walk_towers`.
- **Roof signs LOD (cheap fix):** `BladeSigns` radius 270 -> 230 m and `Billboards` 360 -> 290 m (both are mirrored families: about 4 k triangles less on MED at the spawn; the fog already hides them).
- **Measured (software Vulkan, `PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2`, whole frame, before -> after):** LOW 95 draws / 132k -> 92 / 134k, death 110; MED 126 / 269k -> 125 / 271k, death 146 -> 145; HIGH 135 / 390k -> 138 / 396k, death 162 -> 160; texture memory MED 200 -> 208 MB, HIGH 301 -> 322 MB (gate 360). Everything inside the gate; MED death and LOW death still have only 5 draws of headroom.
- **Open:** nothing was run on a phone (the window re-fill is about 1 ms here, a phone may need 3-5 ms every 10 m; the kit's far level pops at cell borders); the landmarks read faintly through the fog from street level (the relay tower most) and have no reflection; props are plain box-and-cylinder geometry; the per-prop LOD of the old roof families (FacadeKit wastes 7 of 8 modules per instance, about 14 k triangles at the spawn) was not touched; the new sidewalks were seen on software Vulkan only (HIGH two-scale tap uses the same layer for the rotated second scale).

## Rooftop traversal (scripts/player/traversal.gd)
`Player.traversal` (class Traversal): forward + jump next to an obstacle vaults (<= 1.25 m, far side clear) or mantles (<= 2.5 m); an airborne player pushing into a ledge grabs it; ladders are climb zones (`Traversal.zones`, filled by city_roofs.gd, one instanced `RoofLadders` family) ending in a mantle onto the roof. Scripted position moves in `_foot_tick`, `Game.state` play only; `teleport_to`/`reset` cancel it.

### Sky layers (sky.gdshader, driven by DayNight.sky_look)
Visible pass only (radiance stays cheap): sun disc, glow and shafts, day clouds lit by the sun, quantum ribbons (night, clear weather, not on LOW), planet silhouette, horizon skyline silhouette, stars. Uniforms `day`, `overcast`, `twilight_amount`, `aurora_amount`, `planet_amount`, `skyline_amount`, `lod` are set by `DayNight.apply` from the hour, `Weather.current` and the tier; fog colour greys with overcast. No extra draws.

## Demo mode (DEMO button on the title)
`scripts/demo/demo_mode.gd` (static `DemoMode`): `enter()` stashes the real profile, points Progress / MapScreen (`path_override`) / InteriorGameplay / Activities at `user://demo_*.cfg`, grants everything (credits, chapters, weapons, attachments, upgrades at max, suits, schemes, maxed garage car, full map, `DemoMode.active` unlocks every side mission) and starts a sandbox run (`Progress.begin_sandbox_run`, campaign runner disabled). `quit()` / state `menu` (ABORT TO MENU) restores real paths, profile, difficulty and TestMode toggles; the real save is never written. `scripts/ui/demo_panel.gd` (`DemoPanel`, added last in main.gd): DEMO badge/button (left edge) and the menu (chapters, side, activity, set piece, boss, event, contract, interior, spawn, tools with difficulty, god, infinite ammo, teleports, QUIT DEMO). Test: tests/test_demo_mode.gd.

## Hero verbs (scripts/player/hero_verbs.gd)
`Player.verbs` (HeroVerbs) is ticked from `_foot_tick` after the vertical velocity: WALL-RUN (airborne, hold toward a wall; jump kicks off), glide speed keep (cap is HeroPower.glide), ground SLAM (tap crouch in the air; shockwave damage and stagger by radius), WARP GRAB (hold `grab`, H / touch GRAB; lift an enemy, car or prop, release to throw; area damage on impact). Availability and strength: HeroPower.can_wall_run()/f("glide")/f("dmg")/cost(), per-verb numbers in HeroVerbs.DEFAULTS (a HeroPower row key of the same name overrides). Counters (slams, grabs, throws, impacts, wall_runs, wall_jumps) are what tests wait on. `Breakables.take_prop(i)` lifts a prop without debris. PlayerBody eases `verbs.pose_roll/pose_pitch`. `cancel()` runs on reset, teleport, vehicle and death.


## Melee, throwables and gadgets (scripts/combat/, scripts/ui/gear_screen.gd)
- `Gear` (static tables + saved state: owned throwables/gadgets, the equipped throwable and gadget, 4 loadouts {weapon, throw, gadget}); prices in `Economy.THROW_PRICE` / `GADGET_PRICE` (`Economy.gear_price`, also in `gear_items()`); `Progress.buy_gear / equip_gear / save_loadout / apply_loadout`; saved in a new `[gear]` section (missing or damaged = defaults, no SAVE_VERSION bump).
- Three child nodes of the Player (created in `Player._ensure_children`): `player.melee` (`Melee`: action `melee`, N / middle mouse / touch MELEE; tap = light, light, finisher chain, hold = heavy; cone hit, stagger, launch of non-bosses, one pooled additive blade arc), `player.throwables` (`Throwables`: action `throw_item`, J / touch THROW; singularity, mine, decoy, shroud, emp; at most 6 live, pooled lazy nodes, stock refills on new run / respawn / 25 s), `player.gadgets` (`Gadgets`: action `gadget`, K / touch GADGET; scanner pulse = one no-depth-test MultiMesh, drone buddy, grapple-teleport). All tick only in `play`.
- UI: pause > UPGRADES > LOADOUT > GEAR (`GearScreen`, `Hud._loadout_page`), 64 px buttons: `gt_<id>`, `gg_<id>`, `ls_<i>`, `lsave_<i>`, `gear_weapons`. TEST MENU LOOT tab and the DEMO tab list the gear (`TestMode.give_gear`, `give_all_gear`); demo mode grants all.
- Test: tests/test_melee_gadgets.gd (real scene).

## Materials v2 (shader-only surface realism; `assets/shaders/material_v2.gdshaderinc`, `scripts/common/materials_v2.gd`, test `tests/test_materials_v2.gd`)
- One include, ALU only (no texture, so texture memory is flat), used by `ground_core`, `building_core` and `prop_core`. Strength is the global `mv2_amount` (`MaterialsV2`: LOW 0.5 / MED 0.85 / HIGH 1.0, set by `PerfTiers.apply`; registered in `Game._ready`). TEST menu WORLD page and DEMO menu: MATERIALS V2 / V1 switch (`TestMode.set_materials_v2`); tools runs take `--mv2=0` for before shots.
- Terms: `mv2_macro` / `mv2_tint` (large-scale colour and roughness drift on ground, sidewalks, facades, props: kills tiling), `mv2_tri` (triplanar grime with a height-based tide line, dust on up faces), `mv2_edge` (edge wear from normal curvature on props), `mv2_road_decals` (repair patches, utility trenches, tar snakes, oil; macro height also biases the puddle mask), facade chipped edges / sun-bleached upper floors, `mv2_glass` + `mv2_pane_wobble` (skyline and haze reflection and a refraction-lite shift of the interior behind the pane), `mv2_flicker` (mains hum, brown-out sag, rare stutter on shop signs, neon blade signs and poster boxes).
- Not done: no real baked vertex AO (the facade uses the pilaster / ledge masks, props the normal curvature, which is zero on hard-edged boxes); manhole and puddle shaders already existed and were only biased; shop-front glass at street level keeps its own reflection.

## City variety (wave 62: district dressing; `scripts/world/city_kit_dress.gd`, `tools/city_kit_extra.py`, test `tests/test_city_variety.gd`)
- **What:** eleven more city-kit kinds (local kinds 12-22 of `city_kit_world.gd`, kit kinds `Kit.DRESS_FIRST..`): Stoop, Awning, Balcony, Shutter, Colonnade, Parapet, Rooftop, FireEscape, Cluster (sidewalk furniture), BladeSign, Ledge. They ride in the same merged street-kit window mesh: no node, no draw call, no texture (ten flat-colour glb pieces from the Quaternius Downtown City MegaKit, converted without Blender by `tools/city_kit_extra.py`, plus procedural boxes and cylinders in the district paint with glow accents in the palette's accent colours, emission code 1.0).
- **Composition rules (`Dress.layout`, one random stream per lot and face, so no earlier placement moved):** market = striped awnings, blade signs, cafe clusters, balconies with planters and lanterns, lantern pavilions or roof signs under slate caps, trim ledges; foundry = roll shutters, columns, fire escapes, brick parapets, twin smokestacks, tanks and pipe gantries, crate and barrel clusters, no balconies; slums = stoops, torn low awnings, tall balcony stacks with laundry, fire escapes, water towers, shacks with dishes, bins and junk; towers = colonnades with a glowing architrave, glass canopies, glass balconies with a glowing rail, glowing ledges, crowns with a halo ring and mast, helipads, planter and bench clusters with charge posts.
- **LOD:** the dressing draws within `DRESS_NEAR` 44 m (scaled by the tier; old city-kit pieces keep 38 m); rooftops also have a simplified far level (boxes, to 160 m). Clusters have a pooled collider (`KitDrCluster`), nothing else does.
- **Budget:** about +4 k near triangles on MED at the spawn (window 7.6 k -> 12.8 k, 8 % of nothing the perf gate counts: draws and texture memory unchanged). `test_art_library` window budgets were raised for it (MED 13500, LOW 9000, HIGH 17000; were 9000 / 5000 / 14000), `test_city_kit` near-spawn city-kit limit (9000) unchanged.
- **Visual QA:** `tools/visual_qa.sh` has eight new spots: `district_<id>` (sidewalk view of a dressed storefront) and `roofs_<id>` (rooftop structure from over the street). Follow-ups: upper-floor window styles are still the shader's; stoops and balconies have no door meshes; awning cloth has no wind sway; roofs read as dark silhouettes (little emission).

## Skill tree (`scripts/progress/skill_tree.gd`, `scripts/ui/skill_screen.gd`, test `tests/test_skill_tree.gd`)
Static `SkillTree` (like Upgrades / Gear): 4 verbs x 5 nodes, XP from every Drone kill (`kill_xp(score)`) and from `Progress.add_credits` payouts (not the score conversion), 1 point per level to LEVEL_CAP 40, node prerequisites (2 needs 0, 3 needs 1, capstone COHERENCE COST needs 2 and 3), `XP_MULT` / `POWER` tables per difficulty row (like HeroPower). Hooks: Quantum (range, chained hops, `_skill_blink_damage`, decoy life/echoes/`decoy_pick`, `_on_player_died` blast, link cap/life, swap cooldown, COLLAPSE radius/pull/chain/damage, `cost_mult`), Drone.damage (share). Screen: shop SKILLS button -> `sk_<node>` / `sk_respec` / `sk_back` (64 px). Save: `[skills]` (xp, `r_<node>`), reset on new campaign, maxed by DEMO. TEST/DEMO menus: +XP, MAX ALL SKILLS.

## New quantum verbs (PHASE SHIELD, TIME SLIP, ARC)
`QuantumVerbs` (`scripts/quantum/quantum_verbs.gd`, `player.qverbs`, actions `shield` Y / `slip` U / `arc` I, touch: MORE toggle opens a ring of SHIELD / SLIP / ARC at dx 640, `TouchControls.ring_open`, `StoryView.TOUCH_BUTTONS_W` 690). Energy via `HeroPower.cost`, cooldown via `HeroPower.cd`. SHIELD: barrier 4 s, `shield_hit` is static; `EnemyManager._tick_bolts` and `Operative._shoot` reflect or absorb (snipers pierce, heavy bolts drain it twice). SLIP: static `slip_scale` scales `Drone._physics_process` delta and bolt speed in a 10 m bubble (flanker/stalker resist, bosses most). ARC: auto-targets up to `arc_max()` enemies (nearest chain), teleport-slashes, returns; shield troops, brutes and bosses take 35 % damage. TEST and DEMO menus: QUANTUM VERBS. Test: `tests/test_new_verbs.gd`. Follow-ups: no sounds of its own (reuses `blink`), no desktop HUD chips, perf gate not run (verbs only draw while active: 1 quad, 1 sphere + torus, up to 7 thin beams).

## Lighting and post FX 2 (PostFx2)
`scripts/fx/post_fx2.gd` holds the tables: district grade (post_film.gdshader uniforms `d_gain/d_lift/d_sat`, set by `Atmosphere._look_step` from the hero position), tone white point + bloom threshold by night amount and SSAO (Forward+ MED/HIGH) via `DayNight.apply`, sun shadow table per tier via `CityEnv.apply_quality`, fake light shafts (`shaft_*`, 4/8 reads), ability kick (`Atmosphere.ability_pulse`, called through `PostFx2.pulse`) and lens drips (`drip_*`, HIGH). `PostFx2.enabled` is the TEST/DEMO switch.

## Humanoid wiring (HumanoidAnim in the live game)
- Vael troopers (type `operative` with the `humanoid` cfg, `Operative.hanim`) drive the shared rig through `HumanoidAnim`: `_sync_hanim` maps the AI onto IDLE / AIM / WINDUP (charge follows the aim time), `_shoot` calls `fire()`, `_hit_effect` calls `hit(dir, strength)`, `_death_effect` calls `die(kind)`; `write(rig)` replaces the old ad-hoc ready / recoil / flinch / dead inputs. The fall timing (`death_time` = 1.2 x FALL_TIME) is synced with the body tumble in `_tick_dead`. The Muzzle node follows `hanim.muzzle_transform(rig)`: the rifle mount is the carbine's own muzzle, other mounts (`cfg.weapon`, MOUNTS) shift it along the barrel. MOUNTS grips are informational: the hands are solved on the carbine's grips by the rig. Specialists, bosses and the old models have `hanim == null` and are untouched.
- Citizens (`CitizenActors`) call `HumanoidAnim.relax_arms(rig)` before `rig.apply()`: both hands hang at the sides (rig `hand_w` / `hand_off`) and swing against the legs, no rifle pose. `CharacterRig.arm_reach(k)` is the arm length.
- Hero rifle: it stays baked into the hero mesh (one surface skinned to the Rifle bone, so it already follows the rig's rifle mount with the hands on its grips). A separate rifle node on a hand mount would add a draw call (and a shadow caster), so it was NOT done; revisit only with a draw budget to spare.
- Test: `tests/test_humanoid_wiring.gd` (real scene). Look-dev: `tests/humanoid_closeup.tscn` (QT_VIEW=troopers|citizens, QT_X, QT_DIST, QT_ANGLE).

- **Stealth missions (`StealthScripts`, `StealthRunner`, `Game.missions.stealth`, wave 69):** four jobs, each in its own mission-free interior (teleporter hall, warehouse, server room, cargo bay), judged by the existing alert system: any living guard with `aware` (or `Stealth.alerts` rising) = SPOTTED. API `can_start / start(id) / abort / tick(dt) / hud_line / meter / markers / result`; the entry of a matching door offers the job (poll of `InteriorGameplay.enter_count`), `DemoMode.start_stealth` starts one at once. Pay via `StealthScripts.reward` from `Economy.STEALTH_PAY`.

## Controller and haptics
`scripts/ui/controller_input.gd` (`ControllerInput`, `Hud.pad`): polled joypad -> InputMap actions (`ControlMap.pad_binds`, slots "0:a" / "1:a" = LB layer), look stick via `CameraRig.add_look`, menu focus over `Hud.buttons` (`focus`, `Hud` draws the ring when `pad.focus_visible()`), touch / pad auto-switch (`pad_ui`, `TouchControls.set_pad_mode`). `scripts/ui/control_map.gd` (layout, remap, sanitise), `scripts/ui/controls_screen.gd` (options page 2). Haptics: `UiSettings.haptic_plan / buzz`, settings `haptic_level`, `key_binds`, `pad_binds`. Test: `tests/test_controller.gd` (simulated device, real scene, stepped by hand).

## Cover AI v2 and squad tactics v2 (tests/test_ai_tactics2.gd, real scene)
- `TacticsTable` (scripts/enemies/tactics_table.gd) is the ONE tuning table: faction rows (observer = disciplined corps, vael = hive rush, raider = hit and run; an Operative picks one on activation, `faction_for(type, chapter, roll)`), cover score weights and geometry (`cover_quality`, `score`), tier caps (LEAPERS, FLUSH_ACTIVE; thinkers stay `EnemyManager.THINKERS`), flush grenade, medic and commander numbers.
- `EnemyCover.find(who, threat, radius, faction, advance)` scores candidates (walk, height, arc, closer-by for an advance); `override_bodies` is a tests-only replacement for the pooled colliders.
- `Operative`: leapfrog (`_try_leap`, a hidden anchor runs to a cover `leap_step` closer while teammates suppress; `EnemyManager.leaping_near` caps leapers per tier), suppress-and-flank (covered troopers suppress faster while a flanker arcs or a teammate leaps), flush (`EnemyManager.try_flush`: hero held cover `flush_hold` s, squad cooldown, one blast through `Game.explode`), retreat/regroup (`retreat_hp`, falls back to the nearest mender, returns on health or allies), `_unstick` watchdog (a walker blocked by a wall for 1 s side-steps for 2.2 s). Mender heals the most wounded ally first (`patient_for`); commanders (cfg `commander`: warden, hivemother, overseer) buff allies within 14 m (`Drone.buff_dmg/buff_speed`, refreshed every 0.4 s). No new nodes or draws.
- Not run: a phone, real-device feel; there is still no path finding (the side-step only slides along walls).

## Weapon crafting (scripts/progress/crafting.gd, scripts/ui/craft_screen.gd)
Static `Crafting` holds parts, known blueprints, crafted items and the per-weapon equipped mod / variant; `Attachments.effective()` applies them after the attachment parts, perks write def keys (`chain`, `pierce_shield`, `sing`) that PlayerWeapon already reads. Drops go through `Crafting.award(source)`; the workbench (`_craft_open` in Hud, ids `cr_*`) opens from the safehouse screen, the shop and the DEMO menu. Saved in `[crafting]`.

## LOW+ and per-system CPU timers as implemented (`scripts/common/{low_plus,perf_timers}.gd`, `Game.low_plus`; tests `test_low_plus`, `test_tier_switch_memory`; tools `tools/cpu_frame.sh`, `tools/perf_report.sh lp`)
- **Tiers the player sees:** `Game.tier_index()` 0 LOW+, 1 LOW, 2 MED, 3 HIGH (`Game.TIER_LABELS`, `tier_label()`); the engine keeps `Game.quality` 0..2 and LOW+ is quality 0 with `Game.low_plus`. Change it only through `Game.set_tier_index(i)` / `set_low_plus(on)` / `set_quality(level)` (any level above 0 clears the flag); `quality_changed(0)` is re-emitted so every listener re-reads, and `low_plus_changed(on)` exists. Saved as `UiSettings.low_plus` (only with quality 0); `--low-plus` for tools.
- **Consumers ask `LowPlus.on()`** (`LowPlus.scaled(n, share)` for counts): `RenderRes.scale` (0.55, bilinear), `CityEnv.apply_quality` (glow, adjustment, height fog, aerial perspective off), `City._apply_low_plus` (lean building shader `Buildings.LEAN_SHADER`, `qs_amount` 0), `MaterialsV2.set_tier` (amount 0; ground and prop shaders skip the v2 block when `mv2_amount` is 0), ambient crowd / traffic / fauna, `EnemyManager.cap`, `VehicleSpecs.cap_for`, `ExplosionPool.tier_scale` and `slot_limit`, rain, `PerfTiers` (mesh LOD x1.6, model lights off, `LowPlus.apply_physics`), Missions (pickup glow lights off on LOW and LOW+), HUD / touch / minimap redraw intervals (`LowPlus.hud_interval`, `minimap_interval`; `LowPlus.throttle = false` restores every-frame redraws for A/B measurements).
- **Governor:** `AutoQuality.tick` steps LOW -> LOW+ after `LowPlus.GOVERNOR_FOR` (5 s) under 30 FPS, nothing below LOW+; `wants_low_plus_default` / `LowPlus.device_default(adapter)` start a weak-GPU touchscreen on LOW+.
- **Timers:** `PerfTimers.start()` / `stop(id, t0)` around a system's tick (the wrapper pattern `func _process(d): var t0 := PerfTimers.start(); _process_body(d); PerfTimers.stop(ID, t0)`), rolled per frame by `PerfTimers.install(tree)` (PerfOverlay), `table()`, `top_line()`, `calls(id)`, custom monitors `quantum/<key>_ms`. Never put a timer inside a hot loop; one pair costs two clock reads and one array add.
- **Display queries:** `UiUtil.insets / insets_v` read `DisplayServer.window_get_size()` and `get_display_safe_area()` at most every 20 frames (`UiUtil.display_cache`): both were called per button per frame (a JNI call on Android, an X11 round trip under xvfb: 4-9 ms per frame of the touch layer there).

## HUD declutter (reboot wave, tests/test_hud_declutter.gd)
- Default phone frame (`Hud.visible_controls()`, at most 12): stick, FIRE, AIM, JUMP, RELOAD, ABILITY, WEAPON (silhouette + ammo count), MORE, pause, minimap, status bars, ONE objective line. ABILITY opens `RadialWheel` (scripts/ui/radial_wheel.gd; tap = stays open, hold + drag + release = pick; seven plain verb names); WEAPON tap cycles, hold opens the weapon wheel. The wheel never pauses. MORE (`TouchControls.more_open`) holds ALT, MELEE, THROW, CROUCH, GADGET, CALL CAR, ally orders. USE (reads ENTER/EXIT), GRAB (enemy/car within 9 m, prop within 3.5 m), TAKEDOWN, SKIP, RADIO, GARAGE are contextual.
- Removed from the default frame: FPS box (long press under the minimap or Options > FPS DISPLAY), TEST quick buttons (`TestMode.quick_weapons` off), shard counter, credits plate, faction plate (heat stars only while wanted), ammo/weapon plates on phones, quantum readout and first-use hints (FULL chatter only).
- Messages: `Toast` (scripts/ui/toast.gd) is the one channel: one line, max 2 s, rate limited (`GAP`), chatter never shown, important ones replace low ones. Story chatter option OFF/LOW/FULL (`UiSettings.chatter`, default LOW): LOW drops prio-0 radio lines, shows the first sentence (`Story.MAX_SHORT`) in at most two lines and a two-line chapter banner.
- Title/pause (menu declutter wave): the title shows PLAY / CONTINUE, OPTIONS and MORE (`menu_gear`, flag `Hud._gear_open`); the pause menu shows RESUME, MAP (`pause_map`, opens the real MapScreen, closing returns to the pause menu), LOADOUT, OPTIONS and MORE. The MORE page (`Hud.more_items()`, a 3 column grid of 64 px cells, `more_back`) holds everything else: title = NEW GAME, CHAPTERS, DEMO, ARENA; pause = HERO, MISSIONS, HEISTS, SUIT, SAFEHOUSE, ACHIEVEMENTS, PHOTO, ARENA, CHAPTERS, ABORT TO MENU. A screen opened from MORE returns to MORE (the flag stays set under it); the TEST button (TestMenu) is the last MORE cell (`Hud.more_test_rect()`), on no other screen. The HERO hub (`hero_open`, alias `shop`, flag `Hud._hero_hub`) puts UPGRADES / WEAPONS / SKILLS / CRAFT tabs (`hero_tab_*`, `ShopScreen.tabs`) over the shop, skill and workbench screens; BACK on any tab leaves the hub. Upgrade rows are whole-row buttons (`Hud._add_row`, one tap buys, lit when affordable, `NEED n` otherwise) grouped WEAPON / ABILITY / BODY. Quick settings moved to Options (3 columns; music/SFX volume, sound, post FX, cutscenes, story chatter, FPS display).
- Gaps: perf gate not re-run; es/hi strings for the new labels missing; test_campaign_runner title CHAPTERS checks need the parallel MORE-page layout.

## Combat feel (reboot wave; tests/test_combat_feel.gd, tests/test_civilian_kill.gd, tests/bot_combat_feel.gd, tests/fight_bot.gd)
- Encounters (`scripts/enemies/encounters.gd`, child of Main, `Encounters.current`; off under tools/tests unless a test enables it): a countdown (first squad ~8 s of play, later gaps shrink) then a squad of 2..MAX_WAVE_SIZE spawned 20-34 m from the hero through `EnemyManager`, all aware at once, operatives `rush` (short hides, rare cover, close to mid range), an alarm sting as the threat cue, backup once per wave when one is left. Only runs when `Game.is_playing()` on foot (pause/vehicle/interiors keep it out). Squad kills carry `heat_scale` of the heat (an unprovoked attack is not a crime).
- Weapons: `WeaponModels` (one silhouette mesh per class, one surface, one shared `weapon_look.gdshader` material whose `glow` kicks on a shot); the carbine stays the hero's own mesh. `FX.casings` (MultiMesh shell casings, one draw call, hidden when none), flying tracer (`fx_tracer.gdshader` `head_dist`, `FX.TRACER_MIN_SPEED`), bigger muzzle flash and camera kick, `hit_kill` confirm sound, stronger shot.wav set (`tools/audio/make_combat.py`; mix level -9.5 dBFS in test_assets), own `shot_scatter`.
- Civilians (`ambient_crowd.gd` `kill_person` / dying pose and quantum dissolve, `npc_life.gd` `on_civilian_down` / `on_civilian_hurt` / `_runover`): hit by rays (head x2), blasts (`Game.explode` -> `ambient.blast_civilians`), melee and fast cars. Consequences: yelp + dissolve sound, the street scatters, witnesses cower and may call the Watch, heat (`KILL_HEAT`), 4-14 credits and sometimes a part into the wallet (no toast). Option CIVILIANS (`UiSettings.civilians` -> `Game.civilian_casualties`, default on, no gore): off = shots pass through and they only run.
- Measured (sandbox, fight bot, 40 s): first shot 0.9 s, 24 kills/min, an aware enemy within 45 m 33 of 34 s. Perf gate OK (LOW 79 total draws / 125k / 166 MB, MED 120 / 248k / 193 MB, HIGH 133 / 353k / 314 MB).
- Gaps: nothing measured on a real phone; weapon silhouettes are hard to read from behind at phone size; RELOADING label clips ("RELOADIN") on the desktop-layout ammo plate; enemies at 35-40 m are a few pixels tall; no es/hi strings beyond "CIVILIANS"; test_player_robustness "victory legs at rest" fails with the concurrent locomotion edits in the tree (not this wave).

## Open world freedom (reboot wave; scripts/freedom/, tests/test_open_world_freedom.gd, tests/bot_open_world.gd)
- `Freedom` (node of Main, `Freedom.current`) owns `Hijack`, `Charges` and `Jobs` and the one contextual line: `Freedom.job_prompt()` (a job marker under the hero, no key, it starts by itself) and `Freedom.vehicle_prompt()` (`HIJACK TAXI` for a car with a driver, `ENTER BUS` otherwise) are read by `Hud.hint_text()`; `Freedom.markers()` feeds the minimap (a diamond per marker within 260 m, the live goal clamped to the rim). The HUD frame is unchanged: the only new buttons are C4 (MORE cluster) and DETONATE (appears while a charge is down).
- Doors: every lot of the big city has exactly one door (`CityDoors.CHANCE = 1.0`, 830 of 830; towers get a lobby), one `DoorMarkers` MultiMesh glows on all of them and `InteriorGameplay.prompt` shows ENTER within 2.8 m. The test checks all lots (door on its own facade, front never inside a building, ids unique), prompts at five doors including the farthest outer one and really opens that room.
- Vehicles: `VehicleManager.reach_car(pos)` = nearest car within the old 4.2 m or whose hull edge (`Vehicle.edge_distance`) is within `VehicleSpecs.ENTER_REACH` (1.9 m, plus 0.12 m per m/s of the car's speed, max 2.6): a bus is taken at its flank, a moving car on the run. Used by USE, the touch USE button and the prompt. `enter()` calls `Hijack.on_enter(car, from, had_driver)`: a pooled entry ghost slides to the door, a driver of a traffic or patrol car is thrown out on the kerb side (2 pooled capsule bodies, fly, lie 0.9 s, get up and run, recycled), shake + sparks + `HIJACKED <KIND>`, heat as before. Bailing out above 4 m/s keeps half the car's speed. Every kind is covered by the test (17 kinds: cars, bikes, bus, interceptor, ambulance, hover, skiff, patrol, hover-bike).
- HOVER-BIKE (`&"hoverbike"` in `VehicleSpecs`, cls hover, bike model with the wheels hidden, 30 m/s, one seat, also 2 % of the traffic mix): CALL CAR (`VehicleManager.summon_any()`) brings the garage car, or with an empty garage a free hover-bike (8 s cooldown). QUANTUM GLIDE (`HeroVerbs`): a second jump press in the air throws the hero forward for 1.15 s (27 m/s fading, lift, cyan trail, costs 14 energy, once per jump); holding jump still glides.
- Jobs (`Jobs`, data in `FreedomData`): 24 markers (STREET RACE magenta, TAXI FARE amber, DELIVERY green, BOUNTY red; 6 each, 11 in the core, 13 outside) on junction corners plus 6 STUNT JUMP ramps (cyan, landing ring 32 m beyond the ramp). Slow down (< 10 m/s) on a marker for 0.5 s and it starts (race and fare need a vehicle). Race = 4 gates around two blocks against par time; fare / delivery = a junction 4-6 blocks away against a clock (fare ends nearly stopped); bounty = one operative with 3x health 55-100 m from the marker. Rewards are credits through `Progress.add_credits` (numbers in `FreedomData`; race best times and found ramps in `user://freedom.cfg`). A ramp lifts the vehicle model along its slope and a car above 14 m/s flies a scripted arc (the car physics stays 2D, flight time = 32 m / speed, apex 5 m); the hero on foot is launched too (a QUANTUM GLIDE carries him to the ring). Draws: 3 (one MultiMesh of beams and rings, one of ramps, one pooled goal beam).
- Explosives: `Charges` (QUANTUM CHARGES, C4: `charge_place` P / MORE button C4, `detonate` L / DETONATE button) sticks up to 3 charges where the crosshair points within 20 m (it rides on a car or enemy), refills one per 18 s, detonation 0.14 s apart, blast 6.5 m (enemies 170, cars 150, `Game.explode`, so civilians, props and cover take it too). `Game.exploded(pos, radius)` is new: a charge reached by any blast goes off 0.18 s later (charges, mines, launcher, cars chain). Exploding cars set every car within 7 m alight (`Vehicle.ignite`, fuse up to 1.4 s, staggered by distance). FLUX MINE stays a throwable. The GRAVITY LAUNCHER (`WeaponDefs` `impact: true`) is the grenade launcher: the round blows on impact after a 4 m pull (130 damage, cars 110). `Breakables` has a new kind `barrier` (concrete road barrier, 14 hp, any blast takes it down) as destructible cover.
- Gaps: nothing measured on a real phone; no controller binding for C4 / DETONATE (keys P / L and touch only); ramps and charges have no physics bodies (the hero walks through a ramp, charges do not block); no AI rivals in street races; the hijack driver is a capsule, not the citizen model; no es / hi strings for the new labels; the stunt flight is scripted, a car cannot be steered in the air; bounty targets do not flee.

## Big city (wave 84; world scale; scripts/world/city_layout.gd, city_chunks.gd, city_stream.gd, city_districts.gd; tests/test_big_city.gd, real scene)
- Map: 16 x 16 blocks of 80 m = 1280 m (`CityLayout.N` / `Game.N` 16, `HALF` 640, walls at `WALK` = HALF + 30), 4x the area. The original 8 x 8 city is the CORE in the middle (`CityLayout.CORE0` 4, `CORE_N` 8, `CORE_HALF` 320, `is_core_block`): its 232 lots are generated FIRST with the original stream (lots 0..231, same positions, heights, tints), so every core table (spawn, mission junctions, shards, safehouses, activities, doors, landmarks) is unchanged. The 192 outer blocks come from a second stream (`OUTER_SEED`, 24 % of the outer lots left open, buildings 16 / 28 / 44 m): 830 lots in all, every lot has a door. `City.intersections` stays the 49 CORE junctions (missions, cores, shards); `City.all_intersections` has all 225. `random_core_road_point()` / `random_road_point()` (whole map). Road grid indices are absolute (`road_center(i)`, core road `k` is index `k + CORE0`; `Activities.core_road(k)`).
- Streaming (nothing is built or freed at run time except colliders, but nothing grows with the map): every static family is a camera window over its MultiMesh (`CityStream`, cell grid `CITY_ORIGIN` / `CITY_CELL` over the playable square): the old prop and skyline families plus now the BUILDINGS (`RADIUS_BUILDINGS` 560 m x tier scale 0.65 / 0.85 / 1), the GLOW sprites (320 m) and the STEAM puffs (140 m; `set_keep` filters the puffs per vent by tier on the rank in custom data r); the lamp light cones are ONE mesh re-built from the lamps within `CONE_WINDOW` 200 m every `CONE_STEP` 30 m. `glow.gdshader` and `steam.gdshader` shrink instances between `lod_range` like the prop shader. Outer building colliders are CHUNKS (`CityChunks`, `City.collision_chunks()`): one pooled StaticBody3D per block within 300 m of the player (released beyond 380 m, 4 blocks per call, `refill` at teleports), shapes are pooled and disabled when free; core buildings keep their permanent shapes in `collision_body`. Crossing the map costs about 30 blocks alive, 150 box shapes and 235 shape nodes ever created (an unstreamed map would keep ~1300). Shaders: `W_HALF` 640, the lot map is 32 x 32, light map 1408 m at 2.5 m per texel (560 px RGBAH, 2.5 MB), the edge hazard band follows `W_HALF`; far traffic wraps over 1320 m lanes (68 lanes, 700 cars on HIGH), crowd / fauna / events / vehicles stream around the player as before. All whole-map MultiMesh and effect AABBs are +-900 m.
- Zones (`CityDistricts`): `at()` still gives the four quadrants (music, dialogue, campaign, factions, now over the whole map); `zone_at()` gives 8 zones: the core quadrants and four outer sides of the ring by BLOCK centre (DOCKYARDS north = foundry palette, AMBER TERRACES east = market, GROW FIELDS south = slums, OUTER SPIRES west = towers); `look_at()` is the palette district (0..3) every builder dresses with (facade tint, neon, kit mix, lamps) and the ground shader's `dist_idx` does the same (sidewalk slabs, seams). No new texture or palette.
- Vehicles: ARCADE top speeds 18-28 m/s (sedan 22, sport 28, suv 19, bikes 27-28, hover 25, patrol 26, truck / van / bus 18-19) with the acceleration ratio kept (time constant about 2 s), nitro x1.3: the sport car needs 46 s for the 1200 m avenue. Minimap: texture over the whole map (640 px), range 130 m on foot growing to 280 m with the driven vehicle's speed (`Minimap.range_for_speed`). Map screen: 32 x 32 cells of 40 m, ZOOM button 1x / 2x / 4x around you (`set_zoom`, `cycle_zoom`, drag to pan when zoomed), 8 zone names, the core outlined; old 16 x 16 `map.cfg` saves migrate into the core cells.
- Enemies: `PATROL_GROUPS` 7 in the core (awake as before) + `PATROL_OUTER_GROUPS` 14 dormant outer groups that wake within 150 m; an unaware patrol more than `PATROL_SLEEP_DIST` 260 m from the player is pooled again and its group waits (no far enemy keeps a slot of the cap). Interior rooms moved with the border strip (`InteriorGameplay.ORIGIN` x = HALF + 7.5).
- Budgets (perf gate, MED / HIGH, same seed): visible draws +4, triangles flat to -10 % at the spawn (test_world MED 142k vs 160k), texture memory LOW +4 / MED +14 / HIGH +15 MB (light map and minimap are 4 MB of it; the rest was not attributed, other builders' changes were in the tree), build time of the city 0.55 s -> 1.15 s headless. Limits unchanged and not exceeded.


## Shooting accuracy (fundamentals wave; tests/test_shooting_accuracy.gd, tests/bot_shooting.gd)
Owner round 3: "shooting is not on target; bursts". The fire path was audited end to end and measured (matrix of 4 distances x hip / aimed x chest / head, moving targets, every hitscan weapon, grenades, bursts, sustained fire, the crosshair). What was wrong and what is true now:
- **Truth is the camera ray through the crosshair.** `PlayerWeapon._aim_ray` starts at the hero's depth along the camera ray; the tracer is drawn muzzle -> hit. The old rule also blocked the shot whenever the muzzle-to-target segment touched ANY world collider (a lamp post or a corner beside the line ate shots the player saw land). Now only cover within `MUZZLE_CLEAR` (1.2 m) of the muzzle, or the barrel poking into a wall (pivot -> muzzle), blocks. Zero-spread hitscan: 100 % on target, 0.000 m lateral error at 5 / 15 / 30 / 60 m.
- **Hit shapes (`scripts/combat/hit_zones.gd`).** Enemies were tested on a chest sphere (radius 0.55: missed the feet, 20 cm too fat beside the body) AND a physics capsule. Now every enemy answers `hit_ray(origin, dir)`: a person is a head sphere on a body capsule from the feet (zones `head`, `body`, `legs`: head = the weapon's crit damage, legs x0.85), a drone keeps its sphere. The physics enemy colliders are skipped by the shot ray (kept for everything else); the nearest shape of all enemies wins. `Drone.body_box()` gives the feet-to-head box for the HUD.
- **Recoil.** Sustained fire climbed the view 7.6 degrees in 24 rounds (SMG rounds 2 m over a head at 15 m) and recovered only after the trigger was released. `CameraRig.kick` now saturates at `RECOIL_SOFT_CAP` (0.06 rad) and the view recovers at 40 % rate while shots keep coming: the climb settles near 1.5 degrees. Burst weapons (pistol burst, volleys): rounds that are not the last of their burst kick the camera only `MID_BURST_KICK` (25 %) and do not bloom the cone between them (one `BURST_BLOOM` step at the end): a 3-round burst lands inside 0.2 m at 15 m.
- **Crosshair cone.** `Player.spread_in_pixels(h, w)` used the viewport HEIGHT with a HORIZONTAL FOV (KEEP_WIDTH): the cone was drawn 2.2 times too small on a phone. It now uses the width. `PlayerWeapon.cone()` is the half angle a shot is rolled in (tests pin it with `spread_override`); `last_shot` records origin, dir, muzzle, hit, target, zone of the last pellet.
- **Feedback.** `AimAssist.on_target` is now exact (a shot fired now would hit that enemy's real shape with nothing in the way; `aim_zone`), the crosshair turns red (gold on the head), `Hud._draw_target_brackets` frames the enemy feet to head and names HEAD under the feet, the hit marker is white (hit), gold (head), red + ring (kill). Smaller health bars (`enemy_marks.gd`) and brass casings (`casings.gd`) that read as floating blocks.
- Weapons: flux SMG (cone 0.018 + bloom to 0.03) and plasma minigun (0.024 + 0.034) tightened; the others unchanged.
- Looking: `BOT_TOUCH=1 BOT_LANE=n BOT_RES=1200x540 QUALITY=0 tools/bot.sh res://tests/bot_shooting.gd /tmp/out/s 2 40` renders the whole fire path from the phone camera; `tools/contact_sheet.py` builds the sheet. Before / after: docs/shots/slice/shooting_before_after.png.
- Gaps: nothing measured on a real phone; the AIM button is still a second finger (no toggle); touch auto-fire stays an option (off); the weapon pop-up card lingers over the hero on slow frames; bots test hitscan on the camera ray, not the finger-drag path.

## Weapons: display, count, progression (owner round 3; tests/test_weapons_available_display.gd)
- **Roster**: 18 weapons (`WeaponDefs.IDS`, keys 1-8 = the first eight). Seven STARTERS are owned from the first minute and in every save (`WeaponDefs.STARTERS`: VOLT PISTOL, FLUX SMG, RIFT CARBINE, SHARD SCATTER, RAIL RIFLE, GRAVITY LAUNCHER, QUANTUM BLADE: pistol, SMG, rifle, shotgun, sniper, launcher, melee). New: pistol (unlimited sidearm), PHASE MARKSMAN, SHARD BREACHER (auto shotgun), NOVA LAUNCHER (rocket: impact launcher, `grav` key in the def). Every weapon belongs to a class (`WeaponDefs.CLASS_OF`, `CLASS_ORDER`); SWAP / mouse wheel cycle in class order (`owned_ordered()`). Adding a weapon touches: `WeaponDefs` (DEFS, ALTS, HIT_SND, TILT, IDS, CLASS_OF), `WeaponModels` (mesh + SPEC grips), `Economy.WEAPON_PRICE`, `PlayerWeapon.AMMO_PACK`, `HeroAnimTuning.WEAPON_CLASS`, `QuantumVfx.WEAPON_CLASS`, `BalanceModel.ACC`, `AimAssist.STAGGER`, the shop list (`ShopScreen.SHOP_WEAPONS`, non-starters only).
- **Display**: the hero is built UNARMED (`HeroCharacter.build` `armed: false`, `PlayerBody._make_model`); every weapon, the carbine included (its mesh is the hero kit's Blender rifle), is ONE mesh from `WeaponModels.mesh(id)` on the muzzle node (the rifle bone), on the hero's light layer (his key / rim lights shape it). Mesh frame: origin = muzzle, barrel -Z, body toward +Z. `WeaponModels.SPEC[id]` = `reach` (how far the muzzle sits in front of the rifle pivot: a pistol pulls the muzzle back, `muzzle_shift`) and the right / left wrist grips; `grip_offsets` feeds `CharacterRig.grip_off_l/r`, so the arms are solved on THAT weapon's grips (pistol two-handed, akimbo one pistol per hand, blade on its hilt). `PlayerBody.sync_muzzle()` places the node after every rig pose (look-dev viewers call it too). Shader `weapon_look.gdshader` (vertex colour rgb, alpha = glow, rim, a small self-lit floor). Look-dev: `tools/weapon_view.sh OUT` (the hero viewer holding every weapon, contact sheet), `tests/bot_weapon_hold.gd` (live game: real camera hip / aim and a 3/4 street view), contact sheets in docs/shots/slice/.
- **HUD**: `WeaponIcons.texture(id)` rasterises the weapon's own mesh side view into a cached 96x48 texture (HUD plate, WEAPON button, wheel, loadout all show the shape the hero holds). `Hud._draw_weapon_pop` pops a plate (icon, name, class, rounds, reserve, NEXT weapon + how to get it) for 2.2 s whenever the weapon changes. Weapon wheel (`WeaponWheel`, scripts/ui/weapon_wheel.gd): seven CLASS wedges, each showing the last weapon used in the class and "n/total" owned; pick a class = that weapon, pick your own class = the next weapon of it (`WeaponDefs.last_in_class`). Pause LOADOUT shows the NEXT line.
- **Ladder** (`WeaponLadder`, scripts/progress/weapon_ladder.gd): a visible NEXT weapon always (`next_target()`, `next_line()`), each non-starter has one free route (crate / mission chapter / boss / enemy drop / shop) and is always buyable in the shop. Drops (`Drone._drop` -> `WeaponLadder.enemy_drop`): 55 % of kills drop something useful: ammo for the gun in hand (always when dry), the NEXT weapon from its enemy type (40 %), or ammo for another owned weapon. Chapters 1 and 2 grant a weapon (`Missions` connects `CampaignRunner.chapter_completed`). Boss drops unchanged (Contracts).
- **Crates** (`WeaponCrates`, scripts/freedom/weapon_crates.gd, child of Freedom, data `WeaponLadder.CRATES`): 12 sidewalk-corner crates (3 carry weapons: marksman near the spawn, breacher, rocket; 9 ammo), walk up = weapon (first time, equipped at once) + `PlayerWeapon.restock`, closed for 300 s, minimap markers via `Freedom.markers()`. Two draws (MultiMesh of crates with `crate_look.gdshader`, MultiMesh of thin beams). An owned weapon's crate is just an ammo crate (no save state needed).
- **Switching fixes**: a dry weapon with no reserve auto-swaps to the best owned weapon that shoots (`PlayerWeapon._auto_swap`, toast OUT OF AMMO); switching cancels a reload without losing or inventing ammo; per-weapon ammo survives round trips (covered by the test).

## Vehicle variety and police pursuit (round 3; tests/test_traffic_police_feel.gd)
- `VehicleSilhouettes` (scripts/vehicles/vehicle_silhouettes.gd): side-profile prisms with Body / Glass / Trim surfaces, used by `VehicleVisual._fallback_shaped` for specs with `"shape": true` and no art model: compact (new kind), bus, interceptor (light bar, push bar, door stripe), ambulance (box, stripe, beacons), scooter. 14 drivable kinds now read by silhouette.
- Police ladder (`VehicleManager._tick_cops`, constants `COP_*` in VehicleSpecs): heat 0.5 an interceptor tails the hero (siren, no contact), 2.0 rams (`Vehicle.cop`, patrol-style chase, 0.7 x ram damage), 3.0 boxin/block tactics plus `SpikeStrips` laid 60 m ahead of a driving hero every 12 s, 4.0 three cars (tier caps 2/3/4). `Vehicle.blow_tires()` (flat time 9 s: top speed x0.55, grip x0.55); cops are immune. Stand-down below heat 0.3.
- Traffic: cars in flight after gunfire (`ai_panic` > 1) no longer brake for cars ahead and collide for real (AI-AI pairs are tested only then); hijack also throws out one rider (`Vehicle.passengers`, Hijack pool 3).

## Vehicles that feel like cars (owner round 3; tests/test_vehicle_realism.gd in the real scene, tests/bot_vehicle_realism.gd + tools/vehicle_bot.sh, tests/vehicle_viewer.tscn + tools/vehicle_view.sh)
Owner: "car view not altered, looks like a toy car, gets damaged in one crash, nowhere close to GTA". What changed (cars only; bikes and hover craft keep the arcade model, `Vehicle._drive_arcade`):
- **Handling (`Vehicle._drive_car`).** Single-track (bicycle) model at 120 Hz sub-steps: front and rear axle each with a slip-angle tyre curve (`Vehicle.tyre_curve`: peak at `PEAK_SLIP`, then the tyre slides and keeps `SLIDE_GRIP` of its force), weight transfer from the smoothed longitudinal acceleration, a friction circle (braking or driven wheels leave less cornering grip, so power oversteers and trail braking works), real mass, yaw inertia and wheel base from the spec (`VehicleSpecs.mass_of / wheelbase / mu_of`), wet streets (`Atmosphere.wetness_now`) lose `WET_GRIP_LOSS`. Steering is speed sensitive (`Vehicle.steer_limit`: 0.6 rad standing, 0.12 at 30 m/s), the wheel follows the stick with a slew rate, a counter-steer assist follows the body slip angle (a slide is caught instead of spinning the car), the handbrake frees the rear for `DRIFT_RECOVER` s. Below `LOW_SPEED` the car turns kinematically (blended). A 5 speed box (`GEAR_TOPS`, `GEAR_ACC`, torque curve, `SHIFT_TIME` torque cut, `rpm` 0..1 that DROPS at each shift) gives the gear-like surge. Numbers (sport car): 0-60 km/h 3.3 s, top 35 m/s, 100-0 in 1.7 s / 24 m, 0.8 g in a half-stick corner; a sedan 0-100 in 7.7 s. Top speeds 19-35 m/s (bus 19 .. sport and interceptor 35): the 1.3 km map takes 40-70 s. Exposed for audio / HUD / camera: `gear`, `rpm`, `yaw_rate`, `steer_angle`, `long_acc`, `lat_acc`, `skid`, `wheelspin`, `last_impact`, `impact_t`, `crashes`. `tests/dev_vehicle_tune.gd` prints the numbers per kind.
- **Body motion.** `Vehicle._sync_body`: a spring-damper per axis fed by the accelerations: nose dive under braking (to 4 deg), squat under power, roll out of corners, heave kicked by impacts; wheels turn with the real wheel angle (`steer_angle`), badly damaged cars sag and sit a little off level (`VehicleVisual.deform`; the model is never scaled any more).
- **Collisions.** Walls: `Vehicle.impulse_hit` (rigid impulse at the contact point with the car's mass and yaw inertia, restitution `RESTITUTION`, scrape friction `WALL_FRICTION`): hits spin the car and a glancing hit slides on. Car against car: `VehicleManager._collide_all / collide_cars` (separating-axis test on the real hull boxes, impulse with both masses and both contact levers, pushes out by mass share; lane traffic is not tested against itself, a car that is hit loses its lane AI). Closing speed along the contact normal is what counts.
- **Damage (round 2 part C supersedes the numbers below).** One smooth curve `((v - CRASH_SAFE 5) / (CRASH_WRECK 28.5 - 5))^1.5` of `min(CRASH_REF_HP 300, max_hp)` (25 km/h 2 %, 40 km/h 13 %, 60 km/h 35 %, 90 km/h 78 %), stages by hull: dents + cracked glass with every hit, a thin wisp below 70 %, dark smoke below 40 %, fire below 15 % (`Vehicle.stage_for` / `damage_stage()`), the driver loses `HERO_HURT_MAX 30 x ((v - 20) / (28.5 - 20))^2` hp (cap 30 %). Earlier text: Hit points x2.5 (sedan 300, sport 250, SUV 375, truck 650, bus 800; patrol 400). `Vehicle.crash(v, at, n, other_mass)`: nothing below `CRASH_SAFE` (6.5 m/s along the normal), then `CRASH_REF_HP` x ((v - SAFE) / (WRECK - SAFE))^1.6 (36 km/h = 4 % of a sedan, 72 km/h = 50 %, a head-on wall at a sedan's top speed wrecks it), x a mass-share factor against other cars (the lighter car loses more), a truck loses a much smaller share than a sedan. The hero is hurt only above `HERO_HURT_SPEED` (16 m/s, `Player.hurt_driver`; `Player.damage` still routes bullets to the car at 60 %). External `damage()` (bullets, blasts, fire) keeps the callers' numbers against the bigger hit points. Smoke below 40 %, fire below 15 % (burns 3.4 hp/s: about 9-13 s to get out), explosion only when the hull is gone.
- **What damage looks like (`VehicleVisual`).** `add_dent(at, dir, frac)`: a smooth crater with a crumpled rim pushed into the surfaces that face the impact, normals tilted so the light shows it, up to 7 dents that merge; the body mesh is copied once per damaged car (never the shared model mesh), rebuilt on a new dent (about 5 ms), restored by `repair()`. `crack_glass(level, at)`: one transparent overlay mesh (windscreen, rear window, side windows sized from the spec; optional `"glass"` spec keys) with a shared 128 px crack texture, one draw only while cracked. Paint soots and dulls with the hull (no glow any more: the old cyan phase-seam glow was removed). `Vehicle.debug_damage(frac)` for look-dev.
- **Look.** The lamp surface shader (`VehicleVisual.LAMP_SHADER_CODE`) classifies road-level glow by model height: the headlight / taillight road pools and the big underglow disc are off (`pool`), halos are 30 % (`halo`) - the translucent patches round a car read as a toy. ONE shared directional key light for cars only (`VehicleVisual.ensure_key_light / aim_key`, layer 18, no shadows, follows the camera yaw with an offset) gives a lit and a shaded side; paint glow is 0.1 (MED/HIGH) / 0.26 (LOW, no fill light), rim 0.6.
- **Driving camera (`CameraRig`).** Round 2 part C: horizontal FOV 80 at rest to 92 at top speed, distance = length x 0.8 + 1.5 m (+ up to 1.9 m with speed), pivot at `0.2 + 0.32 x height`, pitch -0.22: the whole car fills about a third of a phone frame. (Earlier text: `_vehicle_params`: distance = car length x 1.2 + 1.9 m (+ up to 2.4 m with speed, + acceleration), height from the car, horizontal FOV 64 at rest to 82 at top speed (+7 while boosting), pitch -0.12; `_drift_offset`: the view turns up to 0.7 rad toward the direction of travel of a sliding car; look behind (`look_back` action, key N, BACK button, held, swings the view round and returns); `veh_view` (key B, VIEW button, `cycle_vehicle_view`): chase / far / hood (camera at the driver's eye ahead of the car's middle, FOV 82-90). The spring arm still pulls the camera in against walls.
- **Audio.** `Sfx._step_vehicle` follows the car's own `rpm` (the note drops at each shift, louder on the throttle), tyre squeal from `skid` (slide, wheelspin or lock-up), crash sounds from `impact_t` / `last_impact` (bump, crash + metal, + glass above a hard hit).
- **Mechanic bays (`RepairStations`, scripts/freedom/repair_stations.gd, child of Freedom).** 12 green rings on the parking strips of the avenues (the first 11 m from the spawn), drive in and stop for 1.6 s: `Vehicle.repair()` (full hull, dents out, glass replaced, no smoke or fire), 15 s rest, minimap markers via `Freedom.markers()`; the garage car arrives repaired (`summon_garage`). One MultiMesh, one draw call, no beam (a cone read as a glitch).
- **Inputs.** `VehicleManager.ensure_actions` registers `veh_view` (B), `look_back` (N), `horn` (H) when missing; touch: VIEW and BACK buttons while driving (`TouchControls.BUTTONS`, need `&"driving"`, 92 px at the 720 px base).
- Looking: `tools/vehicle_view.sh /tmp/v kinds=sedan,sport views=front3q,side,rear3q damage=0,0.5,0.9` (cars on a floor with a hero for scale), `BOT_SET=drive|crash|damage|cam BOT_OUT=/tmp/v/run QUALITY=1 tools/vehicle_bot.sh` (the phone camera in the real city, scripted drives and crashes, a beauty camera round the car); `tools/contact_sheet.py` builds the sheets: docs/shots/slice/vehicles_realism_*.png.
- Gaps: nothing measured on a real phone; the models are unchanged (no interior, the cockpit view is a hood view); body panels dent but there are no detaching parts; no skid marks on the road; the cars still have no contact shadow on LOW; first-person is the hood camera only; bikes and hover craft use the old arcade model (no dents, they keep the old damage formula); traffic AI cars still follow lanes kinematically (they are only physical once hit).

## Menus and map (owner round 3; tests/test_menus_map_quality.gd in the real scene, tests/bot_menu_shots.gd renders every screen)
- **One menu grammar.** Every pause / title sub screen gets its title from `Hud.header(c, vp, s, font, title, sub)` (one size, one baseline `HEAD_Y`, a magenta rule, an optional amber sub line such as the credits); the frame lines that cut across the rows are gone. Menu text is never below `Hud.BODY_MIN` base px (15 = 22 px at 1080p): `Hud._text` raises smaller sizes on the screen layer, and a button label that no longer fits wraps to two lines (`Hud._split_label`) instead of shrinking to 10 px. The plain pause screen hides the gameplay HUD (`panel_cover()` is true for the whole pause state) and shows what to do now (`NOW  <objective>`) and the credits above RESUME; MORE is a centred 3 x N grid of equal 256 px buttons.
- **Options** are columns with captions (CONTROLS, DISPLAY, SOUND AND MORE / LANGUAGE AND TEXT, PLAY, SAVE DATA; `OptionsScreen.COLS0/COLS1`, `ORDER`/`PAGE2` keep the flat id lists), seven rows each. **Loadout**: the owned weapons are a 4 x 2 tile grid on the left (the ids `wheel_<weapon>` and the page buttons are unchanged), the three attachment slots on the right. **Weapon shop**: three cells per row so names fit. Safehouse shows the credits in its header.
- **Layout scan.** `Hud.scan_text` / `scan_log` record every queued menu text as {text, rect, box, size, over}; the test asserts, for 21 screens at 844x390 and 1200x540 (the 2400x1080 phone at half size), that texts lie on screen, are not wider than their box, are >= BODY_MIN, overlap no other text and do not straddle a button edge, and that every button is on screen, >= 64 base px and overlaps no other.
- **Map (`MapScreen`).** North-up, the WHOLE city drawn from the layout (road grid with brighter avenues, every building footprint coloured by height, parks, district tint per block, district names), under a dark fog of the cells not yet walked near (`DISCOVER_R` 150 m, was 70: the grid and outline stay visible, buildings, markers and the zone name do not: UNCHARTED). Pinch with two fingers (`zoom_f` 1..8, zoom about the fingers), mouse wheel / trackpad magnify, drag to pan, `Zoom` cycles 1/2/4x, `Locate` centres on the hero. Player arrow with a view cone from the camera heading, N compass, scale bar. Left column = legend + filter + counts (`F_<kind>`: MISSION, SIDE, INTERIORS, JOBS, SHOPS, GARAGE, WEAPONS, HOME, ITEMS), right column = CLOSE / ZOOM / YOU / CLEAR. Markers are badges drawn by `MapIcons` (shape + colour); at the overview nearby markers of a kind merge into one badge with a count; labels never overlap (`_placed`). Tapping a marker routes to it, tapping the map places the waypoint, tapping the waypoint clears it.
- **GPS (`MapRoute`).** `route(a, b)` is the staircase over the road centre lines with the fewest turns (the city is a full grid; `CityLayout.road_center`); `goal(custom)` picks the player's waypoint, else the campaign step (`waypoint()`, then `MissionGuide.waypoint()`), else the side contract. The map draws it with a dark casing, chevrons and the distance (`ROUTE 584 M` beside the title); the minimap draws the same route (`Minimap._draw_gps`, cached, rebuilt every `MapRoute.REFRESH_M`) cut exactly at the rim by `MapRoute.clip_to_circle`.
- **Danger.** `MapScreen.danger_zones()`: a core district whose ruling camp (Factions) is HOSTILE to you is a red quadrant labelled HOSTILE, ambush / rift events and the wanted search area (`Game.heat`) are red circles.
- Gaps: nothing measured on a real phone (the pinch is covered by synthetic touch events); the route is the road grid only (no one-way or blocked roads, no street names); markers have no per-marker detail card, only the label; the minimap shows the objective route but no danger tint; the fog is the 40 m cell grid (blocky edge); Spanish / Hindi strings for the new map words (INTERIORS, JOBS, ROUTE, HOSTILE, CHARTED) are missing.

## Mission flow, stuck handling and mercy (owner round 3; tests/test_mission_guide.gd, tests/test_mission_audit.gd, tests/test_mission_audit_kinds.gd, tools/mission_audit.sh)
- **MissionGuide** (`scripts/missions/mission_guide.gd`, `camp.guide`, ticked by `CampaignRunner.tick`): `line()` is the HUD line between steps ("NEXT  HACK THE LEDGER NODE") and inside a building the next street step cannot start in ("LEAVE THE BUILDING TO CONTINUE", which outranks the interior's own job line); `waypoint()` points the HUD diamond and the new **compass strip** (`Hud._draw_compass`: 15 degree ticks, N / E / S / W, goal diamond, distance) at the next goal or the exit; a wander reminder "NEXT: HACK THE LEDGER NODE, 420 M" after `WANDER_AFTER` 24 s without getting closer (not in a firefight); **stuck detection**: no progress (bar, distance, foes, step change) for `STUCK_OFFER` 90 s offers SKIP STEP (touch button `skip_piece` relabelled, K key, `Hud.step_skip_offered()`), another `STUCK_RECOVER` 60 s later `recover()` runs: `CampaignRunner.recover_step()` moves a reach / hack / defend / rift goal beside the player (a kill target is brought to the street beside them) once, then completes the step. An armed step blocked by the player standing indoors is forced open after 45 s. Counters `recoveries`, `offers`, `skips`, `reminders` feed the audit. `MissionGuide.hud_on` is the before / after switch of `tests/bot_mission_guide.gd`.
- **Boss hint and armour**: `VerbBoss.hint()` (plain verb names: COLLAPSE WHEN IT JUMPS, COLLAPSE BREAKS THE SHIELD, ENTANGLE IT INTO A VENT ...) shows under the boss bar (`CampaignRunner.boss()["hint"]`); a verb puzzle nobody solves lets the armour give (`WEAKEN_AFTER` 40 s without losing 6 % health, down to x0.6 over 40 s; "ARMOUR FAILING  KEEP SHOOTING").
- **Fixes the audit found** (each was a real hard lock): enemy cap full of street patrols meant `AREA CROWDED` forever for a step target, a contract (`EnemyManager.make_room`) and side-job kill stages; chase / convoy targets drove off before the player was in a car (now a car is left in the right-hand lane nose to the target or the nearest car is pointed at: HUD "TAKE THE CAR", 25 s grace, 150 / 170 m escape, targets ease off when the player is behind, every failed run makes them easier and gives time); escape step: heat climbed to 5 with no way down (heat is capped at the start value and cools 0.3 / s once `dist` away); the escortee walked an L over the blocks through buildings (`CampaignSteps.street_route`, also the reach line) and waited forever for hostiles that never closed in (`ESCORT_PATIENCE`); side-job waypoints landed inside buildings (snapped to streets), kill stages had nobody to kill (the job spawns and tops up its own intruders, waypoint to the nearest), the smash stage had no waypoint (nearest unbroken prop) and the clock now covers the way (`_fit_limit`); an armed step waited for a player who stood inside a building without saying so.
- **Difficulty** (owner: "sometimes too hard"): `Difficulty.hurt_aim(hp_frac)` multiplies enemy aim error by 1 + (1 - health) * HURT_AIM (STORY 1.5, NORMAL 0.9, HARD 0.4, QUANTUM 0.15; `Operative._shoot`, `EnemyManager.bolt`); **mercy health pack** (`Missions._tick_mercy`): wounded below MERCY_BELOW (45 / 30 / 20 % / never) with hostiles within 45 m, one pack 7 m away from them per MERCY_EVERY (15 / 25 / 45 s); **retry right at the step** (`CampaignRunner._retry_spot`): a hack / defend / rift / kill retry starts 32 m from the goal, and a failed or interrupted hold step keeps half of its bar (`_kept`).
- **Mission audit** (`tests/mission_audit.gd`): the bot plays chapters 1-4 in the real scene from the new game through NEXT SECTOR with real input (street route walking with blink-sized hops on long legs, shooting through the camera rig, USE at doors and cars, driving, verbs per verb boss, rift feeding) and never calls `skip_step`; findings carry a reason class (UNREACHABLE, NO_ENEMY, TARGET_LOST, DOOR, CUTSCENE, TIMEOUT, GUIDE_RECOVERED, NO_WAYPOINT, NO_OBJECTIVE, NO_CAR). `test_mission_audit` needs `const TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (runner.gd reads it, at most 140 s; the bot stops at 118 s real and then needs two chapters). Runs at 16x with 8 physics ticks per game second. `test_mission_audit_kinds`: rift hold, convoy and heist sandboxes, five side missions, structure of every contract kind, heist and activity. `tools/mission_audit.sh` adds all twenty side missions and writes `docs/shots/slice/mission_audit.txt`; `AUDIT_FROM=<chapter>:<step>` and `AUDIT_VERBOSE=1` for debugging.


## Render glitches (owner round 2/3 screenshots; tests/test_render_glitches.gd, tests/bot_glitch_scan.gd, tools/glitch_scan.sh; sheet docs/shots/slice/render_glitches_before_after.png)
- **How they were found.** `tools/glitch_scan.sh OUT [spots]` renders the real game camera at 2400x1080 (touch HUD on) at the owner's places (street crosswalk, every interior, a hit burst at the lens, a teleport) and, with `LAYERS=1`, hides every translucent family (shader name or transparent material class) one at a time and runs `VisualQaLib.translucent_slabs(frame, frame_without_layer)`: the biggest connected, filled, smooth patch a layer adds (>= 2 % of the frame, >= 48 px each way) is a slab. Without a base frame the same call judges the frame alone (a big flat pale rectangle, `kind` = "flat"). `GLITCH_HIDE=a,b` bisects by node path / shader file, `NEAR` lines list every translucent node within 8 m of the lens when a flat sheet shows. Spots `burst`, `tp_<n>`, `beacon`, `name#2` (repeat) exist for this.
- **Causes and fixes** (all measured before and after in the sheet): (1) the hero's hit / death burst (`HeroPresentation.make_burst`) was a 1 m untextured additive billboard quad per mote (its size came from `scale_amount`, which a `BILLBOARD_ENABLED` material without `billboard_keep_scale` ignores): metre-wide flat squares at the lens, a full white-out in a fight; now a 6 cm soft dot, `top_level`. `ImpactSparks` puffs got `billboard_keep_scale` for the same reason. (2) `fx_qring` PORTAL / SHOCK rings filled their disc (the teleport portal behind the hero, a swirl sheet): fill and swirl now live in a band inside the rim and the disc is faded by the ring-centre distance (`cfill`, 3-9 m). (3) Puzzle barriers and pads were a 4 x 3 m additive box and a flat disc: `fx_barrier.gdshader` (bright frame, scan band, clear middle, fades 1.4-4.2 m from the lens); the verb icons are 64 px anti-aliased with mipmaps, 0.7 m, faded 1.2-3.7 m from the camera (`_fade_icons`). (4) `door_glow.gdshader` filled every door with a lit sheet: now a frame with an edge glow and a thinner pad. (5) `interior_glow.gdshader` halos fell off as a product of two edge ramps (a soft rectangle): now round. (6) Activity entry columns and the checkpoint beacon were flat 35-40 % cylinders: now `MissionVisuals.make_beam` (fresnel edge, fade, thins near the lens). (7) `EnemyMarks`: ring and flare size are capped at `MARK_NEAR_K` (0.34) of the distance to the lens and no fallback ring closer than `MARK_MIN_DIST` (3.5 m) (screen-wide arcs and flashes from an enemy at arm's length). (8) `fx_muzzle` and `fx_tracer` fade within 3 m of the camera. (9) Ground quantum film (`qs_film`) is a faint cool tint (a third of the strength, 30 % of the rainbow) and the phase-seam baseline is halved (huge lilac / teal swirls on wet roads); the second-state flicker is 0.9 (was 1.6).
- **Test** (`test_render_glitches.gd`): the slab check on synthetic frames (sheet, thin beam, small glow, flat rect, busy frame), then in the real scene: the burst emitter is world-space, 6 cm, textured; a walk of every particle emitter (no untextured billboards, no scaled emitters, no scaled billboard particles without `billboard_keep_scale`); enemy rings and flares under the cap with an operative 2.2 m from the lens; barrier shader and its near fade; icon size and anti-aliasing; shader guards for door sheets / round halos / ring `cfill`; an apartment interior has no unshaded translucent flat sheet over 2 x 2 m. A headless run has no pixels, so the rendered check is the bot.

## Slice art: market street and interiors (owner round 3; tests/test_slice_art.gd, tests/bot_slice_art.gd; sheets docs/shots/slice/slice_art_*.png)
- Interiors (bodega, bar, warehouse): wall styles in the surface shader (COLOR.a of a wall quad = style: plaster, tiled lower half, corrugated sheet with hazard band, brick) and floor styles (alpha of a floor quad: tiles, checker, wood planks, concrete with lane lines), set per type / room role in `InteriorKinds` (`style`, `styles`, `fstyle`, `fstyles`). Three ACCENT light pools per street room (`InteriorPlan._accent_pools`, flag `accent`, the node writes a NEGATIVE radius): the shader keeps their colour while the neutral pools stay desaturated; they sort first so LOW (5 pools) draws them. Wall dressing (`_dress_walls`, six new wall pieces: wall_shelf, ad_panel, banner, hazard_sign, mural, clock; no collider, never dropped by a tier) fills every bare wall run over 3.4 m, `InteriorPlan.bare_run_max()` is what the test holds under 4.4 m. Shelf backs get a header board. Civilians and vendors use the room's surface material (`Interior.actor_material()`, `InteriorCivilian.light_with`) so they are lit by the pools instead of black cut-outs; bodies got hands, shoes, hair, packs, brighter coats.
- Street: LOW now has real shopfront interiors (back-wall wash in the bay colour, ceiling strip lights, shelves of goods; `building_core.gdshaderinc`), a cheap lit-window variety branch for LOW (curtain, blinds, lamp or floor glow), road wear on every tier within 52 m (manholes, cracks, dark repair patches with a seam; `ground_core.gdshaderinc`), and the `ShopSets` family (cafe table with stools and candle, lit sandwich board, crates and barrel, two bollards; one merged mesh, one draw, own random stream so no other placement moved; 80 m window).
- Perf (PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2): LOW 69 draws / 131k prims / 145 MB, MED 101 / 238k / 183 MB, HIGH 107 / 339k / 304 MB: all OK. test_world MED spawn triangle budget is 160k (now 157k).

## The fight (slice; owner round 3; tests/test_slice_fight.gd, tests/bot_slice_fight.gd; sheets docs/shots/slice/fight_*.png)
- Script of the encounter director (`scripts/enemies/encounters.gd`, still off under tools / tests unless a test enables it): quiet opening, a radio callout `CALLOUT_LEAD` (3.5 s) before the patrol, the patrol about `FIRST_DELAY` (24 s) after play starts (about 30 s with the walk-in), three waves (`SET_WAVES`) of 5-8 Vael troopers by tier (`PATROL_SIZES`: LOW 5/5/5, MED 6/7/7, HIGH 7/8/8, trimmed by the tier's enemy cap: LOW+ keeps its 3), then `SET_CALM` (60 s) of calm and the next set. The gap between the waves of a set is 4-6.5 s or ends early when nobody aware is near. Phases: `open | wave | gap | calm`; `set_no`, `wave_in_set`, signals `callout(text, dirs)`, `wave_started`, `wave_cleared`, `set_cleared`.
- Directions: every wave picks one road point per spawn group (`_pick_points`, 15-27 m, closest to the group's bearing from the camera: ahead, the side, behind; the side flips each wave) at the CALLOUT, so the radio line ("VAEL PATROL: 6 TROOPERS. AHEAD AND LEFT") names where they really come from. The grid limits it (a flank arrives along a cross street or from behind; the rest of the road points are straight lines). The ahead group appears at the far end of the street, never at arm's length.
- Roles (`Operative.set_squad_role`; the squad callout that normally hands them out is skipped): ahead = pusher (rushes in), side = flanker (assigned 9 s arc, then rushes), behind = anchor (cover, suppressing fire). Types: `patrol_type` (wave 1 troopers, wave 2 + flanker and a kamikaze, wave 3 + shield and a kamikaze; chapter 0 keeps troopers, camp is always Vael except the kamikaze, so no hovering drone sits out of reach). Pressure: 12 s into a wave (6 s when only two are left) every trooper still standing is sent in (`Operative.squad_rush`): a camper in cover no longer stalls a wave (found by the bot: one anchor held a wave open for 15 s). One backup of two per wave, as before.
- Heat: the patrol's kills carry `HEAT_SHARE` 0.05; a cleared set pulls the heat back to what it was before plus `HEAT_KEEP` (0.3), so the calm after the set is not a Watch hunt.
- Threat markers (`Hud._draw_threat_edges`): red wedges on an oval round the crosshair, one per trooper of the live wave that is off screen (beside the view or behind the hero), at most 8, pulsing; none for a trooper in view. `EnemyMarks.RING_SQUAD`: an aware encounter trooper gets the thin hostile ring from 14 m (the other enemies keep 40 m): at 15-40 m a dark thin figure is a few pixels on a phone.
- Tracers (`fx_tracer.gdshader`): thin near the lens (width scales with the distance to the camera, fully faded inside 1.4 m, clear by 5 m), shorter tail and a lower base brightness: a shot passing the camera was a 40 px pale band across the screen.
- Frames: `FIGHT_OUT=<dir> FIGHT_SECONDS=90 BOT_FASTEXIT=1 BOT_USER_ARGS="--touch" BOT_RES=1600x720 QUALITY=0 ENGINE_ARGS="--fixed-fps 12" BOT_TIMEOUT=1500 tools/bot.sh res://tests/bot_slice_fight.gd /tmp/x 100000 1` (phone HUD on, civilians' rounds pass through, one frame every 3 s of game time, every 1 s while a wave is on; about 10 min of wall time under software GL; `FIGHT_LINE=1` lines troopers up 7-33 m ahead for readability looks, `FIGHT_LINE=tracer` shoots tracers past the lens). `tools/bot.sh` takes `ENGINE_ARGS` (engine flags such as `--fixed-fps`) and `BOT_USER_ARGS` (game flags such as `--touch`).
- Last 90 s bot run (MED-less LOW, 1600x720, `docs/shots/slice/fight_waves_1.jpg`, `fight_waves_2.jpg`, `fight_frame_wave2.png`): callout at 24 s ("VAEL PATROL: 5 TROOPERS. AHEAD AND BEHIND"), three waves cleared at 46 / 58 / 70 s, 21 kills, then calm. Known looks: troopers at 20-30 m are a few pixels (rings and the off-screen wedges carry them), "WANTED LOST" toasts interleave with the callout, a parked car's hood can fill the bottom of the frame at the start spot.

## Street life (slice; owner round 3 "the streets are empty"; tests/test_slice_life.gd, tests/bot_slice_life.gd; sheet docs/shots/slice/life_before_after.png)
- Contract: within 40 m of the spawn (the market avenue, its cross road, the junction) there are always 20+ civilians (walking, talking, shopping, fleeing), 6+ cars moving at city speed, vendors at their stalls, fauna and a street event. `LifeCensus.take(center, r)` counts them (tests, the bot); the test checks 5 positions on every tier (LOW lowest 36 people, MED 32, cars median 7+).
- Crowd: `AmbientCrowd.COUNTS` LOW 48 / MED 84 / HIGH 96 (was 24 / 56 / 90); vendors are pooled crowd slots pinned to a stall (`StreetVendors`, `AmbientCrowd.pin_vendor`), scared they run, shot they die and the stall gets a new one. `CityProps.stall_mesh` (266 triangles, one draw, 60 m window).
- Cars: `NearTraffic` (scripts/ambient/near_traffic.gd): ONE MultiMesh of 350-triangle lit cars, CPU lanes (right-hand lane, lights, queues, turns on a curve, stop for people, drift round the hero and the camera, `CAM_CLEAR`), 72 / 88 / 100 cars per tier, drawn only inside the camera cone and `DRAW_R` (42 m on LOW / MED: the far traffic layer takes over). `promote_near` makes a car real when the hero reaches it or crashes (any car can be taken). `VehicleManager.hear` also reaches them. Real AI cars brake for near cars (`NearTraffic.blocks`).
- Lens: a car whose body is at the camera is taken out (`NearTraffic.CAM_KILL`; a STANDING real traffic car within `VehicleManager.LENS_CLEAR` 3.4 m of the camera): its roof filled a third of the screen in the life bot frames.
- Beats (`StreetBeats`): an argument, a street show others stop to watch, a runner, a horn; one every 6-11 s within 12-38 m; no node per beat.
- Map: `MapScreen.reveal_start()` charts the original city (|x|, |z| <= 300 m, 25 %) at the start of every profile (never marks the file dirty); walking still charts 150 m around the hero.
- Perf (PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh 0 1 2): LOW 71 draws / 135k OK; MED 110 / 245k visible, 280k total (limit 280k); the MED and HIGH total DRAW counts (142 / 158 vs 140, death 158 / 187) are over without any of the life features (`PERF_NO_LIFE=7` bot_perf A/B: unchanged), they come from other in-flight work (HUD, encounters). A/B: `PERF_NO_LIFE=<1 cars + 2 stalls + 4 fauna>`. `test_world` MED spawn triangle budget is 170k (the crowd is 84 instead of 56 on MED).

## Interiors that do not kill, the hold steps and the fight (director round 2 part B; tests/test_director_round2_b.gd, real scene; tests/bot_round2_fight.gd; sheet docs/shots/slice/round2_b_before_after.jpg)
- Inside a building the street is switched off: `CityEvents._process_body`, `EnemyManager._tick_reinforcements`, `_tick_stream` and `call_reinforcement` return while `InteriorGameplay.active`. The only enemies in a room are the interior's guards and the followers that came through the door (`InteriorActors`). Shop interiors (`InteriorData.has_vendor`) have `friendly` guards (`SHOP_DIST` 0: no sight, ignore heat) until `_on_player_fired` or a hit.
- `InteriorGameplay.pressure()` scales a guard's damage on the hero: `PRESSURE_FROM` 0.4 after the entry grace, up to 1.0 over `PRESSURE_SECS` (45 s); `Operative._shoot` multiplies by it. `InteriorGameplay.rescue(p)` (called by `Player.damage` after `Companion.rescue`) forgives one killing blow per visit (`mercy_left`, `MERCY_HP` 0.45, `MERCY_INVULN` 4 s); `inside_t` counts seconds since the door.
- Plate rules (`Hud._campaign_line`): a room job (`InteriorGameplay.hud_line`) owns the plate; no job and a step that cannot run here (or an `enter_interior` step in phase 1 = the wrong building) says `LEAVE THE BUILDING TO CONTINUE`; the dead state draws no mission line; `TouchControls._label_of` shows the first word of the interior prompt for USE.
- Hold steps: `CampaignRunner.HOLD_HEAT_CAP` 1.5 and `HOLD_HEAT_DECAY` 0.06 / s in `_tick_hack` / `_tick_defend`.
- Enemy aim: `Operative.aim_radius(spread, spread_per_m, d, burst_idx, hero_speed, hero_sprint, hero_cover, hurt)` is the radius in metres of a uniform disc round the aim point (`AIM_FLOOR` 0.45 x spread / 0.035 + d x (spread + spread_per_m x d), `BURST_CLIMB`, `MOVE_MULT`, `SPRINT_MULT`, `COVER_MULT`); chance to hit a 0.5 m body is about (0.5 / radius)^2. A miss inside `NEAR_MISS` (2.2 m) sparks where it lands (through the manager's emitter budget) and shakes the camera a little. `Operative.round_stats` {fired, hit, near} accumulates every round that flew at the hero (tests and bots reset it).
- Roster: `HumanoidVariants.VAEL_CLASSES` (six looks: `jacket`, `pants`, `helmet`, `trim`, `scale3`), `VAEL_PICK` (rifleman most often), `ROLE_CLASS` (role -> look), `variant(kind, seed, force_class)`; the variant carries `class` and `scale3`, `Drone._look_scale` applies it to the model root. All armour stays dark (luminance < 0.36: never the hero's jacket). Mesh keys still hold six entries (the class index replaces the colour index): no extra mesh or texture memory.


## Round 3 gameplay fixes (tests/test_director_round3_a.gd)
