- **BridgeKit / BridgeWorld**: per bridge TWO meshes and two draws (structure: slab with kerbs and parapets, girders, railing posts, piers every `piers` metres, abutments, for the landmark towers, main cables on a parabola, hangers every 14 m, back stays, lamps, a red-painted steel truss; road surface: a ribbon with the outskirts road shader, so the lane marks continue over the deck), structure and road culled beyond 1.45 km (`SHOW_RANGE`: the perf gate counts every draw in the frustum and the start of the city looks south at the harbour 1.5 km away; the harbour kit, ground and road use the same range, its detail 450 m), ONE StaticBody3D per bridge on the world layer (a box per straight segment for the deck with its top exactly at the deck height, a parapet wall per side). All kit meshes use `harbour_kit.gdshader` (vertex colour = paint, COLOR.a = material code: 1 container, 2 concrete, 3 steel, 4 lamp, 5 asphalt, 6 cladding; world-position noise, ribs and stains, no textures). 10 bridges, 17 meshes, about 20 k triangles in all (the gate 4.8 k, the trestle 4.8 k).
- **BridgeTraffic**: four scenery cars (the real street car models, `VehicleVisual`) on each road bridge longer than 60 m and on the harbour gate; built lazily and shown only while the hero is within 520 m (`BridgeWorld._process`), no physics, they follow the deck in their own lane at 9-16 m/s, wrap at the ends. Real cars (the hero's, hijacked ones) cross on the real deck.
- **The harbour** (`Harbour`, pure data + `carve`): `TerrainHeight.height_at` now ends with `Harbour.carve`: an APRON (x -340..400, z 1340..1640) filled to the quay height 1.2 m (blending 40 m on the land sides, a 2.5 m quay wall on the sea side) and a dredged BASIN (x -100..160, z 1400..1740, 8 m deep, open to the sea in the south, banks over 3 m). `HarbourKit` builds four merged meshes: GROUND (concrete slabs, yellow quay-edge line, `harbour_ground.gdshader`), KIT (quay walls with coping and fenders, three finger piers on piles, 238 containers, 5 warehouses with gabled roofs, the harbour office, ferry terminal, three gantry cranes with trolley and hanging box, tank farm with pipes, the lighthouse), DETAIL (lamps, bollards, crates; culled beyond 450 m), ROAD (harbour road ribbon from the coast road to the bridge). `HarbourWorld` adds 60 collision boxes and the FERRY (38 m passenger boat on a loop out of the basin and back, an AnimatableBody3D that bobs through `WaterWorld.buoyancy`, wakes, only simulated while the hero is within 1.6 km). A 7th road, HARBOUR ROAD, branches off the COAST ROAD to the apron. `QT_NO_BRIDGES=1` / `QT_NO_HARBOUR=1` leave the two nodes out for perf A/B runs.
- **Boats** (`VehicleCraft._boat`, `_keep_afloat`, `place_boat`): the SPEEDBOAT, new JET SKI (33 m/s, light, twitchy, `procedural` model) and PATROL BOAT (9 m, 760 hp, BOW CANNON) share class `boat`. In the flooded quarter nothing changed (flat plane, clamped to the rect). Anywhere else the hull rides the real water: height, pitch and roll from `WaterWorld.buoyancy` (smoothed), a river's current pushes it, the hull rises onto the plane at speed (`State.planing`), it must find draft + 0.6 m under its centre and +0.3 m under its bow or it RUNS AGROUND (put back on `last_wet`, speed x -0.2, `State.groundings`), so quay walls, beaches and shallows stop it. `Vehicle._terrain_step` leaves a boat outside the plateau alone, `Vehicle.activate` calls `place_boat`. Wake (`_wake`): `BoatWake` (scripts/vehicles/boat_wake.gd, one soft white ribbon of at most 28 points that spreads and fades over 3.2 s, ONE draw, only on a boat near the player), stern spray, a bow wave from 40 % speed, a rooster tail from 70 %, a ripple ring on the surface every 0.32 s. The procedural boat bodies wear no lamp nodes (the additive car-lamp halos blew a jet ski out to a white blob at night). Berths (`Harbour.BERTHS`, six, appended by `VehicleSpawns.all_points()`, polled by `VehicleManager._tick_spawn_points`): speedboats at pier 1 and 2 and the east quay, two jet skis, the patrol boat at the west quay. Exit: `VehicleManager._exit_point` puts the hero on a pier or quay when `Harbour.exit_point` finds deck within 9 m, else he steps into the water and swims.
- **Boat mission hooks** (`BoatRun`, child of HarbourWorld): the BOAT RUN (drive through the cyan start buoy, six buoys, gold beam to the next, pays 120 + 40 per 10 s under 75 s through `Progress.add_credits`, best time kept for the session, ends quietly when the hero leaves the boat) and the signals `started`, `checkpoint(i)`, `finished(seconds, ok, reward)`, `cancelled(why)`, `docked(dock_id, boat)` (a boat held slow inside a `Harbour.DOCKS` radius for 1 s: PIER A/B/C, FERRY LANDING, WEST QUAY); `BoatRun.hooks()` lists docks and buoys as data for the mission board (not offered by the board yet).
- **Budgets**: no textures, no new materials per object (two shared ShaderMaterials + one ground + two road materials), harbour 4 draws + ferry, bridges 2 per bridge; harbour 14 k triangles, all culled by distance and far from the city centre (1.4 km). Texture memory unchanged. Perf gate (`PERF_CHECK=1 tools/perf_report.sh`): LOW 78 draws / 100 total (same as without the new nodes), MED 103 / 135, nodes +320 (collision shapes). Sheets docs/shots/slice/bridges_harbour_boats_before_after.jpg and bridges_harbour_overview.jpg.
- Tests edited (meaning kept): test_terrain_world (the profile-under-road check skips bridge spans).
- Gaps: no boat model art (procedural bodies; the speedboat is the open CC0 model); traffic cars on bridges are scenery (real traffic still stays on the city grid); no cargo ship, no trains yet; the BOAT RUN is not offered by the mission board; the hero swims out of a boat in open water (no boarding from the water); no sound for the jet ski beyond the engine loop; the ferry does not stop for boats (it is solid: boats bump it); the harbour has no NPCs or wildlife; roster picture `v_jetski.png`, `v_patrol.png` are not made (the two kinds are not in `VehicleSpecs.ROSTER`); es / hi strings for the new names.


## Mountain and outskirt sites, vegetation, mountain air (wave 105; scripts/terrain/outskirts_sites.gd, outskirts_kit.gd, outskirts_world.gd, cave_system.gd, guard_rails.gd, veg_kit.gd, veg_scatter.gd, vegetation_world.gd, mountain_weather.gd; shaders vegetation / outskirts_kit / cave / cloud_layer; test tests/test_outskirts_sites.gd in the real scene, about 100 s; look-dev bot tests/bot_outskirts.gd, tests/veg_viewer.tscn)
- **Eleven sites** (`OutskirtsSites.DEFS`, pure data, `ensure()` is called at the end of `TerrainRoads.ensure()` because some sites stand where a road ends): MOUNTAIN OBSERVATORY (end of SUMMIT ROAD), RADIO TOWER (end of RIDGE TRACK), OLD MINE (end of QUARRY TRACK), ALIEN CRASH SITE (a crater with a rim in the desert south-east of the city), HIDDEN CAVE (mouth in the east wall of MIRROR LAKE; hidden on the map until found), LAKESIDE LODGE and HUNTERS CABINS (four log cabins, campfire, fence), PINE HOLLOW (forest village, 12 log houses) and DUST WELLS (desert village, adobe houses, water tank), SOUTH FARM and NORTH FARM (barn, two silos, farmhouse, fields, windmill that turns). Each has a flat PAD (`r` flat metres, then `blend`) cut into the finished terrain: `OutskirtsSites.carve` is chained at the end of `TerrainHeight.height_at` (after the road benches and the harbour quay), so meshes, colliders, vehicles and the hero's feet agree. Pad height = the road profile at the road's end, or the mean of the land under the pad; the crash site is a bowl 7 m deep (stays above the sea) with a raised rim. `inside(x, z, margin)` keeps vegetation and rail posts off the pads; `to_world(site, local)` turns the kit's local frame (front = -Z) into world space; `hooks()` is the mission-hook list (`{id, kind, name, pos, r, reward, tag}`, the same idea as `BoatRun.hooks()`), `map_markers()` the pins (kind `site`, a peak glyph, legend row SITES; the cave only after `is_found`), `vehicle_points()` the parked rides (added to `VehicleSpawns.all_points`: off-road kinds at the mountain sites, pickups, quads, a van, a hover car at the crash site).
- **Two mountain roads with real hairpins** (`TerrainRoads.DEFS`): SUMMIT ROAD (853 m, branches off the end of PASS ROAD, climbs the rim of MIRROR LAKE to 150 m) and RIDGE TRACK (1175 m off MOUNTAIN TRACK, to 153 m with a hairpin up the east range). New in the router: `"wide": true` adds the 8 knight moves (16 headings, a hillside steeper than the grade limit can only be climbed by turning across the fall line; the 8-direction lattice cannot gain height on a 40 % slope at all). QUARRY TRACK now ends at (1860, -540), clear of QUARRY LAKE (the mine stands there).
- **Site meshes** (`OutskirtsKit.build(site)` -> {mesh, boxes, rotors, lamps, use, tris}): everything in one merged ArrayMesh per site (one draw call) with `outskirts_kit.gdshader` (no textures: vertex colour = paint, alpha = material code: paint, timber with log seams, rock with moss, metal, lit window, shingles, corrugated sheet, alien hull with thin glowing veins, soil; snow dusting above `snow_start`; night windows). Sites are built lazily by `OutskirtsWorld` within 1000 m of the hero (one per 0.25 s tick) and freed beyond 1800 m; 0.9 to 2.9 k triangles each, distance culled at 1100 m, so the city views never draw them. Collision = a few boxes per site in one StaticBody3D. Two pooled OmniLights (MED and HIGH) hop to the nearest lamps within 70 m of the hero.
- **Discovery and activities** (free roam, no menu): within `r * 0.8 + 26` m of a site it is DISCOVERED (toast, a quarter of the reward, the pin is on the map, persisted in `user://outskirts.cfg`); its USE spot pays the rest ONCE: radio tower console (SIGNAL RESTORED), observatory console (SKY SCAN: every site, the hidden cave included, goes on the map), mine (shaft stash), crash site (core fragment), cave stash. Cabins, villages and farms heal the hero and restock ammo on walking up (45 s cooldown). Nothing needs a button: it is a walk-up trigger like the weapon crates.
- **The cave** (`CaveSystem`): four chambers joined by tunnels, built from a footprint (discs and capsules) on a 1.6 m grid as a floor sheet and a ceiling sheet that pinch together at the edges (rounded, no wall quads), stalactites, glowing crystals, a stash chest; about 3 k triangles, one trimesh collider; it hangs inside the north massif at `CaveSystem.ORIGIN` (350, 12, -2430): 170 m of rock above, no water near, above the void line (`Player.VOID_Y` -60). Walking into the mouth (`OutskirtsWorld._use`) calls `enter` (register the `InteriorSpace`, so the city rescue and blink clamp leave the hero alone, and teleport); the exit ring brings him back to the mouth. `cave.gdshader` is unshaded: the hero's headlamp (`torch`) and 8 crystal glows are computed per pixel, so the sun can never reach in.
- **Guard rails** (`GuardRails`): wherever the land falls more than 3.5 m below a mountain road within 9 m of the flat bench, a W-beam on posts follows the edge (`segments()`, 4 m pieces; chunked into one mesh per 400 m cell, culled at 520 m) and a pool of 26 collision boxes follows the hero (nearest pieces within 90 m, refreshed after 12 m of movement).
- **Vegetation** (`VegetationWorld`, child `vegetation` of the City, `QT_NO_VEGETATION=1` leaves it out): `VegScatter.cell(layer, ci, cj)` is a pure deterministic function (32 m cells, jittered grid, hash-based; four layers: trees, shrubs, grass, rocks) of the biome weights, the altitude (tree line about 215 m, ragged), slope, water, roads and pads: forests of pines and broadleaf trees on the NW / W hills and the foothills, pines and dead trunks climbing the mountains, lone trees and bushes on the plains, scrub and dead trunks in the desert, rocks and boulders everywhere (cliff-sized on steep or high ground). `VegKit` loads the 16-piece Quaternius kit (`assets/models/nature_kit.glb`, converted by `tools/open_models/convert_nature.py`; flat vertex colours, one 1024 x 512 alpha atlas) and generates the mid and far tree LODs (cones, blobs) in code. ONE MultiMesh per piece and LOD, so the whole forest is at most 17 draw calls: a worker task (serialised, cache of generated cells) sorts the plants of the cells around the hero into pieces by distance (near piece, mid LOD, far LOD, the far LOD draws 55 % of the trees 30 % bigger; the next LOD dissolves in over the last 15 % of the previous one through a dither in the shader, no popping) and the main thread only assigns the buffers (`visible_instance_count`, capacity grows in steps). A re-arrangement starts after 5 m of movement. Per tier (LOW MED HIGH): near radius 24 / 31 / 42 m, mid 80 / 98 / 132, far 140 / 220 / 320, shrubs 30 / 44 / 64, grass 15 / 22 / 32, rocks 80 / 120 / 170, pine and broadleaf variants 1 / 2 / 2, trunk colliders 26 / 38 / 52. A pool of StaticBody3D cylinders (the nearest trunks and boulders within 30 m) makes trees solid for the hero and for cars. `vegetation.gdshader`: wind (sway by height squared, a gust that moves over the world, leaf flutter), snow dusting by world height, fill light so rock and bark never go black, backlight for leaves, night and wet grades.
- **Mountain air** (`MountainWeather`, child of the City): one cloud deck disc (`cloud_layer.gdshader`, drifting noise) at y 188 that follows the camera, alpha by the mountain weight and the camera's height above or below the deck (gone over the city); snow flurries (CPU particles) above 150 m in the mountains, heavier with height; `MountainWeather.fog_boost` thickens the haze inside the cloud band (read by `Weather._apply_env`, one edit).
- **Budgets measured by the test** (forest cell at the Hunters Cabins side of the west hills, dense forest in view): LOW 59 k triangles (limit 65 k), MED 102 k (120 k), HIGH 178 k (210 k); the sites 0.9 to 2.9 k triangles each (all eleven together 20 k), the rails 7 k in ten chunk meshes (nodes sit at their chunk centre: a node's visibility range is measured to its origin, a chunk node at the world origin was drawn in every city view); a forest view is at most 17 vegetation draws, a site 1 draw (+1 for the farm windmill, the crash site's scorch ring), the rails 1 or 2. Texture memory: the atlas 1024 x 512 VRAM compressed with mipmaps (about 0.6 MB), nothing else; the shared city views are untouched (no vegetation or site within the draw range in the city; `QT_NO_VEGETATION=1`, `QT_NO_SITES=1` for A/B runs).
- **Roads and pads**: a site at a road's end does not touch the road bench (`carve` fades the pad out within 14 m of the centre line); the free-standing sites keep their whole pad plus blend clear of every road and of the far skyline of towers (190 m beyond the plateau), checked by the test. Vegetation keeps boulders off whole road benches (the coarse terrain mesh cannot follow a road cut: boulders hovered over it) and digs rocks and trunks into slopes.
- **Shared edits you may trip over**: `TerrainHeight.height_at` (+ `OutskirtsSites.carve`) and `warm()`; `TerrainRoads` (two roads, `wide`, QUARRY TRACK via point, `roads_built()`, a call to `OutskirtsSites.ensure()` at the end of `ensure()`); `City` (vars `vegetation`, `sites`, attach, `set_quality`); `Weather._apply_env` (+ `MountainWeather.fog_boost`); `VehicleSpawns.all_points` (+ the site rides); `MapScreen` / `MapIcons` (kind `site`: KINDS, KIND_LABEL, KIND_COLOR, the glyph, the markers). tools/requirements.txt gained pillow (the converter). Tests edited (meaning kept): test_terrain_world (the straight SUV run to the sea turns the vegetation off: trunks are solid now), test_menus_map_quality passes through `MapIcons.KINDS` + `site`.
- **Gaps**: no mission-board type for the sites yet (`OutskirtsSites.hooks()` is the entry point; the board's step placement is grid based, an `expedition` recipe with a fixed goal needs a small change in `MissionBoard.build` and the type-count tests); no guards, wildlife or aliens at the sites (enemies are clamped to the city by `Drone.WORLD_LIMIT`; the animals wave and the enemy-roster wave should lift it); the cave is empty apart from the stash; the villages and farms have no people; trees do not cast shadows on LOW and MED (shadow-casting multimeshes cost a lot); the far cloud deck is a single plane (no clouds above the player); the radio tower's red light is a static glow (no blink); no es / hi strings for the new toasts; Android phone timing of the worker job (about 100 ms on a desktop) not measured.


## Railways and ships (wave 106; scripts/rail/*, scripts/ships/*, assets/shaders/rail_kit.gdshader, tools/audio/make_rail.py; test tests/test_railways_ships.gd in the real scene, about 60 s; look-dev bot tests/bot_rail_ships.gd with `RS_VIEWS`)
- **RailNet** (pure data + `carve`): three double-track lines routed by the roads' own A* (`TerrainRoads._route`) and profiled over the terrain: MAGLEV (elevated 11 m on a box-girder viaduct along the street z = -640 through the whole city, WEST TERMINAL, OLD TOWN, CENTRAL, EAST SIDE, DESERT TERMINAL), MOUNTAIN LINE (NORTH STATION, an overpass over the PASS ROAD, FOOTHILLS, a trestle over NORTH CREEK, a long viaduct up the valley, MOUNT STATION, a 346 m tunnel to the underground SUMMIT STATION) and COAST LINE (GREENWAY, a level crossing over the FOREST ROAD, SOUTH HALT, HARBOUR STATION). 8 km of track. The vertical profile is the lower grade-limited envelope of the terrain (5-5.5 % limit, `fill` bias 0.12), lifted 5.2 m over water, pinned flat at stations (zone = platform + 16 m), pinned at the road height at level crossings (|dy| <= 2.2 m, else an overpass 6.2 m over the road), pinned at ELEV over the city plateau. Every sample has a MODE: GROUND (the bench is carved into the terrain: embankment or cutting, `RailNet.carve`, called by `TerrainHeight.height_at` after the roads and before the harbour), VIADUCT (over water, valleys, city streets, road overpasses: terrain untouched) or TUNNEL (terrain 7.5 m or more over the bed). Stations sit on the ground (or on the city viaduct), or underground when the hill over them is deep. At a terminal station the two tracks run together to one centre track (`k`), so a train can reverse there. Trestles over water are emitted as `Bridges` entries of kind `rail` (`RailNet.water_bridges()`, built by BridgeKit._rail with `sep` = track spacing).
  Build order: `TerrainRoads.ensure()` ends with `RailNet.ensure()` (the railway is routed over the finished roads; `TerrainHeight.warm()` does not call it any more); a second builder rewrote `TerrainHeight.height_at` once and dropped `RailNet.carve` from the chain: it must stay between `TerrainRoads.carve` and `Harbour.carve`.
- **RailKit**: a route is cut into 320 m chunks, ONE merged mesh and ONE draw call each (track bed with sleepers drawn by the shader from UV, rails, catenary masts and wires, the maglev guideway, viaduct girders and piers (T piers on the street's middle, never at a junction), tunnel tubes with lamps and portals, platforms, canopies, station halls, stairs and ramps up to the elevated platforms, crossing planks and crossbucks, signal poles). Vertices are relative to the chunk origin. `rail_kit.gdshader` (no textures): kinds 0 paint, 1 container, 2 concrete, 3 steel, 4 lamp, 5 asphalt, 6 plating, 7 window glass, 8 track bed with sleepers.
- **RailWorld** (child of the City, `QT_NO_RAIL=1` leaves it out): builds chunks lazily within 1 km (one per frame), their colliders within 480 m (viaduct decks and parapets, piers, platforms, ramps, tunnel tube as a trimesh), draws chunks within 650 m (nothing of it is in the perf gate's staged view); level crossings (barrier arms that come down by the train timetable, flashing lamps, a bell, solid lowered arms), signals (red while a train stands in the station block or a derailed train is ahead), station name labels, a tunnel light that follows the hero inside a tube, `use_prompt()` / `use_do()` (CLIMB TO ROOF, SIT, STAND UP), minimap `markers()`.
- **Trains** (`Train`, `TrainKit`, `RailSchedule`): six trains (maglev x2, express + ore on the mountain line, regional + container on the coast line), consists double-headed so they reverse at the terminals. A timetable is a pure function of the clock (`RailSchedule.at(t, phase)`: ping-pong over all stations, trapezoid speed profile, dwell 24 s / 45 s at terminals), so trains run on schedule whether watched or not; the two trains of a line are half a period apart and never share a track. Cars are pooled (built once, hidden and without collision beyond 900 m), each an AnimatableBody3D with shared mesh and shapes: the hero stands on the floor, walks in through the door gaps, is CARRIED by the moving car (CharacterBody3D moving platform; `Player.platform_on_leave` is DO_NOTHING so a jump off does not throw him), sits at a seat (`Player.control_hold`), climbs a ladder to the roof (USE) or jumps there. Hits: a vehicle in the way is wrecked, the hero on the track takes 35 damage and a shove; `Train.derail()` stops a train, tilts the cars and blows three of them. Sounds (synthesised, `tools/audio/make_rail.py`, 190 KB): horn, maglev chime, clatter loop, maglev hum, crossing bell, brake squeal.
- **Train missions** (`RailJobs`, child of RailWorld, markers on the minimap through `Freedom.markers()`): HEIST (NORTH STATION: board the ore train, hold a box car roof 4 s, jump off, +350), ESCORT (ride the express to MOUNT STATION, guards storm it after FOOTHILLS, +450), DERAIL (blow the track at the marker on the coast line with a grenade, charge or rocket while the container train runs at it, +600), ROOF CHASE (GREENWAY: board the regional train, four guards stand on its roof, +500). They start when the hero stands in the ring for 1.2 s, time out after 300 s, never trap the player (`abort()`).
- **Ships** (`ShipKit`, `Ship`, `ShipWorld`, child of the City, `QT_NO_SHIPS=1`): NAVAL DESTROYER (140 m, moored at the west headland with a gangway, two gun turrets, a bridge, a helipad aft: walk on the deck, TAKE HELM at the bridge for 17 m/s, MAN GUN at a pedestal: the barrel follows the camera, FIRE sends a shell that explodes where the crosshair rests, 9 m blast), CONTAINER SHIP (190 m, east headland, helm, 12 m/s), SUBMARINE (surfaced at the head of PIER A, walkable casing, helm; no dive), and by the clock at sea CRUISE LINER, TANKER and a second freighter on closed lanes in the deep south, plus ten channel buoys out of the harbour mouth and a lighthouse beam at night. A Ship is an AnimatableBody3D that the world never READS its position from (`sim_pos` is the state, the transform is only written: reading `global_position` after writing it on a body synced to physics gave stale values). Driven ships run aground at shores (draft + 0.5 m), buoyancy from `WaterWorld.buoyancy`. `Harbour.carve` dredges a 10 m berth strip along the south face of both headlands. USE prompts come from `RideUse.prompt()` (HUD hint and touch USE button).
- **Budgets**: no textures; chunks within 650 m only; trains and ships hidden and non-solid when far; perf gate unchanged by this work (A/B with `QT_NO_RAIL=1 QT_NO_SHIPS=1`, see HANDOFF).
- **Gaps**: no submarine dive; no sound for ship guns besides `boom`; train hits on NPC cars (scenery traffic ignores the rails); enemies and civilians do not ride; the camera clips into a car roof indoors; platform side for dir = +1 trains is local -X (tests use it); ships do not avoid each other or the ferry; the lighthouse beam is two additive wedges; no es / hi strings; the elevated stations' ramps are on the street shoulder (cars can clip them); nothing measured on a phone.


## Air and vehicle missions, free roam (wave 107; scripts/missions/{world_sites,world_missions,world_steps,world_roam,path_follow,gate_marks}.gd; tests/test_world_missions.gd + test_world_missions_air.gd + test_world_roam.gd in the real scene, shared helpers tests/wm_base.gd; look-dev bot tests/bot_world_missions.gd; sheets docs/shots/slice/world_missions_*.jpg)
- **Six board missions by location** (`WorldMissions`, own DEFS, the sixteen city types and their tests untouched): AIRPORT HEIST (ground vehicle to the airport, real guards at the hangar, steal the plane on the strip, fly out and land at a field), HELI EXTRACTION (a mountain pad, hold it against waves, the helicopter lands there, fly it to the helipad and land), MOUNTAIN PURSUIT (any ground vehicle to the foot of SUMMIT ROAD / RIDGE TRACK / QUARRY TRACK / MOUNTAIN TRACK, a 4x4 waits, run the smuggler's pickup down the road, secure the cargo), RIVER CHASE (a speedboat left on HIGHLINE RIVER, catch the smuggler's boat, follow it to the mouth), AIR RACE (helicopter on a pad, EASTWATER CANYON up and back or HIGHLINE GORGE, ten gates, land on the pad), PLANE DELIVERY (cargo plane on the strip, fly to a field, land, walk to the buyer). `MissionBoard.world_offers` (on in the live game, off under tools: `Missions` sets it next to the take-over) lets `WorldMissions.inject` replace the last of the three picks: always when the hero is within 1100 m of a mission's site, else 40 % of the time; offers carry `world: true`, the place in the card line ("Hold LAKESIDE LODGE, then fly out by helicopter."), pay = base + 0.1 per metre of route (about 560-900), the usual gift line, and run as a chapter through `CampaignRunner.start_custom` (checkpoint per step, instant retry, MissionRewards through chapter_completed).
- **Places** (`WorldSites`, pure terrain queries): `airport()` reads `Airport.hooks()` of the airport wave (kinds terminal / hangar / runway with heading / helipad; falls back to the old AIRSTRIP and HELIPAD points when the class is missing, `real` false), `heli_pads()`, `landing_fields()` (villages and farms of OutskirtsSites), `road_route(name)`, `river_route(i, a, b)`, `bank_water` (where a boat is left), `canyon_gates` (gates down a river gorge `clear` m over the highest ground within 34 m), `treasure_spots()`, `hooks()`.
- **New step kinds** (`CampaignSteps.KIND_DATA`, `CampaignRunner.StepKinds` now 16, logic in `WorldSteps`, one instance per runner as `runner.ws`): `reach` + `veh` (air, heli, plane, boat, ground: the ring only counts in that kind of vehicle) + `provide` / `provide_at` / `provide_h` (a vehicle waits from the step's begin, held against streaming, an existing one within 40 m is reused so a retry never piles them up) + `land` (an aircraft must be down, `agl < 2.5`, slower than 5 m/s); `gates` (`pts`: 3D gates, `GateMarks` draws the current ring and the next, segment test so a fast aircraft cannot skip one); `pursuit` (`route` polyline, `target` kind, `cruise`, `catch`, `lead`: the target is a REAL vehicle driven through `Vehicle.scripted` / `script_thr` / `script_steer` (a branch in `Vehicle.sim`) by `PathFollow.drive`, it eases off when the hero is behind and waits when he is more than 190 m back, is put back on its line after 3 s stuck, and the step ends when it is wrecked, boxed in (within `catch` m for 2.2 s, it stops) or taken over). HUD line and waypoint for all of them (`GET IN A BOAT`, `GATE 3/10`, `CLOSE IN`, `LAND IT`); MissionGuide's recovery completes a vehicle step instead of moving its goal.
- **Free roam** (`WorldRoam`, child of Freedom: `Freedom.current.roam`, minimap through `Freedom.markers`, big map through `WorldRoam.map_markers()`): FLIGHT SCHOOL (13 rings from the airport strip: along the runway, out over the open land, back), OFF-ROAD TRIAL (7 gates on QUARRY TRACK, a rover and a quad wait at its foot through `VehicleSpawns.all_points`), JET-SKI LAPS (two laps of the harbour buoys), TREASURE HUNT (six caches around the crash site, mine, observatory, hunters' cabins and lodge: the scanner pulse reveals those within 95 m with a beam and a ground ring for 30 s and pings the direction of those within 220 m; walking up digs one out; the scanner is handed over free near the hunt). A course starts by itself when a vehicle of the right kind that is UNDER WAY crosses its start ring (a gold ring and beam show within 520 m), pays 260-340 + 40 per 10 s under par (a repeat pays half), keeps the best time in `user://world_roam.cfg`, ends when the hero leaves the vehicle for 8 s or the time is 2.6 x par, and never runs while a board mission does. The FIRST finish of each activity and every third cache call `MissionRewards.complete(&"activity", ...)`, so the weapon ladder moves (not in DEMO). `reset()` on a fresh game.
- **Render loops** (tests/bot_world_missions.gd, 1200x540 LOW): (1) the air gate ring was lost in the haze at 388 m (additive material, fogged): plain alpha, `disable_fog`, a 2.4 m beam; (2) the flat ring of a ground course was a thick slab: flat gates now use the game's own `ContractVisuals` ring and beam; (3) the board card text ran under the pay chip: the place is in a short line of at most 50 characters; (4) the hero stayed in the helicopter in the next view (bot ordering); (5) the cache had only a beam: a ground ring joins it. Frames: docs/shots/slice/world_missions_before_after.jpg, world_missions_views.jpg.
- Tests (all in the real scene, K = 3 time scale, about 45 s / 47 s / 35 s): `test_world_missions` data (recipes at four origins, validation, dry goals, canyon legs clear of the terrain, PathFollow), the board by location, a MOUNTAIN PURSUIT driven for real through the same inputs a player has (4x4, scripted pickup, boxed in, pay, ladder), a failed pursuit restarting with a fresh target and the recovery, the AIRPORT HEIST (real guard) and the PLANE DELIVERY (air is not a landing, a fast touchdown is not, a stop is) and a RIVER CHASE in a real boat; `test_world_missions_air` the AIR RACE flown for real (ten gates, landing) and the HELI EXTRACTION (real waves, helicopter lands at the zone); `test_world_roam` the three courses, repeat pay, ladder once, leaving, no start during a mission, the treasure hunt with the real scanner pulse. test_campaign_runner now counts 16 step kinds.
- Gaps: es / hi strings for the new lines; no phone measurement (nothing here adds a draw beyond four ring draws and one beam per cache while a course runs); the pursuit target is the stock pickup / speedboat model (no distinct paint); the heli extraction hold is 30 s of waves on mountain pads that have no cover; the plane delivery landing and the airport heist's flight were tested by placing the aircraft (the real takeoff and landing are in test_airport_aircraft and test_vehicle_roster); the off-road trial frame of the look bot did not get the hero into the rover; an air race does not check the heli's fuel or damage (easy by design); MissionBoardScreen still uses the generic MAP icon for five of the six types.


## Airport and aircraft (wave 106; scripts/world/{airport,airport_kit,airport_world,air_traffic,aircraft_scenery}.gd, scripts/vehicles/{aircraft_flight,aircraft_models,air_rockets}.gd, scripts/ui/flight_hud.gd, tools/open_models/convert_aircraft.py, tools/audio/make_air.py; test tests/test_airport_aircraft.gd in the real scene, about 150 s; look-dev tests/aircraft_viewer.tscn + tools/aircraft_view.sh, bot tests/bot_aircraft.gd (`BOT_SHOTS`), field views with tests/bot_outskirts.gd `cam:` views; sheets docs/shots/slice/airport_*.jpg)
- **EASTFIELD AIRPORT** (`Airport` = data + carve, `AirportKit` = meshes, `AirportWorld` = the live node, child of the City, `QT_NO_AIRPORT=1` leaves it out). It lies on the flat plain 1.1 km south of the city's southern edge, EAST-WEST: runway 09 / 27 along z = 1255 from x = 480 to x = 1280 (800 x 44 m), taxiway, three connectors, an apron on the city side with a 190 m terminal (glass front, canopy, four jet bridges and gates), a control tower, two hangars, a cargo shed, a fire station, six fuel tanks, floodlight masts, runway / taxiway / approach lights, PAPI, windsocks, five helipads. The sea and the harbour's open water lie west of it, the plains and the river east, so both approaches are clear of the city and the mountains. Why there: the first plan (the east plain beside the city) collided with the maglev viaduct (a straight line along z = -640 into the desert) and with the far skyline; a probe over the whole 6 km world found this strip free of roads, rails, sites, water and slopes.
- **The field's local frame**: everything is described in a frame where the runway runs along z (north = -z) and the apron lies west of it; `Airport.FRAME` (a quarter turn about Y plus `FRAME_ORIGIN`) places it in the world, `to_world` / `to_local` / `heading_world` convert, and the kit, the colliders and the parked scenery are children of one `Field` node with that transform (so the three field meshes stay local). What leaves the class is WORLD: `carve`, `inside`, `thresholds()` (09 eastbound: touchdown at the west end, 27), `pads()`, `spawn_points()`, `hooks()`, `map_markers()`, `guide()`.
- **The ground**: `TerrainHeight.height_at` ends in `Airport.carve` (after the sites): the whole pad (x 475..1315, z 1093..1373) is cut to y = 0, the height of the city plateau, so it joins the city without a step; 40 m blend (the river is 50 m beyond the east end). `Airport.inside` keeps vegetation out, `Airport.world_rect(margin)` keeps the far skyline towers out (`city_skyline.gd`: the skyline ring of the city fringe would stand on the apron). The mountain pads (RIDGE, PEAK, WEST HILL) are 13 m discs cut into the high country at the ring-mean height of the land under them.
- **Helipads** (`Airport.pads()`, kinds apron / roof / mountain / site): five on the apron (the three flyable machines and two for the air traffic), the CITY ROOF PAD (an elevated 8 m pad on four legs 2.6 m over the roof of a 44 m tower 550 m east of the start, its own cylinder collider, so roof clutter cannot block it), the three mountain pads and one on each of the two mountain sites (observatory, radio tower). Each remote pad is one small mesh (own draw within 700 m); a helicopter or a gunship waits on each one (`sp_pad_*` spawn points, `VehicleManager._tick_spawn_points` puts the aircraft on the pad's height, also on the roof). Map pins: `Airport.map_markers()`.
- **Aircraft** (eight, all on the craft classes `heli` / `plane` of `VehicleCraft`, keys in `VehicleSpecs.SPECS`): SMALL PLANE and HELICOPTER (the round-5 open-source models), JET FIGHTER (CC0 Mig, 14 m, afterburner node `Burner`, guns and homing MISSILES), CARGO PLANE (CC0 wide-body, 44 m, heavy and slow to turn), AIRLINER and BUSINESS JET (CC0, flyable and the scenery of the gates), GUNSHIP (procedural attack helicopter: tandem canopy, stub wings, chain gun and a ROCKET POD) and TRANSPORT HELICOPTER (procedural, five blades, rear ramp, door gun). Numbers per aircraft are spec keys: `max acc stall ceil climb agile jet burner arms2 rotor_r`. `VehicleSpecs.AIRCRAFT_KINDS` lists them; `ROSTER` (pictures `v_*.png` from `tools/icons/build_icons.sh`), `NAMES`, `PALETTES`, `WEAPONS` (CHAIN GUN, DOOR GUN, CANNON), `ALT_WEAPONS` (ROCKET POD, MISSILES) and the audio `ENGINES` families (rotor / jet / prop loops synthesised by `make_air.py`, 6 x 2 s ogg, 100 KB) know them. Models: `convert_aircraft.py` (bpy, flat colours, no texture) turns four OpenGameArt CC0 aircraft into `xv_airliner|bizjet|cargo|jet.glb` (0.55 MB; the airliner and the business jet keep the source's gear, the cargo plane and the fighter get a simple gear), licences in `art_sources/open_aircraft`, credits in docs/THIRD_PARTY.md. `AircraftModels` (a `VehicleCraftModels` subclass) lofts the two helicopters from superellipse ring stacks (`_loft`) with `Rotor` / `TailRotor` nodes; `AircraftScenery.make(kind, paint)` makes a picture-only copy of any of them (airport scenery and air traffic).
- **Flight model** (`AircraftFlight`, called by `VehicleCraft._plane` / `_heli`; `VehicleCraft._move_air` still owns collision, landing damage and exit rules): PLANE: a throttle lever (stick forward / back) drives thrust against drag; V (the speed along the path, `v.speed`) loses speed in a climb and gains it in a dive; the pitch ANGLE follows UP / DOWN (limited so the climb stays at `climb` m/s) and levels out by itself; the stick banks the plane (roll follows the stick) and the TURN comes from the bank (coordinated, 1.05 rad/s at full bank, faster for agile jets, slower for the cargo plane); lift needs V > 0.75 x `stall`: below it the nose falls by itself and the plane sinks 13 m/s, the dive brings the speed back (stall-safe: no spin, recovered with the throttle up); the last 40 m of a descent are a FLARE (the allowed sink shrinks with the height, a steady DOWN never dives into the ground); touchdown with the nose down (-0.32 rad), a wing down (0.45 rad of bank) or a sink over 6.5 m/s costs hit points, over 21 m/s wrecks it. On the ground the wheels steer and the brake is the stick back with the lever at zero. HELICOPTER: rotor spool-up 1.6 s, collective = UP / DOWN (climb `climb` m/s, soft landing flare), the stick forward tilts the nose down with the speed, yaw by the tail rotor, HOVER ASSIST (sticks released = the drift is stopped in about a second, the height held), rotor WASH (dust or spray and water rings within 15 m of the ground, near the player). Ground = `AircraftFlight.ground_y` (the finished terrain outside the plateau; `VehicleCraft._measure_agl` and `_move_air` use it: aircraft rest on the real terrain, the airport, the mountain pads; before this wave y = 0 was the ground outside the city and a plane flew through mountains). `AircraftFlight.info(v)` is the data of the flight HUD (speed, altitude, vertical speed, heading, pitch, roll, throttle, stall, guide, a plain hint).
- **Flight camera** (`CameraRig._chase_pitch`, `_air_roll`): the chase view is flatter (-0.09 rad) and follows the climb and the dive of the aircraft (62 % of its pitch), rolls 32 % with the bank of a plane; `cam_d` / `cam_h` / speed FOV as before.
- **Flight HUD** (`FlightHud.draw`, called by `Hud._draw_hud_body` while the hero flies; direct draw calls on the left edge, clear of the sticks and buttons): an ARTIFICIAL HORIZON (sky and ground split at the horizon, tilting with the bank, sliding with the pitch, pitch ladder, aircraft mark), heading, altitude above the ground (amber under 25 m) with a vertical-speed arrow and number, a throttle bar, a flashing STALL warning, and the LANDING GUIDE (`Airport.guide`: within 2.6 km in front of a runway end and lined up within 0.9 rad: the runway number and the distance, a box with a diamond that must sit on the cross: above the cross = too high, left = the runway is to the left, 5 degree glide path, HIGH: NOSE DOWN / LOW: NOSE UP / ON PATH; it goes out past the threshold). The speedometer plate and the vehicle readout keep the words (ALT, THROTTLE, the hint, the guns and the rocket rounds). The AIM button becomes ROCKET in a gunship and a jet (`touch_controls.gd`, need `arms2`).
- **Rockets and missiles** (`VehicleWeapons.tick_alt` / `fire_alt` on the AIM action; `AirRockets` = a pooled set of real projectiles under the City, at most 14): `salvo` rockets per press from the pylons, `speed` m/s for `life` s, a smoke line, the blast and direct damage on the first thing hit (world, enemy, vehicle, the terrain, the ground); a missile locks the nearest enemy or vehicle within 0.4 rad of the nose and steers 1.6 rad/s; one round comes back every `reload` s; the HUD shows the rounds.
- **Air traffic** (`AirTraffic`, child of `AirportWorld`): seven kinematic FLIGHTS (two airliners, a business jet, a small plane, a light helicopter, a gunship and a transport helicopter), no physics and no collision, a few vectors each. A plane's route (`plane_route`): gate, pushback, taxi, line up, take-off roll, climb, a circuit over the plain and the sea (never lower than 70 m over the terrain), a 5 degree final, flare, touchdown, roll-out, exit by the middle connector, taxi in, park, wait, again. A helicopter's route (`heli_route`): rotor still on its pad, spool-up, lift, a patrol loop over the field and the city's south edge (the big ones go over the city at 140-150 m, above its 88 m towers), return, descend, land. Flights are drawn (`AircraftScenery` models, rotors spinning) only within 900 m of the player, so nothing exists near the city's start; `fast_forward(secs)` runs the whole simulation at once (tests). Stats: take-offs, landings, laps, clearance over the terrain on the cruise legs.
- **Hijack**: a flight that stands or crawls (below 2 m and 14 m/s) when the hero on foot comes within 24 m is turned into a REAL aircraft (`VehicleManager.spawn_parked`, flagged `ai`, meta `hold`); `VehicleManager.enter` then makes it a hijack (heat +0.2, "HIJACKED  GUNSHIP", `Hijack.on_enter` throws the pilot out at the fuselage, 3.2 m from the centre however wide the wings); the flight is gone for 90 s and comes back at its stand. One nobody takes goes back to its plan after 30 s or when the hero walks 70 m away. (`Vehicle._physics_process`: an `ai` craft is not driven by the car AI.)
- **Perf**: nothing of the airport is in view of the start of the game: the three field meshes (7.9 k triangles in all, harbour shader, no texture) are culled at 1 km from the nearest point of their box (the start is 1.2 km from the apron's corner), the remote pads at 450 m, the parked airliners at 950 m, the air traffic at 900 m; the scenery planes are 3 draws each. See the integration note for the perf gate. Texture memory: none added.
- **Gaps**: no retractable gear, no cockpit view, no pilot figure in the aircraft, no parachute, no sound for the rockets besides `shot` and `boom`; the AI planes ignore the player and each other (they are kinematic and never collide: a plane can taxi through another); no ATC voices or radio; es / hi strings for the new names and lines; the cargo plane has no ramp or cargo; no aircraft damage models beyond the hit points; vegetation and rocks are not scattered on the blend ring; no phone measurement.


## Wildlife (wave 107: animals by biome)
- **Files**: scripts/wildlife/ `animal_species.gd` (the table of 18 species and `weights(cls, biome, h, slope, night, farm, shore)`), `critter.gd` (a pooled body: skinned model, own ShaderMaterial, clip player), `wildlife_kit.gd` (shader, scene cache, the lizard mesh built in code), `wildlife_world.gd` (`WildlifeWorld`, child `wildlife` of the City, `QT_NO_WILDLIFE=1` leaves it out); assets/shaders/animal.gdshader; assets/models/animals/*.scn (800 KB: converted by tools/open_models/convert_animals.gd, Godot imports the Quaternius FBX itself, one merged surface per animal = ONE draw, colours baked into vertex colour, the rig and the clips kept; the horse / cow clips also drive the sheep, goat and boar: same bone names); assets/audio/animal_*.ogg (tools/audio/make_animals.py).
- **Species**: deer (antlers generated), mountain goat (horns generated), wild boar, sheep, cow, horse, zebra, wolf, coyote, fox, lizard (code mesh, vertex wiggle), songbird, seagull, eagle, fish, dolphin, shark, whale. Classes LAND / AIR / WATER / SMALL with caps per tier (`CAPS`: LAND 8 / 14 / 22, AIR 5 / 9 / 15, WATER 4 / 7 / 11, SMALL 3 / 6 / 10; LOW+ x0.7).
- **Spawn director** (every 0.3 s, one group per call): picks the class furthest below its cap, a point in a ring around the hero (26-78 / 28-92 / 30-108 m, 60 % ahead of the camera but outside the view frustum, never inside the city skirt of 760 m), the species from `AnimalSpecies.weights` by `TerrainBase.biome_at`, height, slope, time of day (`DayNight.night_now`: songbirds, eagles and lizards are day only, wolves / coyotes / foxes are 1.6-2.5x likelier at night) and farm distance (`OutskirtsSites` farms: sheep, cows, horses). Water species need real depth (fish: river or lake 1 m, dolphins and sharks the ocean, whales 10 m and 60 m away). Bodies beyond 1.55 x the ring are pooled (flocks 1.7 x). `settle(pos)` fills everything at once (tests, captures); `director = false` stops the spawning.
- **Behaviour**: prey graze, wander with the herd inside 42 m of their home and run (the whole herd) from the hero inside their `flee` range (x1.8 when he runs), gunfire (`Player.fired` -> `scare_all` 78 m), blasts (`Game.exploded`), cars faster than 4.5 m/s and hunting predators; predators hunt a prey in 58 m (run it down, the prey dissolves, the pack feeds for 6 s, no bounty for the hero); provoked predators and boars charge the hero and bite (5-7 damage, 1.3 s); grazers lie down at night (squashed pose, wake at 11 m or by day); flocks orbit and bank, scatter and climb away (then leave), gulls also stand on the sand until the hero is within 16 m; fish schools turn at the shore and dart and dive from the hero; dolphins porpoise (arc 1.5 m, splash sound), sharks cruise 0.75 m down with the fin out, whales swim slowly.
- **Hunting** (optional): `WildlifeWorld.ray()` is tested after the civilians in `PlayerWeapon` (and by the beam weapons), `_hit_animal` -> `hurt()` (hit marker, damage number, `animal_hits`). A kill (`kill()`) is the quantum-dust death: dissolve over 1.25 s in `animal.gdshader` (noisy pixels from the feet up, cold glow edge), two `FX.burst`s, `civ_dissolve`, the bounty to `Progress.add_credits` and a toast (deer 30, goat 30, boar 30, sheep 10, cow 15, horse 25, zebra 25, wolf 45, coyote 35, fox 20, eagle 20, seagull / songbird 5, shark 80, lizard 3; fish, dolphin, whale 0), cars run animals over (a `vehicle` node with `speed` > 6 within 2.4 m), blasts kill inside their radius. No gore.
- **Perf**: one draw per animal, 158-1354 triangles, animation frozen beyond 58 m, hidden beyond 150 m, ground sampled at 6 Hz per moving body, nothing in the city. Perf gate (city): LOW 78 draws / 130k, MED 103 / 234k, HIGH 116 / 321k, all OK; worst outskirts case adds at most 20 draws on LOW (caps).
- **Tests and tools**: tests/test_animals_biomes.gd (121 checks: table, rules, director, flee, hunt, night, flocks, sea, river, carbine kill, bounty, blast, cars, pause), tests/wildlife_viewer.gd + tools/wildlife_view.sh (real shader and clips on a plain ground), tests/bot_wildlife.gd (real game camera, OUT_VIEWS "x,z,yaw,species:n,front=,dist=,lift=").
- **Gaps**: no rabbits, bears or goat model of its own (llama body), the deer is the horse body with antlers, the shark is the fish body, fish are hard to see through the water, no map pins or hunting missions, birds do not perch on trees, herds do not path around rivers (they turn at the bank), predators ignore each other, no animals inside the city or interiors, no es / hi strings for the toasts.

## Alien wildlife and factions (wave 107b; scripts/aliens/, tests/test_alien_world.gd)
Dangerous alien life outside the city, wired into the City as child `aliens` (`AlienWorld.attach`, `QT_NO_ALIENS=1` leaves it out). Pure data first: `AlienSpecies` (one TABLE row per species: model or procedural body, mech, hp, speeds, damage, reach, windup, cool, sight, hit spheres, loot, glow), `AlienSites` (15 sites FOUND by a deterministic terrain search, never hand-placed), `AlienLoot` (tech parts).
- **Fauna** (`AlienSpecies.FAUNA`, spawned by a ring director around the hero, outside the camera view, by biome through `AlienSpecies.weights`; never in the city, caps per tier GROUND 4/6/9, AIR 3/5/8, WATER 1/1/2, BURROW 1/1/2, EASY longer gaps and smaller packs, none while the hero is faster than 26 m/s or inside): SKITTER pack hunters (forest, plains, desert fringe; fan out, crouch, leap), SPORE POPPER (walking bomb: fuse ring, a lingering toxic cloud), SWARMER (flying swarm over the mountains: circle, hover, dive), DUNE MAW (giant burrower under the desert sand: tremor wake, a ring marks where it rises, eruption, risen and biting for a few seconds, sinks; untouchable and out of the `enemy` group while buried), REEF LURKER (sea serpent: the same cycle on the water) and DRIFT RAY (floating stinger over the sea).
- **Camp creatures**: HIVE GRUNT (melee), ACID SPITTER (keeps its distance, slow globs), CRAG BRUTE (slam: a ground ring shows the shockwave, a jump clears it), SHADE STALKER (cloaked, pounces, fades away), SPORE SENTRY (turret: aim line, short burst), HIVE HEART (a nest core that hatches skitters until destroyed), SHIELD RELAY.
- **Sites** (`AlienSites.DEFS`): five CAMPS (RIDGE CLAW CAMP, FROSTFANG OUTPOST, EAST RANGE BASE in the mountains, EASTWATER ROOST and HIGHLINE HOLD in the canyons), three HIVE NESTS (forest, dunes, plains), three CRASH SITES (the existing ALIEN CRASH SITE gets guards and the warden; PEAK WRECK; SHORE WRECK) and four boss LAIRS. Each has a one-draw merged mesh (`AlienSiteKit`, 1.5 to 4 k triangles, platform with a skirt so it stands on slopes, boxes for collision) built within 1 km, and a garrison brought to life within 200 to 260 m and sent away beyond 1.9 times that. Clearing (all guards, the heart, the boss) pays `reward`, drops a QUANTA CRYSTAL and (lairs and crashes) the next ladder weapon, and is saved in `user://alien_world.cfg` (`AlienSites.reset_all()` for a new game and tests). `hooks()` is the mission-hook list (`{id, kind, name, pos, r, reward, tag, boss, cleared}`), `map_markers()` the big map's SITES pins (one line in map_screen.gd).
- **Bosses** (`AlienBoss`, one per biome, each a different mechanic, always a telegraph first, the HUD bar says the ONE thing to do): BROODMOTHER (forest: spore clouds, root slam, then WINDED: the sac takes x2; calls skitters at 66 and 33 %), STORM DRAKE (mountains: lightning rings, a marked dive line, then GROUNDED and open x1.5; swarmers in the last third), SAND LEVIATHAN (desert: tunnels, rings, eruption, fan of sand globs, the maw takes extra; erupts twice in later phases), DEEP KRAKEN (ocean: four arms rise and slam rings on the water, the mantle is shielded while any arm lives, then it surfaces open x1.4, the arms grow back twice), HIVE WARDEN (crash site: an energy shield held by three RELAYS, lance line, pulse ring; shield down = stunned and open). A boss the hero walks away from heals and lets go of the boss music; `AlienWorld.boss_bar()` feeds `Hud._draw_alien_boss` (name, health, hint) in free roam and missions alike.
- **Combat contract**: every `AlienCreature` is in groups `enemy` (while hittable) and `alien`, answers `damage(amount, crit, mirrored)`, `center()`, `radius`, `hit_ray()` (body sphere plus head sphere: HitZones crit), `stagger()`, `disable()`, `dead`, `active`, so weapons, blasts (`Game.explode`), verbs, aim assist and the minimap treat them as enemies. The hero is hurt through `Player.damage` (difficulty, cover, vehicle share all apply). At most 2 / 3 / 4 / 4 attackers leap or dive at once (EASY .. QUANTUM, `leap_gate`), windups scale with `Difficulty.mult("aggr")`.
- **Loot** (`AlienLoot`, parts CHITIN PLATE 12, NEURAL GLAND 30, HIVE CORE 80, QUANTA CRYSTAL 150 credits): glowing shards drop from kills, magnetise to the hero within 6.5 m (pool of 14), bank credits, the matching Crafting part (scrap / alloy / core) and every 10 parts a ladder weapon drop; kills also call `Crafting.on_kill` and add score and skill xp.
- **Models**: eleven Quaternius Ultimate Monsters rigs (CC0) converted by `tools/open_models/convert_monsters.sh` into `assets/models/aliens/*.scn` (876 KB for all): one surface (one draw call), atlas colours baked into vertex colours and re-graded (no texture memory), the near-white eyes and teeth become the glow mask in alpha, meshoptimizer LODs pick 0.9 to 3 k triangles. Seven bodies are procedural (`AlienKit`, `AlienMesh`: worm, serpent, kraken mantle and arm, sentry, heart, relay; 64 to 900 triangles). One shader, `alien.gdshader` (vertex colour, glow mask with a breathing beat, chitin sheen and rim, quantum-dust dissolve, stalker cloak, warden shield, worm / tentacle wiggle, hit pulse, boss rage).
- **Effects** (`AlienFx`, all pooled): telegraph rings, aim lines, projectile sprites (22), dust, splash; spore cloud hazards (6), `Game.explode` blasts and `FX.marks.splat` puddles on death.
- **Perf**: nothing runs in the city; ambient bodies cap at 4 / 6 / 9 per tier, a far camp guard is not ticked, bodies hide beyond 190 m, ground is sampled at 12 Hz per moving body.
- **Tests and tools**: tests/test_alien_world.gd (134 checks: table, 15 sites, the enemy contract, every behaviour lands an attack after a telegraph, the five boss mechanics, site clearing and saving, loot, the biome director, the real scene), tests/alien_viewer.gd + tools/alien_view.sh (bodies on a slab), tests/bot_alien.gd (real-game frames: `@site,dist,angle` views).
- **Gaps**: the monster rigs keep their cartoon proportions (grading, glow and rim light only); no mission-board type yet (the sites carry their own clear reward; `AlienSites.hooks()` is the entry point and a `clear_site` campaign step the next change); no sounds of their own beyond the alien barks, `verb_slam`, `boom` and the water splashes; the kraken needs the hero at sea (boat, jet ski or helicopter); trees and rocks do not stop the creatures; creatures do not hurt civilians or cars; the stalker keeps the ninja's two swords; es / hi strings.


## Energy weapons, destructive weapons, grenades, gadgets and tanks (wave 108; scripts/player/{energy_arms,weapon_models_energy}.gd, scripts/vehicles/tank_wreck.gd; tests/test_energy_destructive_weapons.gd and tests/test_tanks.gd; look-dev bot tests/bot_energy_fire.gd)
Owner round 5: COD-type weapons, Halo-inspired energy weapons with ORIGINAL names and designs (nothing trademarked: the old HALO MARKSMAN DMR is now RANGE MARKSMAN DMR and the HALO SIGHT attachment HOLO SIGHT; the test lists protected words), more destructive weapons with a function each, tanks.
- **Categories verified, gaps filled**: every classic category of `WeaponRoster` was already there (pistols, heavy pistol, revolver, machine pistol, SMGs, ARs, battle rifle, LMG, minigun, pump and auto shotguns, sniper, AMR, crossbow, RPG, grenade launcher, frag / smoke / flash, C4, mine, flamethrower, stun gun, katana, knife, bat). New: GUIDED missile launcher (SEEKER), THROWING KNIVES (silent), see below.
- **Fifteen new weapons in `WeaponDefs`** (one def each, `role` = what it is for in the missions, two fire modes each in `ALTS`, `PROJ` holds the one sub-projectile): ENERGY class (`energy`, tab ENERGY): EMBER PLASMA PISTOL (`eplasma`: strips shields, a small plasma pop on every hit; alt OVERCHARGE = a slow plasma ball for 6 rounds that stuns the squad it hits and cripples cars), PULSE REPEATER (`erepeat`: 14 rounds a second, heat gauge; alt SURGE), CRYSTAL SPIKER (`espike`: the needle-type sticky-crystal rifle, six crystals in 4 s supercombine into a violet blast; alt SHATTER pops what is planted), PRISM BEAM RIFLE (`ebeam`: charged beam sniper through two walls with a blast at the end, zoom 14; alt SNAP BEAM), ION BLADE (`eblade`: melee that burns, alt LUNGE), TREMOR MAUL (`emaul`: the gravity hammer, every swing sends a 6.5 m shockwave, alt GROUND SLAM 9 m), HORNET ROCKET POD (`epod`: three rockets per pull, alt ALL PODS six). BLAST class (`blast`, tab BLAST): COIL RAILGUN (`railgun`: through four walls and a tank, 5 m blast, alt OVERLOAD), STORM CHAINGUN (`chaingun`: spin-up, every third round explosive, alt SUPPRESS), HIVE CLUSTER MISSILE (`cmissile`: six bomblets rain after the impact, alt AIRBURST opens it in the air), CINDER NAPALM LAUNCHER (`napalm`: a burning field for 7 s, alt WALL OF FIRE five fields), VOID SINGULARITY CANNON (`sing`: a 3.2 s well of 15 m drags the squad in, then an 11 m collapse; alt REPULSE), ORBITAL STRIKE BEACON (`orbital`: lands, a column of light rises, 2.2 s later three strikes of 13 m; alt BARRAGE three beacons), SEEKER GUIDED LAUNCHER (`seeker`: steered by the crosshair or the lock, alt TOP ATTACK climbs then dives). THROWING KNIVES (`tknife`, MELEE tab: silent, alt FAN OF KNIVES).
- **New def keys, all read by `EnergyArms`** (`weapon.arms`, one per PlayerWeapon, counters for tests: splashes shockwaves crystal_pops cluster_blasts fire_zones_made beacon_strikes guided_steers salvo_rockets blast_stuns oc_bolts): `splash` (+ `splash_dmg splash_car splash_element splash_every`: a `Game.explode` where a round lands, so cover, props, cars and people all feel it), `stick` (+ time, damage, radius, element), `shock` (+ dmg car push element), `salvo`, `cluster_n`, `fire_radius fire_time fire_dps`, `beacon` (+ delay pulses gap), `guided` + `turn`, `slots` (grenade slots, the old cap of 2 collapsed rockets early), `launch` (play the weapon's own sound when it is thrown), `role`. New alt kinds in `PlayerWeapon.alt_fire`: oc_bolt pop slam repulse fan airburst dive fire_line. `Game.explode` got an option `quiet` (no boom, shake or slow motion: a fast weapon's pops). `_fire_lance` passes `wall` walls (cost `wall_k` each behind them) and burns a `splash` at its end; `_throw(yaw, quiet, opts)` takes a projectile id (`PROJ`), `airburst`, `dive`; `_collapse` asks `arms.on_collapse` first (the beacon plants itself) and `arms.after_blast` after (bomblets, fire, stun).
- **Destruction**: every blast goes through `Game.explode`, which already breaks props and cover (`Breakables.blast`), hurts cars, throws liquid and scorch on the street (`FX.blast_marks`, element colours: plasma cyan, fire orange, electric blue, quantum violet), and shakes the camera. Shockwaves also shove people (`stagger`, `disable`, a push) and cars (`Vehicle.apply_impulse`).
- **Grenades and gadgets** (`Gear`): STICKY NOVA (`plasma`, sticks to the first person, car or wall and rides along, 110 damage, 220 to a car) and SPIKE GRENADE (`spike`: 18 spikes in a ring, a person takes as many as fit in the angle he fills, a wall stops them), in `Throwables`; SENTRY TURRET (`turret`: 25 s, 16 damage at 7 shots a second, LOS check), SHIELD EMITTER (`shield`: 4 m dome, 12 s or 400 damage, `Player.damage` asks `Gadgets.shield_absorb` first) and BOOST MODULE (`jetpack`: 1.6 s of climb at 9 m/s) in `Gadgets`. The mine already existed (FLUX MINE).
- **Models and pictures**: procedural meshes in `WeaponModelsEnergy` (one draw each, the weapon_look shader), grips in `WeaponModels.SPEC`; `Gadgets.mesh_of` / `Throwables.mesh_of` for the gear; icons `w_ / wl_ / g_` rendered by `tools/icons/build_icons.sh` (the icon maker has stand-ins for the five gear items). **Sounds**: 42 OGG files built by `make_real.py build_energy` from the clips already in the repo (CC0, docs/AUDIO_SOURCES.md): every weapon and every gear item has its own name (`shot_eplasma ... melee_tknife`, `plasma_blast`, `plasma_stick`, `spike_blast`, `turret_deploy`, `shield_up`, `jet_boost`), two or three takes for the fast ones.
- **Progression**: `WeaponLadder` has 15 more entries (crates 17..24 are new: PLASMA, CRYSTAL, BURN, HANGAR, CONVOY, RIDGE, VAULT, ORBITAL; chapter 11 gives the ION BLADE; drops from sentinel, flanker, heavy and brute; two shop-only), `Economy` prices (the energy and blast weapons 90..900 before the 2.8 scale, grenades 180-200, gadgets 330-390; `FULL_TREE_HOURS` 10 -> 11 and the balance band 13 -> 14.5 h because the tree grew by about 1.2 h), the legacy shop list, and es / hi strings. `WeaponDefs.CLASS_ORDER` has ten classes: the weapon wheel has ten wedges (121 px between disc centres, a disc is 112), the LOADOUT ten tabs, the catalogue eight pages (ENERGY WEAPONS, BLAST WEAPONS, EXPLOSIVES, GADGETS are new pages; at most 12 tiles each), `WeaponRoster.tabs()` twelve.
- **Tanks** (`VehicleSpecs.TANK_KINDS`, same tracked physics as the round-5 tank, `VehicleCraft._tank`): BATTLE TANK (`tank`, now with a COAX MACHINE GUN on the AIM button), LIGHT TANK (`ltank`, 17 m/s, light cannon 1.5 rounds a second, coax), ARMOURED CARRIER (`apc`, 22 m/s, six seats, 1500 hp, autocannon), MOBILE ARTILLERY (`artillery`, slowest, a HOWITZER whose shell lands on the crosshair point up to 520 m away after 1.4 s with an 11 m blast, the gun elevates to 1.1 rad), ANTI-AIR TANK (`aatank`, FLAK CANNON bursts at the aim point in the sky, eight SEEKER MISSILES on AIM, gun to 1.35 rad). Procedural bodies in `VehicleCraftModels.build_tank` (a hull Body with tracks or wheels, a "Turret" on the turret axis, a "Gun" on its trunnion that `VehicleCraft.sync` elevates), pictures `v_*.png`, spawn points at the army depot (`VehicleSpawns` depot_light / depot_apc / depot_gun / depot_aa), engine entries, names (es / hi too), the touch AIM button now reads the weapon (`short`: ROCKET, MISSILE, COAX). **Destruction**: `TankWreck.pop_turret` throws the turret about 6 m up and 3 m aside when a tank blows up (it lies beside the hull for 30 s; `Vehicle.activate` shows it again). Handling: they turn on the spot (`TANK_YAW`, per-kind `steer`), accelerate by their own numbers, the turret slews at 1.7 rad/s.
- **Already there from earlier waves** (checked by test_tanks): naval gun turrets (`ShipKit` destroyer: two turrets you man, shells with a 9 m blast, `ShipWorld`), aircraft weapons (heli minigun, plane wing guns, gunship chain gun + rocket pod, hauler door gun, jet cannon + homing missiles, patrol boat bow cannon, speedboat deck gun).
- **Tests**: `test_energy_destructive_weapons` (about 140 checks: data for all 20 items, then every weapon in the real PlayerWeapon with a counter or a measured effect, grenades, gadgets, the wheel / loadout / catalogue), `test_tanks` (data, handling, turret slew and elevation, every gun, destruction). Existing tests edited with reasons: test_weapon_roster (new slots, tabs +2, protected words), test_weapons_available_display (ENERGY and BLAST have no starter), test_melee_gadgets (8 throwables, 6 gadgets, 13 priced), test_balance2 (band), test_localisation_complete (NOVA is the same word), test_shooting_accuracy (timeout 120 s: the sweep fires nine more weapons). Real-scene destruction: tests/test_energy_destructive_scene.gd (props, a parked car, the wheel and the catalogue in main.tscn).
- **Gaps**: nothing measured on a phone (perf gate not run: the meshes are one draw each, the beacon column is one pooled additive mesh, fire fields burn up to eight at once); the sounds are layered from existing clips, not new recordings; no weapon-specific reload or inspect pose for the new models; the SENTRY TURRET and SHIELD dome are simple meshes; tank tracks do not animate (the wheels are static decoration) and a tank does not crush cars beyond the old push; enemies do not use the new weapons; the AI never drives a tank; the missions only point at the weapons through `role` text and the crates (no mission yet requires a bunker buster).

## Enemy roster 2 (wave 108; scripts/roster/*, tests/test_enemy_roster2.gd in the real scene too; sheets docs/shots/slice/enemy_roster2_*.jpg)
19 new enemies in four factions on the alien world's rails: every one is an `AlienCreature` (`RosterFoe`) or `AlienBoss` (`RosterBoss`) made by `AlienWorld.make` (`AlienSpecies.data/title/family_of/roll_loot` fall back to `RosterSpecies`), so ticking, pooling, despawn, bounty, loot, the `enemy` group, weapons, blasts, aim assist, minimap and the boss bar all work unchanged.
- **Data** `RosterSpecies`: HIVE (HIVELING swarm, SHIELD BEARER, JET ELITE, VEIL STALKER, PLATE BRUTE, BEAM SNIPER), GRID (STRIDER walker, CRAWLER spider mech, WASP DRONE, AUTO TURRET), CONTRACT (merc RIFLE, ROCKET, BREACHER shotgun, MARKSMAN sniper, FIELD MEDIC), TITAN bosses (MOUNTAIN TITAN, MECH COLOSSUS, SEA LEVIATHAN, AIR GUNSHIP). Rows are alien rows plus `faction role tip weak shot n gap speed spread keep cover flank nade retreat revivable medic shield plates`.
- **Bodies** `RosterKit`: procedural, ONE surface and draw each (the brute adds three plate meshes), vertex colour + glow mask in `alien.gdshader` (no textures). The shader got `stride` (legs swing about `hip_y`, no skeleton), `far_k` (body-wide role-colour glow with distance) and the cloak / shield params already there. Readability at phone distance: silhouette (shield slab, jetpack, long rifle, spider legs, rotor ring, launcher tube, white pack), faction paint, role glow, `far_k` and a 1.0 -> 1.5 size growth from 10 to 36 m (hit spheres follow).
- **Brains** `RosterFoe` (`mech`): `ros_gun` (all gunners: advance to `keep` range, back-pedal, strafe; COVER = a spot the hero has no line to, found by terrain + physics rays, crouch, PEEK sideways for a volley; FLANK arc; GRENADE with a ring and a fuse; RETREAT at low health to the nearest medic and rest; MEDIC channels a revive (beam, 2.4 s, interrupted by a hit) or heals; a REVIVABLE unit killed with a medic near goes DOWN (red ring, up to 9 s, one more hit finishes it) and stands up with 45 %), `ros_swarm` (circle, surge together under the leap gate, bite, bounce), `ros_charge` (roar line, charge, stumble = open, slam ring; three armour plates soak shots from their side until broken, then the core), `ros_stalk` (cloaked, uncloaks to windup and pounce), `ros_jet` (boost, hover, land; the jetpack is a component), `ros_walker` (missile SALVO with rings, kneels once at 35 %), `ros_crawler` (climbs, leaps onto a ringed spot, shock), `ros_drone` (orbit, mark a line, strafe it, climb; rotor ring component = crash), `ros_turret`. Shots: bursts (`AlienWorld.shoot`), pellet cone, beam (lock line then ray), rockets / grenades / shells (`RosterFire`: ring for the whole flight, blast through `Game.explode` with enemy_dmg 0). Every attack has a telegraph, windups scale with `_aggr_k`, at most the leap gate's number (EASY 2) windup at once, EASY: spread x1.5, no target lead, smaller groups, longer gaps, one revive per medic. One first-contact line per type ("SHIELD BEARER  FLANK IT  SHOOT THE SPINE"). Weapon sounds per type from assets/audio (`RosterFoe.SND`).
- **Weak points** `RosterWeak`: hit spheres that win over the body sphere when the ray comes from the right side (`from` any / back / front, `need` a broken plate), pay `mult`, return zone HEAD (the weapon's crit number applies) and may carry component hp (jetpack, rotor ring, sniper lens, boss crystals / knees / engines) with a break effect. Blasts and melee are plain damage.
- **Bosses** `RosterBoss`: while a component stands the body takes 45 %; TITAN (stomp ring, boulders, three crystals, kneels), COLOSSUS (gatling arc between two marked lines, missile barrage, stomp, knees then reactor), LEVIATHAN (swims, ring, erupts, surfaced = gills open, tail sweep), GUNSHIP (strafe line, rocket rings, two engines then crash-lands, tail rotor). Phases at 2/3 and 1/3 call hivelings / drones; parts regrow once at half after a kneel.
- **Director** `RosterDirector` (child of AlienWorld, ticked in its ambient loop, `QT_NO_ALIENS=1` removes it): `RosterSpecies.ENCOUNTERS` weighed by biome (`TerrainBase.biome_at`, `city` for the skirt), night, the faction bias of the running MissionBoard mission (`MISSION_BIAS`, read from the `mission_board` group's `active.type`; a boss mission brings the roaming boss in 20 s) and the progress tier (kills 0 / 8 / 30; brutes, walkers, snipers, rocket teams and bosses from tier 1). Ring 34-105 m outside the camera view, caps 4 / 6 / 9 per tier, at most 6 busy units around the hero, never in the city skirt, never inside interiors, gap 20 / 15 / 11 s (x1.4 EASY). One roaming WORLD BOSS per biome from 4 minutes outside at tier 1, removed 420 m behind. `spawn_encounter(eid, pos)` / `spawn_boss(id, pos)` are the entry points for missions.
- **Tests**: tests/test_enemy_roster2.gd (about 200 checks: table, bodies, director data, contract, every unit lands a telegraphed hit, cover / peek / flank / grenade / rocket dodge / beam dodge / retreat, medic revive / interrupt / finish / heal, shield, jetpack, cloak, plates, salvo, kneel, leap, strafe, swarm gate, weak points from the right side only, four bosses, live director, real scene). tests/test_alien_world.gd switches the roster director off for its fauna caps. Look-dev: `tools/alien_view.sh /tmp/o ids=merc_rifle,strider lineup=1`, real-game frames `OUT_VIEWS="x,z,yaw,merc_squad_ids:n,front=24,aggro=1" tools/bot.sh res://tests/bot_alien.gd` (roster ids work like alien ids).
- Looked at (render-look-fix loops, viewer 1280x720, real game LOW 1600x720 and 2400x1080, every frame opened): 1 mercs read as one mannequin (same palette, tiny heads, launcher a pink blob): own palettes, bigger heads, pauldrons, launcher with rockets, white pack with a glow cross; 2 shield bearer's whole body glowed cyan (shield shimmer): 0.85 -> 0.3; 3 figures at 25 m were 30-50 px dark shapes in a forest: `far_k` glow + growth with distance; 4 titan lost among conifers, no front weak hint: lighter rock, bigger eyes, chest core; drone rotor rings invisible: brighter rings; the in-game crawler washed out pink at far_k 0.9: 0.55.
- Gaps: nothing measured on a phone; the perf gate was not rerun with units live (nothing spawns in the city, bodies are 1 draw, 4 / 6 / 9 caps, plates add 3 draws on a brute); the mission board has no mission type that places these units at a step (the bias and `spawn_encounter` are ready); grenades and rockets reuse `enemy_shot` family sounds with pitch; cover is found with rays (no nav mesh) so tall vegetation without colliders gives none; mercenaries do not shoot civilians or cars; the downed pose is a rotated mesh; no es / hi strings for the first-contact lines; roaming bosses can stand among trees that hide them.
